cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.16)

project(ImageConverter VERSION 1.2.0 LANGUAGES CXX)

set(CMAKE_AUTOUIC OFF)
set(CMAKE_AUTOMOC ON)
set(CMAKE_AUTORCC ON)

set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD 17)
set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)

find_package(QT NAMES Qt6 Qt5 REQUIRED COMPONENTS Widgets Pdf Concurrent Svg)
find_package(Qt${QT_VERSION_MAJOR} REQUIRED COMPONENTS Core Widgets Pdf Concurrent Svg)

add_subdirectory(ui-kit)

set(PROJECT_WIDGETS
        widgets/button_action.cpp
        widgets/button_action.h
        widgets/button_toggle.cpp
        widgets/button_toggle.h
        widgets/input_dropdown.cpp
        widgets/input_dropdown.h
        widgets/input_field.cpp
        widgets/input_field.h
        widgets/input_upload.cpp
        widgets/input_upload.h
        widgets/input_invalid.cpp
        widgets/input_invalid.h
        widgets/input_widget.cpp
        widgets/input_widget.h
        widgets/sidebar_panel.cpp
        widgets/sidebar_panel.h
        widgets/button_sidebar_active.cpp
        widgets/button_sidebar_active.h
        widgets/button_sidebar_widget.cpp
        widgets/button_sidebar_widget.h
        widgets/drop_file_widget.h
        widgets/drop_file_widget.cpp
        widgets/slider_widget.h
        widgets/slider_widget.cpp
        widgets/message_box_widget.cpp
        widgets/message_box_widget.h
)

set(PROJECT_STYLES
        styles/colors.h
        styles/text_style.h
        resources/input_type.h
        resources/icon_type.h
        resources/icons.qrc
)

set(PROJECT_PAGES
        pages/main_page.cpp
        pages/main_page.h
        pages/pdf_page.cpp
        pages/pdf_page.h
        pages/gallery_page.cpp
        pages/gallery_page.h
)

set(PROJECT_CONTROLLERS
        controllers/pdf/pdf_utils.cpp
        controllers/pdf/pdf_utils.h
        controllers/pdf/pdf_tool_controller.cpp
        controllers/pdf/pdf_tool_controller.h
        controllers/pdf/compress_controller.cpp
        controllers/pdf/compress_controller.h
        controllers/pdf/merge_controller.cpp
        controllers/pdf/merge_controller.h
        controllers/pdf/split_controller.cpp
        controllers/pdf/split_controller.h
        controllers/pdf/organize_controller.cpp
        controllers/pdf/organize_controller.h
        controllers/pdf/pdf_to_images_controller.cpp
        controllers/pdf/pdf_to_images_controller.h
        controllers/pdf/images_to_pdf_controller.cpp
        controllers/pdf/images_to_pdf_controller.h
        controllers/pdf/grayscale_controller.cpp
        controllers/pdf/grayscale_controller.h
        controllers/pdf/markdown_pdf_controller.cpp
        controllers/pdf/markdown_pdf_controller.h
        controllers/image/svg_convert_controller.cpp
        controllers/image/svg_convert_controller.h
        controllers/image/ico_export_controller.cpp
        controllers/image/ico_export_controller.h
)

set(PROJECT_SOURCES
        main.cpp
        mainwindow/mainwindow.cpp
        mainwindow/mainwindow.h
)

if(${QT_VERSION_MAJOR} GREATER_EQUAL 6)
    qt_add_executable(ImageConverter
        MANUAL_FINALIZATION
        ${PROJECT_SOURCES}
        ${PROJECT_WIDGETS}
        ${PROJECT_STYLES}
        ${PROJECT_PAGES}
        ${PROJECT_CONTROLLERS}
    )
else()
    if(ANDROID)
        add_library(ImageConverter SHARED
            ${PROJECT_SOURCES}
            ${PROJECT_WIDGETS}
            ${PROJECT_STYLES}
            ${PROJECT_PAGES}
            ${PROJECT_CONTROLLERS}
        )
    else()
        add_executable(ImageConverter
            ${PROJECT_SOURCES}
            ${PROJECT_WIDGETS}
            ${PROJECT_STYLES}
            ${PROJECT_PAGES}
            ${PROJECT_CONTROLLERS}
        )
    endif()
endif()

target_link_libraries(ImageConverter PRIVATE ui_kit Qt${QT_VERSION_MAJOR}::Core Qt${QT_VERSION_MAJOR}::Widgets Qt${QT_VERSION_MAJOR}::Pdf Qt${QT_VERSION_MAJOR}::Concurrent Qt${QT_VERSION_MAJOR}::Svg)
target_include_directories(ImageConverter PRIVATE ${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR})

if(${QT_VERSION} VERSION_LESS 6.1.0)
  set(BUNDLE_ID_OPTION MACOSX_BUNDLE_GUI_IDENTIFIER com.example.ImageConverter)
endif()
set_target_properties(ImageConverter PROPERTIES
    ${BUNDLE_ID_OPTION}
    MACOSX_BUNDLE_BUNDLE_VERSION ${PROJECT_VERSION}
    MACOSX_BUNDLE_SHORT_VERSION_STRING ${PROJECT_VERSION_MAJOR}.${PROJECT_VERSION_MINOR}
    MACOSX_BUNDLE TRUE
    WIN32_EXECUTABLE TRUE
)

include(GNUInstallDirs)
install(TARGETS ImageConverter
    BUNDLE DESTINATION .
    LIBRARY DESTINATION ${CMAKE_INSTALL_LIBDIR}
    RUNTIME DESTINATION ${CMAKE_INSTALL_BINDIR}
)

if(UNIX AND NOT APPLE)
    install(FILES resources/image-converter.desktop
        DESTINATION ${CMAKE_INSTALL_DATADIR}/applications)
    install(FILES resources/icons/icon-logo.png
        DESTINATION ${CMAKE_INSTALL_DATADIR}/icons/hicolor/128x128/apps
        RENAME image-converter.png)
endif()

if(QT_VERSION_MAJOR EQUAL 6)
    qt_finalize_executable(ImageConverter)
endif()

# CPack Packaging Configuration
set(CPACK_PACKAGE_NAME "image-converter")
set(CPACK_PACKAGE_VENDOR "Illufox Kusanagi")
set(CPACK_PACKAGE_DESCRIPTION_SUMMARY "Fast offline image converter and PDF compressor built with Qt 6")
set(CPACK_PACKAGE_VERSION_MAJOR ${PROJECT_VERSION_MAJOR})
set(CPACK_PACKAGE_VERSION_MINOR ${PROJECT_VERSION_MINOR})
set(CPACK_PACKAGE_VERSION_PATCH ${PROJECT_VERSION_PATCH})

# Debian (.deb)
set(CPACK_DEBIAN_PACKAGE_MAINTAINER "Illufox Kusanagi")
set(CPACK_DEBIAN_PACKAGE_SECTION "graphics")
set(CPACK_DEBIAN_PACKAGE_SHLIBDEPS ON)
# Ghostscript is invoked as a subprocess, so shared-library scanning misses it.
set(CPACK_DEBIAN_PACKAGE_DEPENDS "ghostscript")

# RPM (.rpm)
set(CPACK_RPM_PACKAGE_LICENSE "MIT")
set(CPACK_RPM_PACKAGE_GROUP "Applications/Graphics")
set(CPACK_RPM_PACKAGE_AUTOREQPROV ON)
set(CPACK_RPM_PACKAGE_REQUIRES "ghostscript")

include(CPack)

# ImageConverter

[![License: MIT](https://img.shields.io/badge/License-MIT-red.svg)](https://opensource.org/licenses/MIT)
[![Release](https://img.shields.io/github/v/release/illufoxKusanagi/Image-Converter?display_name=tag&sort=semver)](https://github.com/illufoxKusanagi/image-converter/releases)
[![Platform](https://img.shields.io/badge/platform-Windows%20%7C%20Linux-blue)](#)
[![C++](https://img.shields.io/badge/C%2B%2B-17-00599C?logo=c%2B%2B&logoColor=white)](https://isocpp.org/)
[![Qt](https://img.shields.io/badge/Qt-6.x-green)](https://www.qt.io/)

<p align="center">
  <img src="public/images/image_convert.png" alt="Convert Image View" width="49%" />
  <img src="public/images/pdf_compress.png" alt="PDF Tools View" width="49%" />
</p>

**ImageConverter (v1.2.0)** is a native, 100% offline C++17 / Qt 6 desktop application for batch image conversion and local PDF processing (compression, merging, splitting, page organization, page extraction, image-to-PDF assembly, and grayscale conversion), built on a custom 30-component Qt Widgets design system (`ui-kit`).

---

## Overview & Design Principles

Most document and image conversion utilities require uploading private files to third-party servers, enforce arbitrary file-size caps or daily quotas, or bundle heavy web runtimes. **ImageConverter** is engineered around three core principles:

1. **Strict Local Execution**: Every image conversion and PDF operation runs locally on your machine. The application makes zero network calls, collects zero telemetry or analytics, and imposes no file-size limits or usage quotas.
2. **Data Integrity & Atomic Saves**: File outputs are staged through temporary directories (`QTemporaryDir`) and committed using atomic `QSaveFile` operations (`setDirectWriteFallback(false)`). Candidate PDFs are validated against `QPdfDocument` for page count and point-dimension integrity before any destination file is written, ensuring original files are never corrupted or overwritten on failure or cancellation.
3. **Dual-Engine PDF Processing**: When Ghostscript (`gs` / `gswin64c`) is available, PDF operations use the `pdfwrite` distiller pipeline to preserve vector text, paths, and embedded fonts while optimizing or transforming embedded streams. When Ghostscript is absent or cannot process a specific file, the application automatically falls back to Qt's native `QPdfDocument` and `QPdfWriter` pipeline.

---

## Features

### Image Conversion

Implemented in `MainPage` and `DropFileWidget`:

- **Supported Formats**: `JPG`, `JPEG`, `PNG`, `WEBP`, `TIFF`, `BMP`, `GIF`, and `PDF`.
- **Three Conversion Workflows**:
  1. **Image to Image**: Single or batch conversion across raster formats with synchronized slider and spinbox quality control (`0-100` for lossy/configurable encoders; automatic `-1` lossless compression for `PNG`).
  2. **Images to Multi-Page PDF**: Combines one or more queued raster images into a single multi-page PDF document with page resolution scaled according to the quality slider.
  3. **PDF to Images**: Renders single-page or multi-page PDF documents into the selected raster image format, composited over a clean white paper background (`renderPdfPageOnWhite`) so transparent PDF pages never render with dark or uninitialized backdrops.
- **Interactive Queue Management (`DropFileWidget`)**:
  - Drag-and-drop files directly from the system file manager or browse via native file dialogs.
  - Automatic duplicate detection, formatted file size display (`B`, `KB`, `MB`), individual item removal, **Add More**, and **Clear All** actions.
  - Live per-file status badges (`Waiting`, `Processing`, `Succeeded`, `Unchanged`, `Failed`, `Cancelled`) paired with an inline animated spinner during active batch jobs.
  - Multi-threaded background execution via `QtConcurrent` and `QFutureWatcher` with responsive cancellation support.

### PDF Tools

Implemented in `PdfPage` and the headless `controllers/` layer. All seven PDF tools operate offline and report per-file progress and completion metrics.

| Tool | Input -> Output | Primary Engine / Fallback | Key Options & Safeguards |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Compress PDF** (`CompressController`) | 1+ PDFs -> 1+ optimized PDFs | Ghostscript (`pdfwrite`) / `QPdfDocument` + `QPdfWriter` | Quality presets (`20`, `50`, `80`, `Custom 0-100`), Smart Size Guard (`Unchanged` if output >= source) |
| **Merge PDF** (`MergeController`) | 2+ PDFs -> 1 combined PDF | Ghostscript multi-input `pdfwrite` / `QPdfDocument` + `QPdfWriter` | Interactive **Merge Order** list (drag-and-drop, `Move Up`, `Move Down`, `Sync from Drop Zone`), total page count validation |
| **Split PDF** (`SplitController`) | 1 PDF -> Multiple PDFs in target folder | Ghostscript (`-dFirstPage` / `-dLastPage`) / `QPdfDocument` + `QPdfWriter` | Custom 1-based page ranges (`1-3, 5, 8-12`), fixed interval (`Every N pages`), or extract every page individually |
| **Organize / Rotate / Delete Pages** (`OrganizeController`) | 1 PDF -> 1 reorganized PDF | `QPdfDocument` + `QPdfWriter` (300 DPI `ExactMatch`) | Live page thumbnail list, multi-selection, `-90°` / `+90°` / `180°` rotation, drag-and-drop reordering, page deletion guard, reset |
| **PDF to Images** (`PdfToImagesController`) | 1 PDF -> Page images (`PNG`, `JPG`, `WEBP`, `TIFF`) | `QPdfDocument` (`renderPdfPageOnWhite`) | Output format selector, target DPI (`72`, `150`, `300`, `600 DPI`), all pages or custom 1-based page range filter |
| **Images to Merged PDF** (`ImagesToPdfController`) | 1+ Images -> 1 multi-page PDF | `QImageReader` (EXIF auto-orient) + `QPdfWriter` | Drag-and-drop image ordering, Page Size (`Fit to Image`, `A4`, `Letter`), Orientation (`Auto`, `Portrait`, `Landscape`), Margin (`0-54 pt`) |
| **PDF to Grayscale** (`GrayscaleController`) | 1+ PDFs -> 1+ grayscale PDFs | Ghostscript (`/DeviceGray`) / `QImage::Format_Grayscale8` + `QPdfWriter` | Single or batch monochrome conversion, preserves vector text via Ghostscript |

#### 1. Compress PDF (`CompressController`)
- **Compression Presets**:
  - `Web / Email` (Quality `20`)
  - `Balanced` (Quality `50`)
  - `High Quality (preserve images)` (Quality `80`)
  - `Custom` (Quality `0-100`, automatically selected when adjusting the slider manually)
- **Adaptive Stream Encoding**:
  - Below quality `80`: Downsamples embedded color and grayscale images (`100-300 DPI` bicubic) and tunes Ghostscript `pdfwrite` JPEG `QFactor` without aggressive chroma subsampling (`HSamples [1 1 1 1]` / `VSamples [1 1 1 1]`) to keep text and line art sharp.
  - At quality `80` and above: Disables lossy JPEG re-encoding of color/grayscale images and preserves native image resolution with lossless `FlateEncode` while stripping redundant PDF objects and compressing uncompressed streams.
- **Smart Size Guard**: Compares the validated candidate PDF size against the original file. If re-encoding does not reduce the file size, the original file is kept untouched (or copied cleanly when saving to a new path) and marked as `Unchanged (already optimal)`.

#### 2. Merge PDF (`MergeController`)
- Combines two or more PDF files into a single output document.
- Provides an interactive **Merge Order** card where files can be reordered via drag-and-drop, **Move Up**, **Move Down**, or re-synchronized with the drop zone via **Sync from Drop Zone**.
- Verifies that the merged output PDF contains the exact sum of pages across all input documents before atomic commit.

#### 3. Split PDF (`SplitController`)
- Splits a source PDF into multiple output PDFs inside a user-selected destination directory using one of three modes:
  - **Custom Page Ranges**: Accepts 1-based comma-separated pages and ranges (for example, `1-3, 5, 8-12`). Each token produces a dedicated output PDF (`<basename>_pages_1-3.pdf`, `<basename>_page_5.pdf`).
  - **Fixed Interval**: Splits the document every `N` pages (`1-1000`), automatically clamping the final chunk to the document's last page.
  - **Extract All Pages**: Exports every page of the source PDF as an individual `<basename>_page_N.pdf` file.

#### 4. Organize / Rotate / Delete Pages (`OrganizeController`)
- Renders miniature page thumbnails (`56x72` icon slots with annotations and preserved aspect ratio) as soon as a PDF is dropped.
- Supports single and multi-page selection with actions to **Rotate Left (-90°)**, **Rotate Right (+90°)**, **Rotate 180°**, **Move Up**, **Move Down**, **Delete Page** (with a safeguard preventing deletion of the final remaining page), and **Reset** to restore the original page sequence and orientations.
- Exports the modified page sequence at `300 DPI` using `QPageSize::ExactMatch` point dimensions, swapping page width and height whenever a net `90°` or `270°` rotation is applied.

#### 5. PDF to Images (`PdfToImagesController`)
- Exports either all pages or a custom 1-based page selection (`1-3, 5`) from a PDF into a chosen output folder.
- Configurable **Image Format** (`PNG` with lossless `-1` compression, or `JPG`, `WEBP`, `TIFF` at quality `92`) and **Render DPI** (`72 DPI`, `150 DPI`, `300 DPI`, or `600 DPI`).
- Renders every page over an opaque white canvas (`renderPdfPageOnWhite`) with annotations enabled.

#### 6. Images to Merged PDF (`ImagesToPdfController`)
- Automatically switches the `PdfPage` drop zone to accept image files (`JPG`, `JPEG`, `PNG`, `WEBP`, `BMP`, `TIFF`, `GIF`).
- Provides an interactive **Image Page Order** list supporting drag-and-drop reordering, **Move Up**, **Move Down**, and **Sync from Drop Zone**.
- Reads images with `QImageReader::setAutoTransform(true)` so camera EXIF orientation tags are honored automatically.
- Configurable layout options:
  - **Page Size**: `Fit to Image` (`72 DPI` 1:1 pixel-to-point mapping), `A4`, or `Letter`
  - **Orientation**: `Auto (Match Image)`, `Portrait`, or `Landscape`
  - **Page Margin**: `None (0 pt)`, `Small (18 pt)`, `Medium (36 pt)`, or `Large (54 pt)`

#### 7. PDF to Grayscale (`GrayscaleController`)
- Converts single or multiple PDF files to monochrome (`/DeviceGray`).
- Uses Ghostscript (`-sColorConversionStrategy=Gray -dProcessColorModel=/DeviceGray`) to convert color spaces while keeping vector text intact, or falls back to `QImage::Format_Grayscale8` page rendering via `QPdfWriter`.

### UI-Kit Design System

`ImageConverter` is built on `ui_kit` (`ui-kit/`), a standalone, zero-dependency C++17 Qt Widgets static library inspired by Shadcn UI:

- **Tokenized Theme Engine (`ui::Theme`)**: Instant runtime switching between **Light**, **Dark**, and **System** (OS-synchronized) themes without restarting the application. All colors (`ThemeColors`), spacing (`ThemeSpacing`), corner radii (`ThemeRadius`), and font scales (`ThemeTypography`) are resolved dynamically without hardcoded QSS hex values.
- **30 Reusable Qt Widgets Components**:
  - `Accordion`, `Alert`, `Avatar`, `Badge`, `Button` (with child `Spinner` loading state), `Card`, `Checkbox` (including tri-state and error states), `Combobox` (searchable model/proxy popup), `Dialog`, `EmptyState`, `FormField`, `Input`, `Menu` (`DropdownMenu` / `ContextMenu`), `NumberInput` / `DecimalInput`, `Popover`, `Progress`, `RadioGroup`, `ScrollArea`, `Select`, `Separator`, `Sidebar`, `Slider`, `Spinner`, `Splitter` (keyboard-accessible handle), `Switch`, `Table`, `Tabs`, `Textarea`, `Toast`, and `Tooltip`.
- **Dedicated Documentation & Standalone Demo**:
  - Library reference and CMake integration guide: [`ui-kit/README.md`](ui-kit/README.md)
  - AI agent component API & rules guide: [`ui-kit/AI_AGENT_GUIDE.md`](ui-kit/AI_AGENT_GUIDE.md)
  - Interactive 30-component showcase executable: build with `-DUI_KIT_BUILD_DEMO=ON` to produce the standalone `ui_kit_demo` binary (`ui-kit/demo/`).

## Tech Stack

| Layer | Technology |
| :--- | :--- |
| **Language** | C++17 (`CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD 17`) |
| **Framework** | Qt 6 (`Core`, `Gui`, `Widgets`, `Pdf`, `Concurrent`, `Svg`) with Qt 5 fallback support |
| **Design System** | Custom static `ui_kit` library (`ui-kit/`) + bundled Lucide SVG icons via `CMAKE_AUTORCC` |
| **PDF Engines** | Ghostscript (`pdfwrite` CLI pipeline) + Qt PDF (`QPdfDocument` / `QPdfWriter`) automatic fallback |
| **Build & Packaging** | CMake 3.16+, Ninja, CPack (`.deb`, `.rpm`, Windows portable `.zip`) |

---

## Architecture & Directory Layout

```text
ImageConverter/
├── .github/workflows/                # CI/CD release pipelines (Linux .deb/.rpm & Windows .zip)
├── controllers/                      # Headless PDF processing engines & background task controllers
│   ├── pdf_tool_controller.h/.cpp    # Abstract QObject base class (CancellationToken, ProgressCallback)
│   ├── pdf_engine_utils.h/.cpp       # Ghostscript lookup, PDF validation, white-page rendering, range parser
│   ├── compress_controller.h/.cpp    # Single & batch PDF compression (Ghostscript + Qt fallback)
│   ├── merge_controller.h/.cpp       # Multi-PDF merging with total page count verification
│   ├── split_controller.h/.cpp       # PDF splitting (custom ranges, fixed intervals, extract all)
│   ├── organize_controller.h/.cpp    # Thumbnail loading, per-page rotation, reordering & deletion
│   ├── pdf_to_images_controller.h/.cpp # PDF page export to PNG/JPG/WEBP/TIFF at 72-600 DPI
│   ├── images_to_pdf_controller.h/.cpp # Image sequence to PDF assembly (page size, orientation, margins)
│   └── grayscale_controller.h/.cpp   # Single & batch monochrome (/DeviceGray) PDF conversion
├── mainwindow/                       # Top-level shell window, header bar, theme toggle & tab routing
│   └── mainwindow.h/.cpp
├── pages/                            # Application views
│   ├── main_page.h/.cpp              # Image conversion UI & asynchronous batch orchestration
│   └── pdf_page.h/.cpp               # Unified 7-mode PDF Toolkit UI & controller coordination
├── widgets/                          # Application-specific composite widgets
│   ├── drop_file_widget.h/.cpp       # Drag-and-drop file queue, status badges & image save helpers
│   ├── slider_widget.h/.cpp          # Synchronized slider + spinbox quality control
│   ├── button_action.h/.cpp          # Primary action button wrapper
│   ├── input_widget.h/.cpp           # Labeled form input & format selector
│   └── message_box_widget.h/.cpp     # Styled modal alert dialog wrapper
├── ui-kit/                           # Standalone Qt Widgets component library (static target: ui_kit)
│   ├── theme/                        # Tokens, Theme manager, StyleHelper, AnimationHelper, Icons
│   ├── components/                   # 30 reusable UI components (Button, Card, Combobox, Dialog, etc.)
│   ├── demo/                         # Standalone interactive showcase (target: ui_kit_demo)
│   ├── README.md                     # Standalone documentation for embedding ui-kit in other projects
│   └── AI_AGENT_GUIDE.md             # Component usage rules and API reference for AI coding agents
├── resources/                        # Qt Resource manifest (icons.qrc) & Lucide SVG vector assets
├── styles/                           # Legacy text and color helpers
├── CHANGELOG.md                      # Release history and versioned architectural changes
├── improvement-todo.md               # Engineering roadmap and verified milestone checklist
├── CMakeLists.txt                    # Root CMake build & CPack packaging configuration
└── main.cpp                          # Application entry point & theme initialization
```

### UI-to-Controller Separation
- **View Layer (`pages/pdf_page.*`)**: Manages mode selection, dynamic option cards, file dialogs, and progress UI updates on the Qt GUI thread.
- **Controller Layer (`controllers/*`)**: Subclasses of `PdfToolController` encapsulate all file I/O, Ghostscript process execution, and `QPdfDocument` / `QPdfWriter` rendering on background worker threads via `QtConcurrent::run`. Controllers have zero `QWidget` dependencies and communicate exclusively through thread-safe `CancellationToken` flags, `ProgressCallback` invocables, and `TaskResult` structures.

### Ghostscript Discovery Order
`PdfEngineUtils::findGhostscriptExecutable()` locates the Ghostscript binary in the following priority order:
1. `IMAGECONVERTER_GHOSTSCRIPT` environment variable (if set to a valid executable path).
2. Bundled portable binary alongside the application executable (`<appDir>/gs/bin/gswin64c.exe` on Windows or `<appDir>/gs/bin/gs` on Linux).
3. System `PATH` (`gswin64c`, `gswin32c`, or `gs`).
4. If no Ghostscript binary is found, operations automatically use the built-in Qt PDF (`QPdfDocument` + `QPdfWriter`) engine.

---

## Installation

Download the latest pre-built release packages from [**GitHub Releases**](https://github.com/illufoxKusanagi/image-converter/releases):

### Windows (Portable `.zip`)
1. Download `ImageConverter-windows-x64.zip` from the latest release.
2. Extract the archive to any folder.
3. Run `ImageConverter.exe` (no installer required; includes bundled Ghostscript for vector-preserving PDF operations).

### Debian / Ubuntu (`.deb`)
```bash
sudo apt install ./image-converter-*.deb
```

### Fedora / RHEL (`.rpm`)
```bash
sudo dnf install ./image-converter-*.rpm
```

---

## Usage Guide

### 1. Convert Images (`Convert Image` Tab)
1. Open the **Convert Image** tab.
2. Drag and drop one or more image or PDF files into the drop zone, or click to browse.
3. Adjust the **Image Quality** slider (`0-100`) and choose the target **Output Format** (`JPG`, `JPEG`, `PNG`, `WEBP`, `TIFF`, `BMP`, `GIF`, or `PDF`).
4. Click **Convert Files**:
   - For a single input file, choose the output file path in the save dialog.
   - For multiple input files (or when converting a multi-page PDF to images), choose the output folder. When converting multiple images into `PDF`, you can choose to combine all images into a single multi-page PDF or export separate PDFs.

### 2. Process PDFs (`PDF Tools` Tab)
1. Switch to the **PDF Tools** tab and select one of the seven tools from the **PDF Tool** selector:
   - **Compress PDF**: Drop one or more PDFs, pick a preset (`Web / Email`, `Balanced`, `High Quality`, or `Custom`), and click **Compress PDF**.
   - **Merge PDF**: Drop at least two PDFs, arrange their sequence in the **Merge Order** list, and click **Merge PDFs** to save the combined file.
   - **Split PDF**: Drop a PDF, select `Custom Page Ranges` (e.g., `1-3, 5, 8-12`), `Fixed Interval (Every N Pages)`, or `Extract All Pages (1 Page per PDF)`, and click **Split PDF** to choose an output directory.
   - **Organize / Rotate / Delete Pages**: Drop a PDF to load page thumbnails, use the toolbar buttons or drag-and-drop to rotate, reorder, or remove pages, and click **Save Organized PDF**.
   - **PDF to Images**: Drop a PDF, select the **Image Format** (`PNG`, `JPG`, `WEBP`, `TIFF`), **Render DPI** (`72-600 DPI`), and **Pages to Export** (`All Pages` or `Custom Range`), then click **Export PDF to Images**.
   - **Images to Merged PDF**: Drop one or more images, arrange their page order, select **Page Size** (`Fit to Image`, `A4`, `Letter`), **Orientation**, and **Page Margin**, then click **Create PDF from Images**.
   - **PDF to Grayscale**: Drop one or more PDFs and click **Convert to Grayscale** to produce monochrome PDFs.

### 3. Switching Themes
- Click the **Theme** button in the top-right header bar to cycle between **System**, **Light**, and **Dark** themes at runtime.

---

## Building from Source

### 1. Install Dependencies

#### Ubuntu / Debian
```bash
sudo apt update
sudo apt install -y build-essential cmake ninja-build \
  qt6-base-dev qt6-pdf-dev qt6-svg-dev libgl1-mesa-dev ghostscript
```

#### Fedora
```bash
sudo dnf install -y gcc-c++ cmake ninja-build \
  qt6-qtbase-devel qt6-qtpdf-devel qt6-qtsvg-devel mesa-libGL-devel ghostscript
```

#### Windows
- Install **Qt 6.x** via the [Qt Online Installer](https://www.qt.io/download), selecting **Qt PDF** and **Qt SVG** modules alongside **MinGW 64-bit** or **MSVC 2022 64-bit**.
- Ensure **CMake 3.16+** and **Ninja** are available in `PATH`.
- *(Optional)* Install **Ghostscript** (`gswin64c.exe`) in `PATH` or place a portable Ghostscript build under `gs/bin/` next to the compiled executable.

### 2. Compile the Application

```bash
git clone https://github.com/illufoxKusanagi/image-converter.git
cd image-converter

cmake -B build -G Ninja -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release
cmake --build build --parallel
```

### 3. Build the Standalone UI-Kit Demo (Optional)

To build and launch the interactive 30-component `ui_kit_demo` showcase application:

```bash
cmake -B build -G Ninja -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release -DUI_KIT_BUILD_DEMO=ON
cmake --build build --target ui_kit_demo --parallel
./build/ui-kit/ui_kit_demo
```

### 4. Building in Qt Creator
1. Launch **Qt Creator** and open `CMakeLists.txt` from the repository root.
2. Select a configured **Qt 6** kit (GCC/Clang on Linux, or MinGW 64-bit / MSVC 64-bit on Windows).
3. Build (`Ctrl+B`) and run (`Ctrl+R`).

---

## Packaging

After building in `Release` mode, generate installable packages using `cpack`:

```bash
cd build
cpack -G DEB   # Debian / Ubuntu package (.deb)
cpack -G RPM   # Fedora / RHEL package (.rpm)
cpack -G ZIP   # Portable archive (.zip)
```

FILE: controllers/image/ico_export_controller.cpp
================================================================================
#include "controllers/image/ico_export_controller.h"
#include "controllers/pdf/pdf_utils.h"

#include <QBuffer>
#include <QDir>
#include <QFile>
#include <QFileInfo>
#include <QImage>
#include <QImageReader>
#include <QPainter>
#include <QRectF>
#include <QSaveFile>
#include <QSet>
#include <QSvgRenderer>
#include <QtConcurrent/QtConcurrent>
#include <QtEndian>
#include <algorithm>
#include <cstdint>

namespace {

void appendUInt16LE(QByteArray &out, uint16_t val) {
  const uint16_t le = qToLittleEndian<uint16_t>(val);
  out.append(reinterpret_cast<const char *>(&le), sizeof(uint16_t));
}

void appendUInt32LE(QByteArray &out, uint32_t val) {
  const uint32_t le = qToLittleEndian<uint32_t>(val);
  out.append(reinterpret_cast<const char *>(&le), sizeof(uint32_t));
}

} // namespace

IcoExportController::IcoExportController(QObject *parent)
    : PdfToolController(parent) {
  qRegisterMetaType<IcoExportController::Result>();
}

void IcoExportController::start(const Params &params) {
  waitForRunningJob();
  m_isCancelled = false;
  QFuture<void> future = QtConcurrent::run([this, params]() {
    runJob(params);
  });
  m_watcher.setFuture(future);
}

void IcoExportController::runJob(const Params &params) {
  Result result;
  result.outputDir = params.outputDir;

  QVector<int> validSizes;
  for (int sz : params.sizes) {
    if (sz >= 1 && sz <= 256 && !validSizes.contains(sz)) {
      validSizes.append(sz);
    }
  }
  if (validSizes.isEmpty()) {
    validSizes = {16, 32, 48, 64, 128, 256};
  }
  std::sort(validSizes.begin(), validSizes.end());
  result.sizes = validSizes;

  const QStringList &sourcePaths = params.sourcePaths;
  const int totalFiles = sourcePaths.size();

  emit progressChanged(0, totalFiles, "Building multi-size .ico icons...");

  int iconsCreated = 0;
  int failedFiles = 0;
  QStringList errors;
  QSet<QString> usedFileNames;

  for (int i = 0; i < totalFiles; ++i) {
    if (m_isCancelled) {
      for (int rem = i; rem < totalFiles; ++rem) {
        emit fileStatusChanged(sourcePaths.at(rem),
                               static_cast<int>(FileStatus::Cancelled),
                               "Icon export cancelled");
      }
      break;
    }

    const QString &sourcePath = sourcePaths.at(i);
    const QFileInfo sourceInfo(sourcePath);

    emit fileStatusChanged(
        sourcePath, static_cast<int>(FileStatus::Processing),
        QString("Packing %1 icon size(s) into .ico").arg(validSizes.size()));
    emit progressChanged(
        i, totalFiles,
        QString("Creating icon for %1 (%2 of %3)...")
            .arg(sourceInfo.fileName())
            .arg(i + 1)
            .arg(totalFiles));

    const bool isSvg =
        (sourceInfo.suffix().compare("svg", Qt::CaseInsensitive) == 0);
    QSvgRenderer svgRenderer;
    QImage srcImage;
    QSize svgBaseSize(256, 256);

    if (isSvg) {
      if (!svgRenderer.load(sourceInfo.absoluteFilePath()) ||
          !svgRenderer.isValid()) {
        failedFiles++;
        const QString err = "Invalid or unreadable SVG file.";
        errors.append(QString("%1: %2").arg(sourceInfo.fileName(), err));
        emit fileStatusChanged(sourcePath, static_cast<int>(FileStatus::Failed),
                               err);
        continue;
      }
      svgBaseSize = svgRenderer.defaultSize();
      if (!svgBaseSize.isValid() || svgBaseSize.width() <= 0 ||
          svgBaseSize.height() <= 0) {
        svgBaseSize = svgRenderer.viewBoxF().size().toSize();
      }
      if (!svgBaseSize.isValid() || svgBaseSize.width() <= 0 ||
          svgBaseSize.height() <= 0) {
        svgBaseSize = QSize(256, 256);
      }
    } else {
      QImageReader reader(sourceInfo.absoluteFilePath());
      reader.setAutoTransform(true);
      srcImage = reader.read();
      if (srcImage.isNull()) {
        failedFiles++;
        const QString err = "Cannot load source image.";
        errors.append(QString("%1: %2").arg(sourceInfo.fileName(), err));
        emit fileStatusChanged(sourcePath, static_cast<int>(FileStatus::Failed),
                               err);
        continue;
      }
    }

    struct EncodedFrame {
      int size{0};
      QByteArray pngBytes;
    };
    QVector<EncodedFrame> frames;
    frames.reserve(validSizes.size());
    QString frameError;

    for (int sz : validSizes) {
      if (m_isCancelled) {
        break;
      }

      QImage frame(sz, sz, QImage::Format_ARGB32);
      if (frame.isNull()) {
        frameError = QString("Failed to allocate %1×%1 icon frame.").arg(sz);
        break;
      }
      frame.fill(Qt::transparent);

      if (isSvg) {
        const qreal scale = qMin(static_cast<qreal>(sz) / svgBaseSize.width(),
                                 static_cast<qreal>(sz) / svgBaseSize.height());
        const qreal drawW = svgBaseSize.width() * scale;
        const qreal drawH = svgBaseSize.height() * scale;
        const QRectF targetRect((sz - drawW) / 2.0, (sz - drawH) / 2.0, drawW,
                                drawH);
        QPainter painter(&frame);
        painter.setRenderHint(QPainter::Antialiasing, true);
        painter.setRenderHint(QPainter::SmoothPixmapTransform, true);
        svgRenderer.render(&painter, targetRect);
      } else {
        const QImage scaled =
            srcImage.scaled(sz, sz, Qt::KeepAspectRatio, Qt::SmoothTransformation);
        if (scaled.isNull()) {
          frameError = QString("Failed to scale image to %1×%1.").arg(sz);
          break;
        }
        QPainter painter(&frame);
        painter.setRenderHint(QPainter::SmoothPixmapTransform, true);
        const int offsetX = (sz - scaled.width()) / 2;
        const int offsetY = (sz - scaled.height()) / 2;
        painter.drawImage(offsetX, offsetY, scaled);
      }

      QByteArray pngData;
      QBuffer buf(&pngData);
      if (!buf.open(QIODevice::WriteOnly) || !frame.save(&buf, "PNG", -1)) {
        frameError = QString("Failed to encode %1×%1 PNG payload.").arg(sz);
        break;
      }
      buf.close();
      frames.push_back({sz, pngData});
    }

    if (m_isCancelled) {
      emit fileStatusChanged(sourcePath, static_cast<int>(FileStatus::Cancelled),
                             "Icon export cancelled");
      for (int rem = i + 1; rem < totalFiles; ++rem) {
        emit fileStatusChanged(sourcePaths.at(rem),
                               static_cast<int>(FileStatus::Cancelled),
                               "Icon export cancelled");
      }
      break;
    }

    if (!frameError.isEmpty()) {
      failedFiles++;
      errors.append(QString("%1: %2").arg(sourceInfo.fileName(), frameError));
      emit fileStatusChanged(sourcePath, static_cast<int>(FileStatus::Failed),
                             frameError);
      continue;
    }

    const QString baseStem = sourceInfo.completeBaseName().isEmpty()
                                 ? QStringLiteral("icon")
                                 : sourceInfo.completeBaseName();
    QString uniqueStem = baseStem;
    int suffix = 1;
    while (true) {
      const QString candidateName = uniqueStem + ".ico";
      const QString candidatePath = QDir(params.outputDir).filePath(candidateName);
      const QFileInfo candidateInfo(candidatePath);
      const bool collidesWithSource =
          pdfutil::isSamePdf(sourceInfo, candidateInfo);
      if (!usedFileNames.contains(candidateName.toLower()) &&
          !QFile::exists(candidatePath) && !collidesWithSource) {
        break;
      }
      uniqueStem = QString("%1_%2").arg(baseStem).arg(suffix++);
    }
    const QString outFileName = uniqueStem + ".ico";
    usedFileNames.insert(outFileName.toLower());
    const QString outIcoPath = QDir(params.outputDir).filePath(outFileName);

    QByteArray icoHeader;
    icoHeader.reserve(6 + 16 * frames.size());
    appendUInt16LE(icoHeader, 0); // idReserved = 0
    appendUInt16LE(icoHeader, 1); // idType = 1 (ICO)
    appendUInt16LE(icoHeader, static_cast<uint16_t>(frames.size()));

    uint32_t offset = static_cast<uint32_t>(6 + 16 * frames.size());
    for (const EncodedFrame &ef : frames) {
      const uint8_t bWidth = (ef.size >= 256) ? 0 : static_cast<uint8_t>(ef.size);
      const uint8_t bHeight = (ef.size >= 256) ? 0 : static_cast<uint8_t>(ef.size);
      icoHeader.append(static_cast<char>(bWidth));
      icoHeader.append(static_cast<char>(bHeight));
      icoHeader.append(static_cast<char>(0)); // bColorCount
      icoHeader.append(static_cast<char>(0)); // bReserved
      appendUInt16LE(icoHeader, 1);           // wPlanes = 1
      appendUInt16LE(icoHeader, 32);          // wBitCount = 32
      const uint32_t bytesInRes = static_cast<uint32_t>(ef.pngBytes.size());
      appendUInt32LE(icoHeader, bytesInRes);
      appendUInt32LE(icoHeader, offset);
      offset += bytesInRes;
    }

    QSaveFile saveFile(outIcoPath);
    saveFile.setDirectWriteFallback(false);
    bool writeOk = saveFile.open(QIODevice::WriteOnly);
    if (writeOk) {
      writeOk = (saveFile.write(icoHeader) == icoHeader.size());
    }
    if (writeOk) {
      for (const EncodedFrame &ef : frames) {
        if (saveFile.write(ef.pngBytes) != ef.pngBytes.size()) {
          writeOk = false;
          break;
        }
      }
    }
    if (writeOk) {
      writeOk = saveFile.commit();
    }

    if (!writeOk) {
      failedFiles++;
      const QString err = QString("Failed to write '%1'.").arg(outFileName);
      errors.append(QString("%1: %2").arg(sourceInfo.fileName(), err));
      emit fileStatusChanged(sourcePath, static_cast<int>(FileStatus::Failed),
                             err);
      continue;
    }

    iconsCreated++;
    emit fileStatusChanged(
        sourcePath, static_cast<int>(FileStatus::Succeeded),
        QString("Saved %1 (%2 resolution(s))")
            .arg(outFileName)
            .arg(frames.size()));
    emit progressChanged(
        i + 1, totalFiles,
        QString("Created %1 of %2 icon(s)").arg(i + 1).arg(totalFiles));
  }

  result.success = (iconsCreated > 0 && failedFiles == 0 && !m_isCancelled);
  result.cancelled = m_isCancelled.load();
  result.iconsCreated = iconsCreated;
  result.failedFiles = failedFiles;
  result.errors = errors;
  if (!errors.isEmpty()) {
    result.error = errors.join("\n");
  }

  emit finished(result);
}

================================================================================
FILE: controllers/image/ico_export_controller.h
================================================================================
#ifndef ICO_EXPORT_CONTROLLER_H
#define ICO_EXPORT_CONTROLLER_H

#include "controllers/pdf/pdf_tool_controller.h"
#include <QString>
#include <QStringList>
#include <QVector>

class IcoExportController : public PdfToolController {
  Q_OBJECT

public:
  struct Params {
    QStringList sourcePaths;
    QString outputDir;
    QVector<int> sizes{16, 32, 48, 64, 128, 256};
  };

  struct Result {
    bool success{false};
    bool cancelled{false};
    int iconsCreated{0};
    int failedFiles{0};
    QString outputDir;
    QVector<int> sizes;
    QString error;
    QStringList errors;
  };

  explicit IcoExportController(QObject *parent = nullptr);
  ~IcoExportController() override { cancelAndWait(); }

  void start(const Params &params);

signals:
  void finished(const IcoExportController::Result &result);

private:
  void runJob(const Params &params);
};

Q_DECLARE_METATYPE(IcoExportController::Result)

#endif // ICO_EXPORT_CONTROLLER_H

================================================================================
FILE: controllers/image/svg_convert_controller.cpp
================================================================================
#include "controllers/image/svg_convert_controller.h"
#include "controllers/pdf/pdf_utils.h"

#include <QCoreApplication>
#include <QDateTime>
#include <QDir>
#include <QFile>
#include <QFileInfo>
#include <QImage>
#include <QMarginsF>
#include <QPageSize>
#include <QPainter>
#include <QPdfDocument>
#include <QPdfWriter>
#include <QRectF>
#include <QSaveFile>
#include <QSet>
#include <QSvgRenderer>
#include <QtConcurrent/QtConcurrent>

SvgConvertController::SvgConvertController(QObject *parent)
    : PdfToolController(parent) {
  qRegisterMetaType<SvgConvertController::Result>();
}

void SvgConvertController::start(const Params &params) {
  waitForRunningJob();
  m_isCancelled = false;
  QFuture<void> future = QtConcurrent::run([this, params]() {
    runJob(params);
  });
  m_watcher.setFuture(future);
}

void SvgConvertController::runJob(const Params &params) {
  Result result;
  result.outputDir = params.outputDir;
  const QString format = params.format.trimmed().toLower();
  result.format = format;

  const QStringList &sourcePaths = params.sourcePaths;
  const int totalFiles = sourcePaths.size();
  const int effectiveQuality =
      (format == "png") ? -1 : qBound(1, params.quality, 100);
  const bool useWhiteBg = (format == "jpg" || format == "jpeg" || params.whiteBackground);

  emit progressChanged(0, totalFiles, "Converting SVG vector files...");

  int filesConverted = 0;
  int failedFiles = 0;
  QStringList errors;
  QSet<QString> usedFileNames;

  for (int i = 0; i < totalFiles; ++i) {
    if (m_isCancelled) {
      for (int rem = i; rem < totalFiles; ++rem) {
        emit fileStatusChanged(sourcePaths.at(rem),
                               static_cast<int>(FileStatus::Cancelled),
                               "Conversion cancelled");
      }
      break;
    }

    const QString &sourcePath = sourcePaths.at(i);
    const QFileInfo sourceInfo(sourcePath);

    emit fileStatusChanged(
        sourcePath, static_cast<int>(FileStatus::Processing),
        QString("Rendering SVG to %1").arg(format.toUpper()));
    emit progressChanged(
        i, totalFiles,
        QString("Converting %1 (%2 of %3)...")
            .arg(sourceInfo.fileName())
            .arg(i + 1)
            .arg(totalFiles));

    QSvgRenderer renderer(sourceInfo.absoluteFilePath());
    if (!renderer.isValid()) {
      failedFiles++;
      const QString err = "Invalid or unreadable SVG file.";
      errors.append(QString("%1: %2").arg(sourceInfo.fileName(), err));
      emit fileStatusChanged(sourcePath, static_cast<int>(FileStatus::Failed),
                             err);
      continue;
    }

    QSize baseSize = renderer.defaultSize();
    if (!baseSize.isValid() || baseSize.width() <= 0 || baseSize.height() <= 0) {
      baseSize = renderer.viewBoxF().size().toSize();
    }
    if (!baseSize.isValid() || baseSize.width() <= 0 || baseSize.height() <= 0) {
      baseSize = QSize(512, 512);
    }

    QSize targetSize = baseSize;
    if (params.scaleMode == 1) {
      targetSize = baseSize * 2;
    } else if (params.scaleMode == 2) {
      targetSize = baseSize * 4;
    } else if (params.scaleMode == 3) {
      targetSize = QSize(qBound(1, params.customWidth, 16384),
                         qBound(1, params.customHeight, 16384));
    }

    if (targetSize.width() > 16384 || targetSize.height() > 16384) {
      targetSize.scale(16384, 16384, Qt::KeepAspectRatio);
    }
    targetSize.setWidth(qBound(1, targetSize.width(), 16384));
    targetSize.setHeight(qBound(1, targetSize.height(), 16384));

    QRectF renderRect(0.0, 0.0, targetSize.width(), targetSize.height());
    if (params.preserveAspectRatio && baseSize.width() > 0 &&
        baseSize.height() > 0) {
      const qreal scale =
          qMin(static_cast<qreal>(targetSize.width()) / baseSize.width(),
               static_cast<qreal>(targetSize.height()) / baseSize.height());
      const qreal drawW = baseSize.width() * scale;
      const qreal drawH = baseSize.height() * scale;
      renderRect = QRectF((targetSize.width() - drawW) / 2.0,
                          (targetSize.height() - drawH) / 2.0, drawW, drawH);
    }

    const QString baseStem = sourceInfo.completeBaseName().isEmpty()
                                 ? QStringLiteral("vector")
                                 : sourceInfo.completeBaseName();
    QString uniqueStem = baseStem;
    int suffix = 1;
    while (true) {
      const QString candidateName = uniqueStem + "." + format;
      const QString candidatePath = QDir(params.outputDir).filePath(candidateName);
      const QFileInfo candidateInfo(candidatePath);
      const bool collidesWithSource =
          pdfutil::isSamePdf(sourceInfo, candidateInfo);
      if (!usedFileNames.contains(candidateName.toLower()) &&
          !QFile::exists(candidatePath) && !collidesWithSource) {
        break;
      }
      uniqueStem = QString("%1_%2").arg(baseStem).arg(suffix++);
    }
    const QString outFileName = uniqueStem + "." + format;
    usedFileNames.insert(outFileName.toLower());
    const QString outPath = QDir(params.outputDir).filePath(outFileName);

    QString fileError;

    if (format == "pdf") {
      const QString tempPath =
          outPath + QString(".svg_tmp_%1_%2")
                        .arg(QCoreApplication::applicationPid())
                        .arg(QDateTime::currentMSecsSinceEpoch());
      {
        QPdfWriter writer(tempPath);
        writer.setResolution(72);
        writer.setPageMargins(QMarginsF(0, 0, 0, 0));
        writer.setPageSize(QPageSize(QSizeF(targetSize), QPageSize::Point,
                                     QString(), QPageSize::ExactMatch));

        QPainter painter;
        if (!painter.begin(&writer)) {
          fileError = "Failed to initialize PDF writer.";
        } else {
          painter.setRenderHint(QPainter::Antialiasing, true);
          painter.setRenderHint(QPainter::SmoothPixmapTransform, true);
          if (useWhiteBg) {
            painter.fillRect(
                QRectF(0.0, 0.0, targetSize.width(), targetSize.height()),
                Qt::white);
          }
          renderer.render(&painter, renderRect);
          if (!painter.end()) {
            fileError = "Failed to finalize PDF rendering.";
          }
        }
      }

      if (fileError.isEmpty() && !m_isCancelled) {
        QPdfDocument verifyDoc;
        if (verifyDoc.load(tempPath) != QPdfDocument::Error::None ||
            verifyDoc.pageCount() <= 0) {
          fileError = "Generated PDF failed validation.";
        } else {
          verifyDoc.close();
          if (!pdfutil::savePdfAtomically(tempPath, outPath, m_isCancelled,
                                          fileError)) {
            if (fileError.isEmpty() && !m_isCancelled) {
              fileError = "Failed to commit PDF file.";
            }
          }
        }
      }
      QFile::remove(tempPath);
    } else {
      const QImage::Format imgFormat =
          useWhiteBg ? QImage::Format_RGB32
                     : QImage::Format_ARGB32_Premultiplied;
      QImage img(targetSize, imgFormat);
      if (img.isNull()) {
        fileError = "Failed to allocate raster image buffer.";
      } else {
        img.fill(useWhiteBg ? Qt::white : Qt::transparent);
        {
          QPainter painter(&img);
          painter.setRenderHint(QPainter::Antialiasing, true);
          painter.setRenderHint(QPainter::SmoothPixmapTransform, true);
          renderer.render(&painter, renderRect);
        }

        if (!m_isCancelled) {
          QSaveFile saveFile(outPath);
          saveFile.setDirectWriteFallback(false);
          const QByteArray fmtBytes =
              (format == "jpg") ? QByteArray("JPG") : format.toUpper().toUtf8();
          if (!saveFile.open(QIODevice::WriteOnly) ||
              !img.save(&saveFile, fmtBytes.constData(), effectiveQuality) ||
              !saveFile.commit()) {
            fileError =
                QString("Failed to save '%1'.").arg(outFileName);
          }
        }
      }
    }

    if (m_isCancelled) {
      emit fileStatusChanged(sourcePath, static_cast<int>(FileStatus::Cancelled),
                             "Conversion cancelled");
      for (int rem = i + 1; rem < totalFiles; ++rem) {
        emit fileStatusChanged(sourcePaths.at(rem),
                               static_cast<int>(FileStatus::Cancelled),
                               "Conversion cancelled");
      }
      break;
    }

    if (fileError.isEmpty()) {
      filesConverted++;
      emit fileStatusChanged(
          sourcePath, static_cast<int>(FileStatus::Succeeded),
          QString("Saved %1 (%2×%3)")
              .arg(outFileName)
              .arg(targetSize.width())
              .arg(targetSize.height()));
    } else {
      failedFiles++;
      errors.append(QString("%1: %2").arg(sourceInfo.fileName(), fileError));
      emit fileStatusChanged(sourcePath, static_cast<int>(FileStatus::Failed),
                             fileError);
    }

    emit progressChanged(
        i + 1, totalFiles,
        QString("Converted %1 of %2").arg(i + 1).arg(totalFiles));
  }

  result.success = (filesConverted > 0 && failedFiles == 0 && !m_isCancelled);
  result.cancelled = m_isCancelled.load();
  result.filesConverted = filesConverted;
  result.failedFiles = failedFiles;
  result.errors = errors;
  if (!errors.isEmpty()) {
    result.error = errors.join("\n");
  }

  emit finished(result);
}

================================================================================
FILE: controllers/image/svg_convert_controller.h
================================================================================
#ifndef SVG_CONVERT_CONTROLLER_H
#define SVG_CONVERT_CONTROLLER_H

#include "controllers/pdf/pdf_tool_controller.h"
#include <QString>
#include <QStringList>

class SvgConvertController : public PdfToolController {
  Q_OBJECT

public:
  struct Params {
    QStringList sourcePaths;
    QString outputDir;
    QString format{"png"}; // "png", "webp", "jpg", "pdf"
    int scaleMode{0};      // 0 = 1x, 1 = 2x, 2 = 4x, 3 = Custom W x H px
    int customWidth{512};
    int customHeight{512};
    bool preserveAspectRatio{true};
    bool whiteBackground{false}; // always true if format == "jpg"
    int quality{90};             // used for webp/jpg; -1 for png
  };

  struct Result {
    bool success{false};
    bool cancelled{false};
    int filesConverted{0};
    int failedFiles{0};
    QString outputDir;
    QString format;
    QString error;
    QStringList errors;
  };

  explicit SvgConvertController(QObject *parent = nullptr);
  ~SvgConvertController() override { cancelAndWait(); }

  void start(const Params &params);

signals:
  void finished(const SvgConvertController::Result &result);

private:
  void runJob(const Params &params);
};

Q_DECLARE_METATYPE(SvgConvertController::Result)

#endif // SVG_CONVERT_CONTROLLER_H

================================================================================
FILE: controllers/pdf/compress_controller.cpp
================================================================================
#include "controllers/pdf/compress_controller.h"
#include "controllers/pdf/pdf_utils.h"

#include <QDateTime>
#include <QDir>
#include <QFile>
#include <QPdfDocument>
#include <QSet>
#include <QTemporaryDir>
#include <QtConcurrent/QtConcurrent>

CompressController::CompressController(QObject *parent)
    : PdfToolController(parent) {
  qRegisterMetaType<CompressController::Result>();
}

void CompressController::start(const Params &params) {
  waitForRunningJob();
  m_isCancelled = false;
  QFuture<void> future = QtConcurrent::run([this, params]() {
    runJob(params);
  });
  m_watcher.setFuture(future);
}

bool CompressController::compressPdfWithGhostscript(
    const QString &executable, const QString &inputPath,
    const QString &tempOutputPath, int quality, QString &error, bool grayscale,
    const std::atomic<bool> *cancelled) {
  const int clampedQuality = qBound(0, quality, 100);
  const bool preserveImages = clampedQuality >= 80;
  QStringList args = {"-sDEVICE=pdfwrite",
                      "-dCompatibilityLevel=1.7",
                      "-dSAFER",
                      "-dPDFSTOPONERROR",
                      "-dNOPAUSE",
                      "-dQUIET",
                      "-dBATCH",
                      "-dAutoRotatePages=/None",
                      "-dDetectDuplicateImages=true",
                      "-dCompressFonts=true",
                      "-dCompressStreams=true",
                      "-dSubsetFonts=true",
                      "-dPreserveAnnots=true",
                      "-dPreserveMarkedContent=true",
                      "-dWantsOptionalContent=true",
                      "-dDownsampleMonoImages=false"};

  if (grayscale) {
    args << "-sColorConversionStrategy=Gray"
         << "-dProcessColorModel=/DeviceGray";
  } else {
    args << "-sColorConversionStrategy=LeaveColorUnchanged"
         << "-dPassThroughJPEGImages=true"
         << "-dPassThroughJPXImages=true";
  }

  if (preserveImages) {
    args << "-dDownsampleColorImages=false" << "-dDownsampleGrayImages=false"
         << "-dAutoFilterColorImages=false" << "-dAutoFilterGrayImages=false"
         << "-dColorImageFilter=/FlateEncode"
         << "-dGrayImageFilter=/FlateEncode";
  } else {
    const int targetDpi = 100 + (200 * clampedQuality) / 80;
    args << "-dDownsampleColorImages=true" << "-dDownsampleGrayImages=true"
         << "-dColorImageDownsampleType=/Bicubic"
         << "-dGrayImageDownsampleType=/Bicubic"
         << "-dColorImageDownsampleThreshold=1.5"
         << "-dGrayImageDownsampleThreshold=1.5"
         << QString("-dColorImageResolution=%1").arg(targetDpi)
         << QString("-dGrayImageResolution=%1").arg(targetDpi)
         << "-dAutoFilterColorImages=true" << "-dAutoFilterGrayImages=true";
  }

  const QString imageParameters =
      QString("<< /QFactor %1 /Blend 1 /ColorTransform 1 "
              "/HSamples [1 1 1 1] /VSamples [1 1 1 1] >>")
          .arg(0.6 - 0.45 * clampedQuality / 80.0, 0, 'f', 3);
  const QString distillerParameters =
      preserveImages
          ? QString("<< /NeverEmbed [] >> setdistillerparams")
          : QString("<< /NeverEmbed [] /ColorImageDict %1 /GrayImageDict %1 "
                    "/ColorACSImageDict %1 /GrayACSImageDict %1 >> "
                    "setdistillerparams")
                .arg(imageParameters);
  args << "-sOutputFile=candidate.pdf" << "-c" << distillerParameters << "-f"
       << QDir::toNativeSeparators(inputPath);

  std::atomic<bool> defaultCancelled{false};
  const std::atomic<bool> &cancelRef = cancelled ? *cancelled : defaultCancelled;
  return pdfutil::runGhostscriptProcess(executable, args,
                                        QFileInfo(tempOutputPath).absolutePath(),
                                        tempOutputPath, cancelRef, error, true);
}

CompressController::SingleResult
CompressController::compressPdf(const QString &inputPath,
                                const QString &outputPath, int quality) {
  SingleResult result;
  if (m_isCancelled) {
    result.status = Result::Status::Cancelled;
    return result;
  }

  const QFileInfo sourceInfo(inputPath);
  const QFileInfo destinationInfo(outputPath);
  result.originalBytes = sourceInfo.size();
  const QDateTime sourceModified = sourceInfo.lastModified();
  if (!sourceInfo.isFile() || result.originalBytes <= 0) {
    result.error = "The input PDF is missing or empty.";
    return result;
  }
  if (pdfutil::isSamePdf(sourceInfo, destinationInfo)) {
    result.error = "Choose a different output file. The original PDF must not "
                   "be overwritten.";
    return result;
  }

  const QString executable = pdfutil::findGhostscriptExecutable();
  if (executable.isEmpty()) {
    result.error = "Ghostscript is required for PDF compression. Install the "
                   "ghostscript package on Linux or include the complete "
                   "gs runtime beside the Windows application. For an "
                   "extracted runtime, set IMAGECONVERTER_GHOSTSCRIPT to its "
                   "executable's full path. No PDF was changed.";
    return result;
  }

  QPdfDocument sourceDocument;
  const auto sourceError = sourceDocument.load(sourceInfo.absoluteFilePath());
  if (sourceError != QPdfDocument::Error::None ||
      sourceDocument.pageCount() <= 0) {
    result.error = QString("Cannot open the input PDF (PDF error %1). "
                           "Check whether it is damaged or password-protected.")
                       .arg(static_cast<int>(sourceError));
    return result;
  }

  QTemporaryDir temporaryDirectory(
      destinationInfo.absoluteDir().filePath(".pdf-compress-XXXXXX"));
  if (!temporaryDirectory.isValid()) {
    result.error = QString("Cannot create temporary output in the destination "
                           "directory: %1")
                       .arg(temporaryDirectory.errorString());
    return result;
  }
  const QString candidatePath = temporaryDirectory.filePath("candidate.pdf");
  if (!compressPdfWithGhostscript(executable, sourceInfo.absoluteFilePath(),
                                  candidatePath, quality, result.error,
                                  false, &m_isCancelled)) {
    if (m_isCancelled) {
      result.status = Result::Status::Cancelled;
    }
    return result;
  }

  QPdfDocument candidateDocument;
  const auto candidateError = candidateDocument.load(candidatePath);
  if (candidateError != QPdfDocument::Error::None ||
      candidateDocument.pageCount() != sourceDocument.pageCount()) {
    result.error = "The compressed PDF failed validation (unreadable output "
                   "or changed page count). No destination was replaced.";
    return result;
  }
  for (int page = 0; page < sourceDocument.pageCount(); ++page) {
    if (m_isCancelled) {
      result.status = Result::Status::Cancelled;
      return result;
    }
    const QSizeF before = sourceDocument.pagePointSize(page);
    const QSizeF after = candidateDocument.pagePointSize(page);
    if (qAbs(before.width() - after.width()) > 0.5 ||
        qAbs(before.height() - after.height()) > 0.5) {
      result.error = "The compressed PDF changed page dimensions. "
                     "No destination was replaced.";
      return result;
    }
  }
  candidateDocument.close();
  sourceDocument.close();

  if (m_isCancelled) {
    result.status = Result::Status::Cancelled;
    return result;
  }
  const QFileInfo currentSource(sourceInfo.absoluteFilePath());
  if (currentSource.size() != result.originalBytes ||
      currentSource.lastModified() != sourceModified) {
    result.error = "The input PDF changed during compression. Please retry.";
    return result;
  }
  const qint64 candidateBytes = QFileInfo(candidatePath).size();
  if (candidateBytes <= 0) {
    result.error = "The compressed PDF is empty. No destination was replaced.";
    return result;
  }
  if (candidateBytes >= result.originalBytes) {
    result.status = Result::Status::Unchanged;
    result.outputBytes = result.originalBytes;
    return result;
  }

  if (pdfutil::isSamePdf(currentSource, QFileInfo(destinationInfo.absoluteFilePath()))) {
    result.error = "The destination now points to the input PDF. "
                   "No destination was replaced.";
    return result;
  }
  if (!pdfutil::savePdfAtomically(candidatePath, destinationInfo.absoluteFilePath(),
                                  m_isCancelled, result.error)) {
    if (m_isCancelled) {
      result.status = Result::Status::Cancelled;
    }
    return result;
  }
  result.status = Result::Status::Compressed;
  result.outputBytes = candidateBytes;
  return result;
}

void CompressController::runJob(const Params &params) {
  Result result;
  result.isBatch = params.isBatch;

  if (!params.isBatch) {
    const QString &sourcePath = params.sourcePath;
    const QString &outputPath = params.outputPath;
    emit progressChanged(0, 1, QString("Compressing %1 (1 of 1)...")
                                   .arg(QFileInfo(sourcePath).fileName()));
    emit fileStatusChanged(sourcePath, static_cast<int>(FileStatus::Processing),
                           "Optimizing PDF with Ghostscript");

    const SingleResult single = compressPdf(sourcePath, outputPath, params.quality);
    result.status = single.status;
    result.origBytes = single.originalBytes;
    result.outBytes = single.outputBytes;
    result.singleOutputPath = outputPath;

    int statusInt = static_cast<int>(FileStatus::Failed);
    QString detail = single.error;
    switch (single.status) {
    case Result::Status::Compressed:
      statusInt = static_cast<int>(FileStatus::Succeeded);
      detail = QString("Saved to %1").arg(outputPath);
      result.successCount = 1;
      break;
    case Result::Status::Unchanged:
      statusInt = static_cast<int>(FileStatus::Unchanged);
      detail = "No smaller PDF produced; no output saved";
      result.unchangedCount = 1;
      break;
    case Result::Status::Cancelled:
      statusInt = static_cast<int>(FileStatus::Cancelled);
      detail = "Compression cancelled; no output saved";
      break;
    case Result::Status::Failed:
      result.failCount = 1;
      result.errors.append(single.error);
      break;
    }
    emit fileStatusChanged(sourcePath, statusInt, detail);
    if (single.status != Result::Status::Cancelled) {
      emit progressChanged(1, 1, "Completed");
    }
    emit finished(result);
    return;
  }

  // Batch compression
  const QStringList &sourcePaths = params.sourcePaths;
  const int totalFiles = sourcePaths.size();
  emit progressChanged(0, totalFiles,
                       QString("Compressing 0 of %1 PDFs...").arg(totalFiles));

  QSet<QString> usedBaseNames;

  for (int i = 0; i < totalFiles; ++i) {
    if (m_isCancelled) {
      break;
    }

    const QString &sourcePath = sourcePaths.at(i);
    emit fileStatusChanged(sourcePath, static_cast<int>(FileStatus::Processing),
                           "Optimizing PDF with Ghostscript");
    emit progressChanged(
        i, totalFiles,
        QString("Compressing %1 (%2 of %3; %4 completed)...")
            .arg(QFileInfo(sourcePath).fileName())
            .arg(i + 1)
            .arg(totalFiles)
            .arg(i));

    auto collidesWithAnySource = [&](const QString &candidateOut) {
      const QFileInfo candInfo(candidateOut);
      for (const QString &src : sourcePaths) {
        if (pdfutil::isSamePdf(QFileInfo(src), candInfo)) {
          return true;
        }
      }
      return false;
    };

    QFileInfo fileInfo(sourcePath);
    QString baseName = fileInfo.baseName() + "_compressed";
    QString uniqueBaseName = baseName;
    int suffix = 1;
    while (usedBaseNames.contains(uniqueBaseName.toLower()) ||
           QFile::exists(QDir(params.outputDir).filePath(uniqueBaseName + ".pdf")) ||
           collidesWithAnySource(QDir(params.outputDir).filePath(uniqueBaseName + ".pdf"))) {
      uniqueBaseName = QString("%1_%2").arg(baseName).arg(suffix++);
    }
    usedBaseNames.insert(uniqueBaseName.toLower());

    const QString outputPath =
        QDir(params.outputDir).filePath(uniqueBaseName + ".pdf");

    const SingleResult single = compressPdf(sourcePath, outputPath, params.quality);
    int statusInt = static_cast<int>(FileStatus::Failed);
    QString detail = single.error;
    switch (single.status) {
    case Result::Status::Compressed:
      statusInt = static_cast<int>(FileStatus::Succeeded);
      detail = QString("Saved to %1").arg(outputPath);
      break;
    case Result::Status::Unchanged:
      statusInt = static_cast<int>(FileStatus::Unchanged);
      detail = "No smaller PDF produced; no output saved";
      break;
    case Result::Status::Cancelled:
      statusInt = static_cast<int>(FileStatus::Cancelled);
      detail = "Compression cancelled; no output saved";
      break;
    case Result::Status::Failed:
      break;
    }
    emit fileStatusChanged(sourcePath, statusInt, detail);

    if (single.status != Result::Status::Cancelled) {
      emit progressChanged(i + 1, totalFiles,
                           QString("Processed %1 of %2 PDFs")
                               .arg(i + 1)
                               .arg(totalFiles));
    }

    if (single.status == Result::Status::Cancelled) {
      break;
    }
    if (single.status == Result::Status::Compressed) {
      result.successCount++;
      result.origBytes += single.originalBytes;
      result.outBytes += single.outputBytes;
    } else if (single.status == Result::Status::Unchanged) {
      result.unchangedCount++;
    } else {
      result.failCount++;
      if (result.errors.size() < 3) {
        result.errors.append(
            QString("%1: %2").arg(fileInfo.fileName(), single.error));
      }
    }
  }

  if (m_isCancelled) {
    result.status = Result::Status::Cancelled;
  } else {
    result.status = (result.failCount == 0) ? Result::Status::Compressed
                                            : Result::Status::Failed;
  }
  emit finished(result);
}

================================================================================
FILE: controllers/pdf/compress_controller.h
================================================================================
#ifndef COMPRESS_CONTROLLER_H
#define COMPRESS_CONTROLLER_H

#include "controllers/pdf/pdf_tool_controller.h"
#include <QFileInfo>
#include <QString>
#include <QStringList>

class CompressController : public PdfToolController {
  Q_OBJECT

public:
  struct Params {
    bool isBatch{false};
    QString sourcePath;
    QString outputPath;
    QStringList sourcePaths;
    QString outputDir;
    int quality{50};
  };

  struct Result {
    enum class Status {
      Compressed,
      Unchanged,
      Failed,
      Cancelled
    };
    bool isBatch{false};
    Status status{Status::Failed};
    qint64 origBytes{0};
    qint64 outBytes{0};
    int successCount{0};
    int unchangedCount{0};
    int failCount{0};
    QStringList errors;
    QString singleOutputPath;
  };

  struct SingleResult {
    Result::Status status{Result::Status::Failed};
    qint64 originalBytes{0};
    qint64 outputBytes{0};
    QString error;
  };

  explicit CompressController(QObject *parent = nullptr);
  ~CompressController() override { cancelAndWait(); }

  void start(const Params &params);

  static bool compressPdfWithGhostscript(const QString &executable,
                                         const QString &inputPath,
                                         const QString &tempOutputPath,
                                         int quality, QString &error,
                                         bool grayscale = false,
                                         const std::atomic<bool> *cancelled = nullptr);

  SingleResult compressPdf(const QString &inputPath, const QString &outputPath,
                           int quality);

signals:
  void finished(const CompressController::Result &result);

private:
  void runJob(const Params &params);
};

Q_DECLARE_METATYPE(CompressController::Result)

#endif // COMPRESS_CONTROLLER_H

================================================================================
FILE: controllers/pdf/grayscale_controller.cpp
================================================================================
#include "controllers/pdf/grayscale_controller.h"
#include "controllers/pdf/pdf_utils.h"

#include <QDateTime>
#include <QDir>
#include <QFile>
#include <QFileInfo>
#include <QImage>
#include <QPageLayout>
#include <QPageSize>
#include <QPainter>
#include <QPdfWriter>
#include <QSet>
#include <QTemporaryDir>
#include <QtConcurrent/QtConcurrent>
#include <memory>

GrayscaleController::GrayscaleController(QObject *parent)
    : PdfToolController(parent) {
  qRegisterMetaType<GrayscaleController::Result>();
}

void GrayscaleController::start(const Params &params) {
  waitForRunningJob();
  m_isCancelled = false;
  QFuture<void> future = QtConcurrent::run([this, params]() {
    runJob(params);
  });
  m_watcher.setFuture(future);
}

bool GrayscaleController::convertPdfToGrayscaleWithQtPdf(
    QPdfDocument &sourceDoc, const QString &tempOutputPath, int quality,
    QString &error) {
  const int clampedQuality = qBound(0, quality, 100);
  const int dpi =
      (clampedQuality >= 80) ? 300 : (100 + (200 * clampedQuality) / 80);

  QPdfWriter writer(tempOutputPath);
  writer.setResolution(dpi);
  std::unique_ptr<QPainter> painter;

  for (int page = 0; page < sourceDoc.pageCount(); ++page) {
    if (m_isCancelled) {
      return false;
    }
    const QSizeF ptSize = sourceDoc.pagePointSize(page);
    QPageLayout layout(QPageSize(ptSize, QPageSize::Point),
                       QPageLayout::Portrait, QMarginsF(0, 0, 0, 0),
                       QPageLayout::Point);
    writer.setPageLayout(layout);
    if (!painter) {
      painter = std::make_unique<QPainter>(&writer);
      if (!painter->isActive()) {
        error = "Failed to initialize PDF writer.";
        return false;
      }
    } else {
      writer.newPage();
    }

    const QSize renderSize(qMax(1, qRound(ptSize.width() * dpi / 72.0)),
                           qMax(1, qRound(ptSize.height() * dpi / 72.0)));
    const QImage colorImg = pdfutil::renderPdfPageOnWhite(sourceDoc, page, renderSize);
    if (colorImg.isNull()) {
      error = QString("Failed to render page %1.").arg(page + 1);
      return false;
    }
    const QImage grayImg = colorImg.convertToFormat(QImage::Format_Grayscale8);
    painter->setRenderHint(QPainter::SmoothPixmapTransform, true);
    painter->drawImage(
        QRectF(0, 0, painter->device()->width(), painter->device()->height()),
        grayImg);
  }

  if (painter) {
    painter->end();
  }
  return QFileInfo(tempOutputPath).size() > 0;
}

CompressController::SingleResult
GrayscaleController::convertPdfToGrayscale(const QString &inputPath,
                                          const QString &outputPath,
                                          int quality) {
  using Status = CompressController::Result::Status;
  CompressController::SingleResult result;
  if (m_isCancelled) {
    result.status = Status::Cancelled;
    return result;
  }

  const QFileInfo sourceInfo(inputPath);
  const QFileInfo destinationInfo(outputPath);
  result.originalBytes = sourceInfo.size();
  const QDateTime sourceModified = sourceInfo.lastModified();
  if (!sourceInfo.isFile() || result.originalBytes <= 0) {
    result.error = "The input PDF is missing or empty.";
    return result;
  }
  if (pdfutil::isSamePdf(sourceInfo, destinationInfo)) {
    result.error = "Choose a different output file. The original PDF must not "
                   "be overwritten.";
    return result;
  }

  QPdfDocument sourceDocument;
  const auto sourceError = sourceDocument.load(sourceInfo.absoluteFilePath());
  if (sourceError != QPdfDocument::Error::None ||
      sourceDocument.pageCount() <= 0) {
    result.error = QString("Cannot open the input PDF (PDF error %1). "
                           "Check whether it is damaged or password-protected.")
                       .arg(static_cast<int>(sourceError));
    return result;
  }

  QTemporaryDir temporaryDirectory(
      destinationInfo.absoluteDir().filePath(".pdf-grayscale-XXXXXX"));
  if (!temporaryDirectory.isValid()) {
    result.error = QString("Cannot create temporary output in the destination "
                           "directory: %1")
                       .arg(temporaryDirectory.errorString());
    return result;
  }
  const QString candidatePath = temporaryDirectory.filePath("candidate.pdf");
  const QString executable = pdfutil::findGhostscriptExecutable();

  bool converted = false;
  if (!executable.isEmpty()) {
    converted = CompressController::compressPdfWithGhostscript(
        executable, sourceInfo.absoluteFilePath(), candidatePath, quality,
        result.error, true, &m_isCancelled);
  } else {
    converted = convertPdfToGrayscaleWithQtPdf(sourceDocument, candidatePath,
                                               quality, result.error);
  }

  if (!converted) {
    if (m_isCancelled) {
      result.status = Status::Cancelled;
    }
    return result;
  }

  QPdfDocument candidateDocument;
  const auto candidateError = candidateDocument.load(candidatePath);
  if (candidateError != QPdfDocument::Error::None ||
      candidateDocument.pageCount() != sourceDocument.pageCount()) {
    result.error = "The grayscale PDF failed validation (unreadable output "
                   "or changed page count). No destination was replaced.";
    return result;
  }
  for (int page = 0; page < sourceDocument.pageCount(); ++page) {
    if (m_isCancelled) {
      result.status = Status::Cancelled;
      return result;
    }
    const QSizeF before = sourceDocument.pagePointSize(page);
    const QSizeF after = candidateDocument.pagePointSize(page);
    if (qAbs(before.width() - after.width()) > 0.5 ||
        qAbs(before.height() - after.height()) > 0.5) {
      result.error = "The grayscale PDF changed page dimensions. "
                     "No destination was replaced.";
      return result;
    }
  }
  candidateDocument.close();
  sourceDocument.close();

  if (m_isCancelled) {
    result.status = Status::Cancelled;
    return result;
  }
  const QFileInfo currentSource(sourceInfo.absoluteFilePath());
  if (currentSource.size() != result.originalBytes ||
      currentSource.lastModified() != sourceModified) {
    result.error = "The input PDF changed during conversion. Please retry.";
    return result;
  }
  const qint64 candidateBytes = QFileInfo(candidatePath).size();
  if (candidateBytes <= 0) {
    result.error = "The grayscale PDF is empty. No destination was replaced.";
    return result;
  }
  if (pdfutil::isSamePdf(currentSource, QFileInfo(destinationInfo.absoluteFilePath()))) {
    result.error = "The destination now points to the input PDF. "
                   "No destination was replaced.";
    return result;
  }
  if (!pdfutil::savePdfAtomically(candidatePath, destinationInfo.absoluteFilePath(),
                                  m_isCancelled, result.error)) {
    if (m_isCancelled) {
      result.status = Status::Cancelled;
    }
    return result;
  }
  result.status = Status::Compressed;
  result.outputBytes = candidateBytes;
  return result;
}

void GrayscaleController::runJob(const Params &params) {
  Result result;
  result.isBatch = params.isBatch;

  if (!params.isBatch) {
    const QString &sourcePath = params.sourcePath;
    const QString &outputPath = params.outputPath;
    emit progressChanged(0, 1, QString("Converting %1 to grayscale (1 of 1)...")
                                   .arg(QFileInfo(sourcePath).fileName()));
    emit fileStatusChanged(sourcePath, static_cast<int>(FileStatus::Processing),
                           "Converting PDF to grayscale");

    const CompressController::SingleResult single =
        convertPdfToGrayscale(sourcePath, outputPath, params.quality);
    result.origBytes = single.originalBytes;
    result.outBytes = single.outputBytes;
    result.singleOutputPath = outputPath;

    using Status = CompressController::Result::Status;
    int statusInt = static_cast<int>(FileStatus::Failed);
    QString detail = single.error;
    switch (single.status) {
    case Status::Compressed:
      statusInt = static_cast<int>(FileStatus::Succeeded);
      detail = QString("Saved grayscale PDF to %1").arg(outputPath);
      result.success = true;
      result.successCount = 1;
      break;
    case Status::Cancelled:
      statusInt = static_cast<int>(FileStatus::Cancelled);
      detail = "Conversion cancelled; no output saved";
      result.cancelled = true;
      break;
    case Status::Unchanged:
    case Status::Failed:
      result.failCount = 1;
      result.error = single.error;
      result.errors.append(single.error);
      break;
    }
    emit fileStatusChanged(sourcePath, statusInt, detail);
    if (single.status != Status::Cancelled) {
      emit progressChanged(1, 1, "Completed");
    }
    emit finished(result);
    return;
  }

  // Batch grayscale
  const QStringList &sourcePaths = params.sourcePaths;
  const int totalFiles = sourcePaths.size();
  emit progressChanged(0, totalFiles,
                       QString("Converting 0 of %1 PDFs to grayscale...")
                           .arg(totalFiles));

  QSet<QString> usedBaseNames;

  for (int i = 0; i < totalFiles; ++i) {
    if (m_isCancelled) {
      break;
    }

    const QString &sourcePath = sourcePaths.at(i);
    emit fileStatusChanged(sourcePath, static_cast<int>(FileStatus::Processing),
                           "Converting PDF to grayscale");
    emit progressChanged(
        i, totalFiles,
        QString("Converting %1 (%2 of %3)...")
            .arg(QFileInfo(sourcePath).fileName())
            .arg(i + 1)
            .arg(totalFiles));

    auto collidesWithAnySource = [&](const QString &candidateOut) {
      const QFileInfo candInfo(candidateOut);
      for (const QString &src : sourcePaths) {
        if (pdfutil::isSamePdf(QFileInfo(src), candInfo)) {
          return true;
        }
      }
      return false;
    };

    QFileInfo fileInfo(sourcePath);
    QString baseName = fileInfo.baseName() + "_grayscale";
    QString uniqueBaseName = baseName;
    int suffix = 1;
    while (usedBaseNames.contains(uniqueBaseName.toLower()) ||
           QFile::exists(QDir(params.outputDir).filePath(uniqueBaseName + ".pdf")) ||
           collidesWithAnySource(QDir(params.outputDir).filePath(uniqueBaseName + ".pdf"))) {
      uniqueBaseName = QString("%1_%2").arg(baseName).arg(suffix++);
    }
    usedBaseNames.insert(uniqueBaseName.toLower());

    const QString outputPath =
        QDir(params.outputDir).filePath(uniqueBaseName + ".pdf");

    const CompressController::SingleResult single =
        convertPdfToGrayscale(sourcePath, outputPath, params.quality);

    using Status = CompressController::Result::Status;
    int statusInt = static_cast<int>(FileStatus::Failed);
    QString detail = single.error;
    if (single.status == Status::Compressed) {
      statusInt = static_cast<int>(FileStatus::Succeeded);
      detail = QString("Saved to %1").arg(outputPath);
    } else if (single.status == Status::Cancelled) {
      statusInt = static_cast<int>(FileStatus::Cancelled);
      detail = "Conversion cancelled; no output saved";
    }
    emit fileStatusChanged(sourcePath, statusInt, detail);

    if (single.status != Status::Cancelled) {
      emit progressChanged(i + 1, totalFiles,
                           QString("Processed %1 of %2 PDFs")
                               .arg(i + 1)
                               .arg(totalFiles));
    }

    if (single.status == Status::Cancelled) {
      break;
    }
    if (single.status == Status::Compressed) {
      result.successCount++;
      result.origBytes += single.originalBytes;
      result.outBytes += single.outputBytes;
    } else {
      result.failCount++;
      if (result.errors.size() < 3) {
        result.errors.append(
            QString("%1: %2").arg(fileInfo.fileName(), single.error));
      }
    }
  }

  result.cancelled = m_isCancelled.load();
  result.success = (result.successCount > 0 && result.failCount == 0);
  if (!result.errors.isEmpty()) {
    result.error = result.errors.join("\n");
  }
  emit finished(result);
}
