================================================================================
FILE: controllers/pdf/images_to_pdf_controller.cpp
================================================================================
#include "controllers/pdf/images_to_pdf_controller.h"
#include "controllers/pdf/pdf_utils.h"

#include <QDir>
#include <QFile>
#include <QFileInfo>
#include <QImage>
#include <QPageLayout>
#include <QPageSize>
#include <QPainter>
#include <QPdfDocument>
#include <QPdfWriter>
#include <QTemporaryDir>
#include <QtConcurrent/QtConcurrent>
#include <memory>

ImagesToPdfController::ImagesToPdfController(QObject *parent)
    : PdfToolController(parent) {
  qRegisterMetaType<ImagesToPdfController::Result>();
}

void ImagesToPdfController::start(const Params &params) {
  waitForRunningJob();
  m_isCancelled = false;
  QFuture<void> future = QtConcurrent::run([this, params]() {
    runJob(params);
  });
  m_watcher.setFuture(future);
}

void ImagesToPdfController::runJob(const Params &params) {
  Result result;
  result.outputPath = params.outputPath;
  result.pageCount = params.imagePaths.size();

  const QStringList &orderedImages = params.imagePaths;
  const QVector<qreal> marginPts = {0.0, 18.0, 36.0, 54.0};
  const qreal marginPt = marginPts.value(params.marginMode, 0.0);

  emit progressChanged(
      0, orderedImages.size(),
      QString("Combining %1 image(s) into PDF...").arg(orderedImages.size()));

  for (const QString &imgPath : orderedImages) {
    emit fileStatusChanged(imgPath, static_cast<int>(FileStatus::Processing),
                           "Adding image to PDF");
  }

  QString error;
  bool succeeded = false;
  qint64 outputBytes = 0;
  const QFileInfo destinationInfo(params.outputPath);

  QTemporaryDir tempDir(
      destinationInfo.absoluteDir().filePath(".img-to-pdf-XXXXXX"));
  if (!tempDir.isValid()) {
    error = tempDir.errorString();
  } else {
    const QString candidatePath = tempDir.filePath("candidate.pdf");
    QPdfWriter writer(candidatePath);
    writer.setResolution(300);
    std::unique_ptr<QPainter> painter;

    for (int i = 0; i < orderedImages.size(); ++i) {
      if (m_isCancelled) {
        break;
      }
      const QString &imgPath = orderedImages.at(i);
      QImage rawImg(imgPath);
      if (rawImg.isNull()) {
        error = QString("Cannot load image '%1'.")
                    .arg(QFileInfo(imgPath).fileName());
        break;
      }

      // Composite onto opaque white so alpha channels render cleanly on white paper
      QImage img(rawImg.size(), QImage::Format_RGB32);
      img.fill(Qt::white);
      {
        QPainter imgPainter(&img);
        imgPainter.drawImage(0, 0, rawImg);
      }

      if (params.pageSizeMode == 0) {
        // Fit to Image
        double dpiX = rawImg.dotsPerMeterX() > 0
                          ? rawImg.dotsPerMeterX() * 0.0254
                          : 96.0;
        double dpiY = rawImg.dotsPerMeterY() > 0
                          ? rawImg.dotsPerMeterY() * 0.0254
                          : 96.0;
        if (dpiX < 36.0 || dpiX > 1200.0)
          dpiX = 96.0;
        if (dpiY < 36.0 || dpiY > 1200.0)
          dpiY = 96.0;
        const qreal wPt =
            qMax(72.0, img.width() * 72.0 / dpiX) + 2.0 * marginPt;
        const qreal hPt =
            qMax(72.0, img.height() * 72.0 / dpiY) + 2.0 * marginPt;
        QPageLayout layout(QPageSize(QSizeF(wPt, hPt), QPageSize::Point),
                           QPageLayout::Portrait, QMarginsF(0, 0, 0, 0),
                           QPageLayout::Point);
        writer.setPageLayout(layout);
      } else {
        const QPageSize pageSize(params.pageSizeMode == 1 ? QPageSize::A4
                                                         : QPageSize::Letter);
        QPageLayout::Orientation orient = QPageLayout::Portrait;
        if (params.orientationMode == 0) {
          orient = (img.width() > img.height()) ? QPageLayout::Landscape
                                                : QPageLayout::Portrait;
        } else if (params.orientationMode == 2) {
          orient = QPageLayout::Landscape;
        }
        QPageLayout layout(pageSize, orient, QMarginsF(0, 0, 0, 0),
                           QPageLayout::Point);
        writer.setPageLayout(layout);
      }

      if (!painter) {
        painter = std::make_unique<QPainter>(&writer);
        if (!painter->isActive()) {
          error = "Failed to initialize PDF writer.";
          break;
        }
      } else {
        writer.newPage();
      }

      const qreal devW = painter->device()->width();
      const qreal devH = painter->device()->height();
      painter->fillRect(QRectF(0, 0, devW, devH), Qt::white);

      const qreal marginPx = marginPt * 300.0 / 72.0;
      const QRectF availRect(marginPx, marginPx,
                             qMax(1.0, devW - 2.0 * marginPx),
                             qMax(1.0, devH - 2.0 * marginPx));
      const qreal scale = qMin(availRect.width() / img.width(),
                               availRect.height() / img.height());
      const qreal drawW = img.width() * scale;
      const qreal drawH = img.height() * scale;
      const QRectF targetRect(
          availRect.left() + (availRect.width() - drawW) / 2.0,
          availRect.top() + (availRect.height() - drawH) / 2.0, drawW, drawH);

      painter->setRenderHint(QPainter::SmoothPixmapTransform, true);
      painter->drawImage(targetRect, img);

      if (!m_isCancelled) {
        emit progressChanged(
            i + 1, orderedImages.size(),
            QString("Added %1 of %2 image(s)...").arg(i + 1).arg(orderedImages.size()));
      }
    }

    if (painter) {
      painter->end();
    }

    if (error.isEmpty() && !m_isCancelled) {
      QPdfDocument verifyDoc;
      if (verifyDoc.load(candidatePath) != QPdfDocument::Error::None ||
          verifyDoc.pageCount() != orderedImages.size()) {
        error = "Generated PDF failed validation.";
      } else {
        verifyDoc.close();
        if (pdfutil::savePdfAtomically(candidatePath,
                                       destinationInfo.absoluteFilePath(),
                                       m_isCancelled, error)) {
          succeeded = true;
          outputBytes = QFileInfo(destinationInfo.absoluteFilePath()).size();
        }
      }
    }
  }

  for (const QString &imgPath : orderedImages) {
    if (m_isCancelled) {
      emit fileStatusChanged(imgPath, static_cast<int>(FileStatus::Cancelled),
                             "Creation cancelled");
    } else if (succeeded) {
      emit fileStatusChanged(imgPath, static_cast<int>(FileStatus::Succeeded),
                             QString("Added to %1").arg(params.outputPath));
    } else {
      emit fileStatusChanged(imgPath, static_cast<int>(FileStatus::Failed),
                             error);
    }
  }

  result.success = succeeded;
  result.cancelled = m_isCancelled.load();
  result.outputBytes = outputBytes;
  result.error = error;

  emit finished(result);
}

  };

  struct Result {

    bool cancelled{false};
    QString outputPath;
    int pageCount{0};
    qint64 outputBytes{0};

  ~ImagesToPdfController() override { cancelAndWait(); }

  void start(const Params &params);

signals:
  void finished(const ImagesToPdfController::Result &result);

private:
  void runJob(const Params &params);
};

Q_DECLARE_METATYPE(ImagesToPdfController::Result)

#endif // IMAGES_TO_PDF_CONTROLLER_H

================================================================================
FILE: controllers/pdf/markdown_pdf_controller.cpp
================================================================================
#include "controllers/pdf/markdown_pdf_controller.h"
#include "controllers/pdf/pdf_utils.h"

#include <QCoreApplication>
#include <QDateTime>
#include <QDir>
#include <QFile>
#include <QFileInfo>
#include <QFont>
#include <QMarginsF>
#include <QPageLayout>
#include <QPageSize>
#include <QPdfDocument>
#include <QPdfWriter>
#include <QSet>
#include <QTextDocument>
#include <QUrl>
#include <QtConcurrent/QtConcurrent>

MarkdownPdfController::MarkdownPdfController(QObject *parent)
    : PdfToolController(parent) {
  qRegisterMetaType<MarkdownPdfController::Result>();
}

void MarkdownPdfController::start(const Params &params) {
  waitForRunningJob();
  m_isCancelled = false;
  QFuture<void> future = QtConcurrent::run([this, params]() {
    runJob(params);
  });
  m_watcher.setFuture(future);
}

void MarkdownPdfController::runJob(const Params &params) {
  Result result;
  result.outputDir = params.outputDir;

  const QStringList &sourcePaths = params.sourcePaths;
  const int totalFiles = sourcePaths.size();
  const int clampedMarginPt = qBound(0, params.marginPt, 144);
  const int clampedFontPt = qBound(6, params.baseFontPt, 36);

  emit progressChanged(0, totalFiles, "Rendering documents to PDF...");

  int pdfsCreated = 0;
  int failedFiles = 0;
  QStringList errors;
  QSet<QString> usedFileNames;

  const QString defaultCss = QStringLiteral(
      "body { color: #111827; line-height: 1.5; }"
      "h1, h2, h3, h4, h5, h6 { color: #09090b; font-weight: 600; margin-top: 14pt; margin-bottom: 6pt; }"
      "p { margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 8pt; }"
      "pre, code { font-family: 'JetBrains Mono', 'Fira Code', 'Consolas', monospace; background-color: #f4f4f5; }"
      "pre { padding: 8pt; border: 1px solid #e4e4e7; margin-top: 6pt; margin-bottom: 8pt; }"
      "blockquote { color: #52525b; border-left: 3px solid #d4d4d8; margin-left: 8pt; padding-left: 8pt; }"
      "table { border-collapse: collapse; margin-top: 6pt; margin-bottom: 8pt; }"
      "th, td { border: 1px solid #d4d4d8; padding: 4pt 8pt; }"
      "th { background-color: #f4f4f5; font-weight: 600; }"
      "a { color: #2563eb; text-decoration: underline; }");

  for (int i = 0; i < totalFiles; ++i) {
    if (m_isCancelled) {
      for (int rem = i; rem < totalFiles; ++rem) {
        emit fileStatusChanged(sourcePaths.at(rem),
                               static_cast<int>(FileStatus::Cancelled),
                               "PDF export cancelled");
      }
      break;
    }

    const QString &sourcePath = sourcePaths.at(i);
    const QFileInfo sourceInfo(sourcePath);

    emit fileStatusChanged(sourcePath, static_cast<int>(FileStatus::Processing),
                           "Typesetting document to PDF");
    emit progressChanged(
        i, totalFiles,
        QString("Exporting %1 (%2 of %3)...")
            .arg(sourceInfo.fileName())
            .arg(i + 1)
            .arg(totalFiles));

    QFile inFile(sourceInfo.absoluteFilePath());
    if (!inFile.open(QIODevice::ReadOnly)) {
      failedFiles++;
      const QString err =
          QString("Cannot open file: %1").arg(inFile.errorString());
      errors.append(QString("%1: %2").arg(sourceInfo.fileName(), err));
      emit fileStatusChanged(sourcePath, static_cast<int>(FileStatus::Failed),
                             err);
      continue;
    }
    const QByteArray rawBytes = inFile.readAll();
    inFile.close();

    const QString content = QString::fromUtf8(rawBytes);

    const QString baseStem = sourceInfo.completeBaseName().isEmpty()
                                 ? QStringLiteral("document")
                                 : sourceInfo.completeBaseName();
    QString uniqueStem = baseStem;
    int suffix = 1;
    while (true) {
      const QString candidateName = uniqueStem + ".pdf";
      const QString candidatePath =
          QDir(params.outputDir).filePath(candidateName);
      const QFileInfo candidateInfo(candidatePath);
      const bool collidesWithSource =
          pdfutil::isSamePdf(sourceInfo, candidateInfo);
      if (!usedFileNames.contains(candidateName.toLower()) &&
          !QFile::exists(candidatePath) && !collidesWithSource) {
        break;
      }
      uniqueStem = QString("%1_%2").arg(baseStem).arg(suffix++);
    }
    const QString outFileName = uniqueStem + ".pdf";
    usedFileNames.insert(outFileName.toLower());
    const QString outPdfPath = QDir(params.outputDir).filePath(outFileName);

    const QString tempPath =
        outPdfPath + QString(".md_tmp_%1_%2")
                         .arg(QCoreApplication::applicationPid())
                         .arg(QDateTime::currentMSecsSinceEpoch());

    QString fileError;
    {
      QTextDocument doc;
      doc.setBaseUrl(QUrl::fromLocalFile(sourceInfo.absolutePath() + "/"));
      QFont font("Inter", clampedFontPt);
      doc.setDefaultFont(font);
      doc.setDocumentMargin(0.0);
      doc.setDefaultStyleSheet(defaultCss);

      const QString ext = sourceInfo.suffix().toLower();
      if (ext == "html" || ext == "htm") {
        doc.setHtml(content);
      } else {
        doc.setMarkdown(content);
      }

      QPdfWriter writer(tempPath);
      writer.setResolution(300);
      writer.setTitle(sourceInfo.completeBaseName());
      writer.setPageSize(
          QPageSize(params.useLetterPageSize ? QPageSize::Letter : QPageSize::A4));
      writer.setPageOrientation(params.landscape ? QPageLayout::Landscape
                                                 : QPageLayout::Portrait);
      writer.setPageMargins(QMarginsF(clampedMarginPt, clampedMarginPt,
                                      clampedMarginPt, clampedMarginPt),
                            QPageLayout::Point);

      doc.print(&writer);
    }

    if (!m_isCancelled) {
      QPdfDocument verifyDoc;
      if (verifyDoc.load(tempPath) != QPdfDocument::Error::None ||
          verifyDoc.pageCount() <= 0) {
        fileError = "Generated PDF failed validation.";
      } else {
        verifyDoc.close();
        if (!pdfutil::savePdfAtomically(tempPath, outPdfPath, m_isCancelled,
                                        fileError)) {
          if (fileError.isEmpty() && !m_isCancelled) {
            fileError = "Failed to save PDF.";
          }
        }
      }
    }
    QFile::remove(tempPath);

    if (m_isCancelled) {
      emit fileStatusChanged(sourcePath, static_cast<int>(FileStatus::Cancelled),
                             "PDF export cancelled");
      for (int rem = i + 1; rem < totalFiles; ++rem) {
        emit fileStatusChanged(sourcePaths.at(rem),
                               static_cast<int>(FileStatus::Cancelled),
                               "PDF export cancelled");
      }
      break;
    }

    if (fileError.isEmpty()) {
      pdfsCreated++;
      emit fileStatusChanged(sourcePath, static_cast<int>(FileStatus::Succeeded),
                             QString("Saved %1").arg(outFileName));
    } else {
      failedFiles++;
      errors.append(QString("%1: %2").arg(sourceInfo.fileName(), fileError));
      emit fileStatusChanged(sourcePath, static_cast<int>(FileStatus::Failed),
                             fileError);
    }

    emit progressChanged(
        i + 1, totalFiles,
        QString("Exported %1 of %2 document(s)").arg(i + 1).arg(totalFiles));
  }

  result.success = (pdfsCreated > 0 && failedFiles == 0 && !m_isCancelled);
  result.cancelled = m_isCancelled.load();
  result.pdfsCreated = pdfsCreated;
  result.failedFiles = failedFiles;
  result.errors = errors;
  if (!errors.isEmpty()) {
    result.error = errors.join("\n");
  }

  emit finished(result);
}

================================================================================
FILE: controllers/pdf/markdown_pdf_controller.h

#ifndef MARKDOWN_PDF_CONTROLLER_H
#define MARKDOWN_PDF_CONTROLLER_H

#include "controllers/pdf/pdf_tool_controller.h"
#include <QString>

  Q_OBJECT

public:
  struct Params {
    QStringList sourcePaths;
    QString outputDir;
    bool useLetterPageSize{false}; // false = A4, true = Letter
    bool landscape{false};
    int marginPt{36};    // 18, 36, 54, 72 pt
    int baseFontPt{11};  // 9..16 pt
  };

  struct Result {
    bool success{false};
    bool cancelled{false};
    int pdfsCreated{0};
    int failedFiles{0};
    QString outputDir;
    QString error;
    QStringList errors;
  };

  explicit MarkdownPdfController(QObject *parent = nullptr);
  ~MarkdownPdfController() override { cancelAndWait(); }

  void start(const Params &params);

signals:
  void finished(const MarkdownPdfController::Result &result);

private:
  void runJob(const Params &params);
};

Q_DECLARE_METATYPE(MarkdownPdfController::Result)

#endif // MARKDOWN_PDF_CONTROLLER_H

================================================================================
FILE: controllers/pdf/merge_controller.cpp
================================================================================
#include "controllers/pdf/merge_controller.h"
#include "controllers/pdf/pdf_utils.h"

#include <QDir>
#include <QFile>
#include <QFileInfo>
#include <QImage>
#include <QPageLayout>
#include <QPageSize>
#include <QPainter>
#include <QPdfDocument>
#include <QPdfWriter>
#include <QTemporaryDir>
#include <QtConcurrent/QtConcurrent>
#include <memory>

MergeController::MergeController(QObject *parent)
    : PdfToolController(parent) {
  qRegisterMetaType<MergeController::Result>();
}

void MergeController::start(const Params &params) {
  waitForRunningJob();
  m_isCancelled = false;
  QFuture<void> future = QtConcurrent::run([this, params]() {
    runJob(params);
  });
  m_watcher.setFuture(future);
}

bool MergeController::mergePdfsWithGhostscript(const QString &executable,
                                               const QStringList &inputPaths,
                                               const QString &tempOutputPath,
                                               QString &error) {
  QStringList args = {"-sDEVICE=pdfwrite",
                      "-dCompatibilityLevel=1.7",
                      "-dSAFER",
                      "-dPDFSTOPONERROR",
                      "-dNOPAUSE",
                      "-dQUIET",
                      "-dBATCH",
                      "-dAutoRotatePages=/None",
                      "-dCompressFonts=true",
                      "-dCompressStreams=true",
                      "-dSubsetFonts=true",
                      "-dPreserveAnnots=true",
                      "-sOutputFile=candidate.pdf",
                      "-f"};
  for (const QString &path : inputPaths) {
    args << QDir::toNativeSeparators(path);
  }
  return pdfutil::runGhostscriptProcess(
      executable, args, QFileInfo(tempOutputPath).absolutePath(),
      tempOutputPath, m_isCancelled, error, false);
}

bool MergeController::mergePdfsWithQtPdf(const QStringList &inputPaths,
                                         const QString &tempOutputPath,
                                         QString &error) {
  QPdfWriter writer(tempOutputPath);
  writer.setResolution(300);
  std::unique_ptr<QPainter> painter;

  for (const QString &inputPath : inputPaths) {
    if (m_isCancelled) {
      return false;
    }
    QPdfDocument doc;
    const auto loadErr = doc.load(inputPath);
    if (loadErr != QPdfDocument::Error::None || doc.pageCount() <= 0) {
      error = QString("Cannot open '%1' for merging.")
                  .arg(QFileInfo(inputPath).fileName());
      return false;
    }

    for (int page = 0; page < doc.pageCount(); ++page) {
      if (m_isCancelled) {
        return false;
      }
      const QSizeF ptSize = doc.pagePointSize(page);
      QPageLayout layout(QPageSize(ptSize, QPageSize::Point),
                         QPageLayout::Portrait, QMarginsF(0, 0, 0, 0),
                         QPageLayout::Point);
      writer.setPageLayout(layout);
      if (!painter) {
        painter = std::make_unique<QPainter>(&writer);
        if (!painter->isActive()) {
          error = "Failed to initialize PDF writer.";
          return false;
        }
      } else {
        writer.newPage();
      }

      const QSize renderSize(qMax(1, qRound(ptSize.width() * 300.0 / 72.0)),
                             qMax(1, qRound(ptSize.height() * 300.0 / 72.0)));
      const QImage pageImg = pdfutil::renderPdfPageOnWhite(doc, page, renderSize);
      if (pageImg.isNull()) {
        error = QString("Failed to render page %1 of '%2'.")
                    .arg(page + 1)
                    .arg(QFileInfo(inputPath).fileName());
        return false;
      }
      painter->setRenderHint(QPainter::SmoothPixmapTransform, true);
      painter->drawImage(
          QRectF(0, 0, painter->device()->width(), painter->device()->height()),
          pageImg);
    }
  }

  if (painter) {
    painter->end();
  }
  return QFileInfo(tempOutputPath).size() > 0;
}

void MergeController::runJob(const Params &params) {
  Result result;
  result.outputPath = params.outputPath;

  const QStringList &orderedPaths = params.inputPaths;
  emit progressChanged(0, orderedPaths.size(),
                       QString("Merging %1 PDF files...").arg(orderedPaths.size()));

  for (const QString &srcPath : orderedPaths) {
    emit fileStatusChanged(srcPath, static_cast<int>(FileStatus::Processing),
                           "Merging into combined PDF");
  }

  QString error;
  int expectedTotalPages = 0;
  for (const QString &srcPath : orderedPaths) {
    if (m_isCancelled) {
      break;
    }
    const QFileInfo srcInfo(srcPath);
    if (!srcInfo.isFile() || srcInfo.size() <= 0) {
      error = QString("Input file '%1' is missing or empty.")
                  .arg(srcInfo.fileName());
      break;
    }
    QPdfDocument doc;
    const auto err = doc.load(srcInfo.absoluteFilePath());
    if (err != QPdfDocument::Error::None || doc.pageCount() <= 0) {
      error = QString("Cannot open '%1' (damaged or password-protected).")
                  .arg(srcInfo.fileName());
      break;
    }
    expectedTotalPages += doc.pageCount();
  }

  bool succeeded = false;
  qint64 outputBytes = 0;
  const QFileInfo destinationInfo(params.outputPath);

  if (error.isEmpty() && !m_isCancelled) {
    QTemporaryDir tempDir(
        destinationInfo.absoluteDir().filePath(".pdf-merge-XXXXXX"));
    if (!tempDir.isValid()) {
      error = QString("Cannot create temporary directory: %1")
                  .arg(tempDir.errorString());
    } else {
      const QString candidatePath = tempDir.filePath("candidate.pdf");
      const QString gsExec = pdfutil::findGhostscriptExecutable();
      bool generated = false;
      if (!gsExec.isEmpty()) {
        QString gsErr;
        generated = mergePdfsWithGhostscript(gsExec, orderedPaths,
                                             candidatePath, gsErr);
        if (generated) {
          QPdfDocument checkDoc;
          if (checkDoc.load(candidatePath) != QPdfDocument::Error::None ||
              checkDoc.pageCount() != expectedTotalPages) {
            generated = false;
            QFile::remove(candidatePath);
          }
        }
      }
      if (!generated && !m_isCancelled) {
        generated = mergePdfsWithQtPdf(orderedPaths, candidatePath, error);
      }

      if (generated && !m_isCancelled) {
        QPdfDocument candidateDoc;
        if (candidateDoc.load(candidatePath) != QPdfDocument::Error::None ||
            candidateDoc.pageCount() != expectedTotalPages) {
          error = "Merged PDF validation failed (unexpected page count).";
        } else {
          candidateDoc.close();
          if (pdfutil::savePdfAtomically(candidatePath,
                                         destinationInfo.absoluteFilePath(),
                                         m_isCancelled, error)) {
            succeeded = true;
            outputBytes = QFileInfo(destinationInfo.absoluteFilePath()).size();
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }

  for (const QString &srcPath : orderedPaths) {
    if (m_isCancelled) {
      emit fileStatusChanged(srcPath, static_cast<int>(FileStatus::Cancelled),
                             "Merge cancelled");
    } else if (succeeded) {
      emit fileStatusChanged(srcPath, static_cast<int>(FileStatus::Succeeded),
                             QString("Merged into %1").arg(params.outputPath));
    } else {
      emit fileStatusChanged(srcPath, static_cast<int>(FileStatus::Failed),
                             error);
    }
  }

  if (!m_isCancelled && succeeded) {
    emit progressChanged(orderedPaths.size(), orderedPaths.size(), "Completed");
  }

  result.success = succeeded;
  result.cancelled = m_isCancelled.load();
  result.totalPages = expectedTotalPages;
  result.outputBytes = outputBytes;
  result.error = error;

  emit finished(result);
}

                                const QStringList &inputPaths,
                                const QString &tempOutputPath,

                          const QString &tempOutputPath,
                          QString &error);

================================================================================
FILE: controllers/pdf/organize_controller.cpp
================================================================================
#include "controllers/pdf/organize_controller.h"
#include "controllers/pdf/pdf_utils.h"

#include <QDir>
#include <QFile>
#include <QFileInfo>
#include <QImage>
#include <QPageLayout>
#include <QPageSize>
#include <QPainter>
#include <QPdfDocument>
#include <QPdfWriter>
#include <QTemporaryDir>
#include <QTransform>
#include <QtConcurrent/QtConcurrent>
#include <memory>

OrganizeController::OrganizeController(QObject *parent)
    : PdfToolController(parent) {
  qRegisterMetaType<OrganizeController::PageOp>();
  qRegisterMetaType<OrganizeController::Result>();
}

void OrganizeController::start(const Params &params) {
  waitForRunningJob();
  m_isCancelled = false;
  QFuture<void> future = QtConcurrent::run([this, params]() {
    runJob(params);
  });
  m_watcher.setFuture(future);
}

void OrganizeController::runJob(const Params &params) {
  Result result;
  result.outputPath = params.outputPath;
  result.pageCount = params.pageOps.size();

  emit progressChanged(0, params.pageOps.size(),
                       QString("Saving %1 organized page(s)...").arg(params.pageOps.size()));
  emit fileStatusChanged(params.sourcePath,
                         static_cast<int>(FileStatus::Processing),
                         "Writing organized PDF");

  QString error;
  bool succeeded = false;
  qint64 outputBytes = 0;
  const QFileInfo destinationInfo(params.outputPath);

  QPdfDocument sourceDoc;
  const auto loadErr = sourceDoc.load(params.sourcePath);
  if (loadErr != QPdfDocument::Error::None || sourceDoc.pageCount() <= 0) {
    error = "Cannot open source PDF.";
  } else {
    QTemporaryDir tempDir(
        destinationInfo.absoluteDir().filePath(".pdf-organize-XXXXXX"));
    if (!tempDir.isValid()) {
      error = tempDir.errorString();
    } else {
      const QString candidatePath = tempDir.filePath("candidate.pdf");
      QPdfWriter writer(candidatePath);
      writer.setResolution(300);
      std::unique_ptr<QPainter> painter;

      for (int i = 0; i < params.pageOps.size(); ++i) {
        if (m_isCancelled) {
          break;
        }
        const PageOp &op = params.pageOps.at(i);
        if (op.originalPageIndex < 0 ||
            op.originalPageIndex >= sourceDoc.pageCount()) {
          error = "Page index out of bounds.";
          break;
        }

        const QSizeF origPtSize =
            sourceDoc.pagePointSize(op.originalPageIndex);
        const bool swapDims =
            (op.rotationDegrees == 90 || op.rotationDegrees == 270);
        const QSizeF effectivePtSize =
            swapDims ? QSizeF(origPtSize.height(), origPtSize.width())
                     : origPtSize;

        QPageLayout layout(QPageSize(effectivePtSize, QPageSize::Point),
                           QPageLayout::Portrait, QMarginsF(0, 0, 0, 0),
                           QPageLayout::Point);
        writer.setPageLayout(layout);

        if (!painter) {
          painter = std::make_unique<QPainter>(&writer);
          if (!painter->isActive()) {
            error = "Failed to initialize PDF writer.";
            break;
          }
        } else {
          writer.newPage();
        }

        const QSize renderSize(
            qMax(1, qRound(origPtSize.width() * 300.0 / 72.0)),
            qMax(1, qRound(origPtSize.height() * 300.0 / 72.0)));
        QImage pageImg =
            pdfutil::renderPdfPageOnWhite(sourceDoc, op.originalPageIndex, renderSize);
        if (pageImg.isNull()) {
          error = QString("Failed to render page %1.")
                      .arg(op.originalPageIndex + 1);
          break;
        }
        if (op.rotationDegrees != 0) {
          pageImg = pageImg.transformed(QTransform().rotate(op.rotationDegrees),
                                        Qt::SmoothTransformation);
        }

        painter->setRenderHint(QPainter::SmoothPixmapTransform, true);
        painter->drawImage(QRectF(0, 0, painter->device()->width(),
                                  painter->device()->height()),
                           pageImg);

        if (!m_isCancelled) {
          emit progressChanged(
              i + 1, params.pageOps.size(),
              QString("Rendered page %1 of %2...").arg(i + 1).arg(params.pageOps.size()));
        }
      }

      if (painter) {
        painter->end();
      }

      if (error.isEmpty() && !m_isCancelled) {
        QPdfDocument verifyDoc;
        if (verifyDoc.load(candidatePath) != QPdfDocument::Error::None ||
            verifyDoc.pageCount() != params.pageOps.size()) {
          error = "Organized PDF failed validation.";
        } else {
          verifyDoc.close();
          if (pdfutil::savePdfAtomically(candidatePath,
                                         destinationInfo.absoluteFilePath(),
                                         m_isCancelled, error)) {
            succeeded = true;
            outputBytes = QFileInfo(destinationInfo.absoluteFilePath()).size();
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }

  sourceDoc.close();

  if (m_isCancelled) {
    emit fileStatusChanged(params.sourcePath,
                           static_cast<int>(FileStatus::Cancelled),
                           "Operation cancelled");
  } else if (succeeded) {
    emit fileStatusChanged(
        params.sourcePath, static_cast<int>(FileStatus::Succeeded),
        QString("Saved organized PDF to %1").arg(params.outputPath));
  } else {
    emit fileStatusChanged(params.sourcePath,
                           static_cast<int>(FileStatus::Failed), error);
  }

  result.success = succeeded;
  result.cancelled = m_isCancelled.load();
  result.outputBytes = outputBytes;
  result.error = error;

  emit finished(result);
}

================================================================================
FILE: controllers/pdf/pdf_to_images_controller.cpp
================================================================================
#include "controllers/pdf/pdf_to_images_controller.h"
#include "controllers/pdf/pdf_utils.h"

#include <QDir>
#include <QFile>
#include <QFileInfo>
#include <QImage>
#include <QPdfDocument>
#include <QSaveFile>
#include <QSet>
#include <QtConcurrent/QtConcurrent>

PdfToImagesController::PdfToImagesController(QObject *parent)
    : PdfToolController(parent) {
  qRegisterMetaType<PdfToImagesController::Result>();
}

void PdfToImagesController::start(const Params &params) {
  waitForRunningJob();
  m_isCancelled = false;
  QFuture<void> future = QtConcurrent::run([this, params]() {
    runJob(params);
  });
  m_watcher.setFuture(future);
}

void PdfToImagesController::runJob(const Params &params) {
  Result result;
  result.outputDir = params.outputDir;
  result.format = params.format;
  result.dpi = params.dpi;

  const QStringList &sourcePaths = params.sourcePaths;
  const QString &format = params.format.toLower();
  const int effectiveQuality = (format == "png") ? -1 : params.quality;
  const int dpi = params.dpi;

  emit progressChanged(0, sourcePaths.size(), "Exporting PDF pages to images...");

  int totalExportedImages = 0;
  int failedDocs = 0;
  QStringList errors;
  QSet<QString> usedFileNames;

  for (int i = 0; i < sourcePaths.size(); ++i) {
    if (m_isCancelled) {
      break;
    }
    const QString &sourcePath = sourcePaths.at(i);
    const QFileInfo sourceInfo(sourcePath);

    emit fileStatusChanged(
        sourcePath, static_cast<int>(FileStatus::Processing),
        QString("Rendering pages to %1 at %2 DPI")
            .arg(format.toUpper())
            .arg(dpi));
    emit progressChanged(
        i, sourcePaths.size(),
        QString("Exporting %1 (%2 of %3)...")
            .arg(sourceInfo.fileName())
            .arg(i + 1)
            .arg(sourcePaths.size()));

    QPdfDocument doc;
    if (doc.load(sourceInfo.absoluteFilePath()) != QPdfDocument::Error::None ||
        doc.pageCount() <= 0) {
      failedDocs++;
      const QString err = "Cannot open PDF (damaged or encrypted).";
      errors.append(QString("%1: %2").arg(sourceInfo.fileName(), err));
      emit fileStatusChanged(sourcePath, static_cast<int>(FileStatus::Failed), err);
      continue;
    }

    QVector<int> targetPages;
    if (params.rangeFilter.isEmpty()) {
      for (int p = 1; p <= doc.pageCount(); ++p) {
        targetPages.append(p);
      }
    } else {
      QVector<QPair<int, int>> ranges;
      QString rangeErr;
      if (!pdfutil::parsePageRanges(params.rangeFilter, doc.pageCount(), ranges, rangeErr)) {
        failedDocs++;
        errors.append(QString("%1: %2").arg(sourceInfo.fileName(), rangeErr));
        emit fileStatusChanged(sourcePath, static_cast<int>(FileStatus::Failed), rangeErr);
        continue;
      }
      for (const auto &r : ranges) {
        for (int p = r.first; p <= r.second; ++p) {
          if (!targetPages.contains(p)) {
            targetPages.append(p);
          }
        }
      }
    }

    int exportedForThisPdf = 0;
    QString docError;
    for (int pageNum : targetPages) {
      if (m_isCancelled) {
        break;
      }
      const int pageIdx = pageNum - 1;
      const QSizeF ptSize = doc.pagePointSize(pageIdx);
      const QSize pixelSize(qMax(1, qRound(ptSize.width() * dpi / 72.0)),
                            qMax(1, qRound(ptSize.height() * dpi / 72.0)));

      const QImage::Format imgFormat =
          (format == "jpg") ? QImage::Format_RGB32 : QImage::Format_ARGB32;
      const QImage pageImg =
          pdfutil::renderPdfPageOnWhite(doc, pageIdx, pixelSize, imgFormat);
      if (pageImg.isNull()) {
        docError = QString("Failed to render page %1.").arg(pageNum);
        break;
      }

      const QString baseStem = QString("%1_page_%2")
                                   .arg(sourceInfo.baseName())
                                   .arg(pageNum, 3, 10, QLatin1Char('0'));
      QString uniqueStem = baseStem;
      int suffix = 1;
      while (usedFileNames.contains(uniqueStem.toLower()) ||
             QFile::exists(
                 QDir(params.outputDir).filePath(uniqueStem + "." + format))) {
        uniqueStem = QString("%1_%2").arg(baseStem).arg(suffix++);
      }
      usedFileNames.insert(uniqueStem.toLower());
      const QString outImagePath =
          QDir(params.outputDir).filePath(uniqueStem + "." + format);

      QSaveFile saveFile(outImagePath);
      saveFile.setDirectWriteFallback(false);
      if (!saveFile.open(QIODevice::WriteOnly) ||
          !pageImg.save(&saveFile, format.toUpper().toUtf8().constData(),
                        effectiveQuality) ||
          !saveFile.commit()) {
        docError = QString("Failed to save image '%1'.")
                       .arg(QFileInfo(outImagePath).fileName());
        break;
      }
      exportedForThisPdf++;
      totalExportedImages++;
    }

    doc.close();

    if (m_isCancelled) {
      emit fileStatusChanged(sourcePath, static_cast<int>(FileStatus::Cancelled),
                             "Export cancelled");
    } else if (docError.isEmpty() && exportedForThisPdf > 0) {
      emit fileStatusChanged(
          sourcePath, static_cast<int>(FileStatus::Succeeded),
          QString("Exported %1 %2 image(s) to %3")
              .arg(exportedForThisPdf)
              .arg(format.toUpper())
              .arg(params.outputDir));
    } else {
      emit fileStatusChanged(sourcePath, static_cast<int>(FileStatus::Failed),
                             docError);
    }

    if (!m_isCancelled) {
      emit progressChanged(
          i + 1, sourcePaths.size(),
          QString("Exported %1 of %2").arg(i + 1).arg(sourcePaths.size()));
    }

    if (!docError.isEmpty() && !m_isCancelled) {
      failedDocs++;
      errors.append(QString("%1: %2").arg(sourceInfo.fileName(), docError));
    }
  }

  result.success = (totalExportedImages > 0 && failedDocs == 0);
  result.cancelled = m_isCancelled.load();
  result.imagesExported = totalExportedImages;
  result.failedDocs = failedDocs;
  result.errors = errors;
  if (!errors.isEmpty()) {
    result.error = errors.join("\n");
  }

  emit finished(result);
}

================================================================================
FILE: controllers/pdf/pdf_tool_controller.cpp
================================================================================
#include "controllers/pdf/pdf_tool_controller.h"

PdfToolController::PdfToolController(QObject *parent)
    : QObject(parent) {}

PdfToolController::~PdfToolController() {
  cancelAndWait();
}

void PdfToolController::cancel() {
  m_isCancelled = true;
}

void PdfToolController::cancelAndWait() {
  cancel();
  waitForRunningJob();
}

bool PdfToolController::isRunning() const {
  return m_watcher.isRunning();
}

bool PdfToolController::isCancelled() const {
  return m_isCancelled.load();
}

void PdfToolController::waitForRunningJob() {
  if (m_watcher.isRunning()) {
    m_watcher.waitForFinished();
  }
}


================================================================================

#ifndef PDF_TOOL_CONTROLLER_H
#define PDF_TOOL_CONTROLLER_H

#include <QFutureWatcher>
#include <QObject>
#include <QString>
#include <atomic>

class PdfToolController : public QObject {
  Q_OBJECT

public:
  enum class FileStatus {
    Waiting = 0,
    Processing,
    Succeeded,
    Unchanged,
    Failed,
    Cancelled
  };
  Q_ENUM(FileStatus)

  explicit PdfToolController(QObject *parent = nullptr);
  ~PdfToolController() override;

  virtual void cancel();
  void cancelAndWait();
  bool isRunning() const;
  bool isCancelled() const;

signals:
  void progressChanged(int current, int total, const QString &statusText);
  void fileStatusChanged(const QString &filePath, int status, const QString &detail);

protected:
  void waitForRunningJob();

  std::atomic<bool> m_isCancelled{false};
  QFutureWatcher<void> m_watcher;
};

#endif // PDF_TOOL_CONTROLLER_H


================================================================================
FILE: controllers/pdf/pdf_utils.cpp
================================================================================
#include "controllers/pdf/pdf_utils.h"

#include <QCoreApplication>
#include <QDebug>
#include <QDir>
#include <QElapsedTimer>
#include <QFile>
#include <QPainter>
#include <QProcess>
#include <QProcessEnvironment>
#include <QSaveFile>
#include <QStandardPaths>

namespace pdfutil {

bool isSamePdf(const QFileInfo &source, const QFileInfo &destination) {
  const QString canonicalSource = source.canonicalFilePath();
  const QString canonicalDestination = destination.canonicalFilePath();
#ifdef Q_OS_WIN
  constexpr Qt::CaseSensitivity pathCase = Qt::CaseInsensitive;
#else
  constexpr Qt::CaseSensitivity pathCase = Qt::CaseSensitive;
#endif
  return source == destination ||
         (!canonicalSource.isEmpty() && !canonicalDestination.isEmpty() &&
          canonicalSource.compare(canonicalDestination, pathCase) == 0);
}

bool savePdfAtomically(const QString &candidatePath, const QString &outputPath,
                       const std::atomic<bool> &cancelled, QString &error) {
  QFile candidate(candidatePath);
  QSaveFile destination(outputPath);
  // Never fall back to truncating an existing destination on a failed save.
  destination.setDirectWriteFallback(false);
  if (!candidate.open(QIODevice::ReadOnly)) {
    error = QString("Cannot read the generated PDF: %1")
                .arg(candidate.errorString());
    return false;
  }
  if (!destination.open(QIODevice::WriteOnly)) {
    error = QString("Cannot save the output PDF: %1")
                .arg(destination.errorString());
    return false;
  }

  while (!candidate.atEnd()) {
    if (cancelled) {
      return false;
    }
    const QByteArray chunk = candidate.read(1024 * 1024);
    if (candidate.error() != QFileDevice::NoError) {
      error = QString("Cannot read the generated PDF: %1")
                  .arg(candidate.errorString());
      return false;
    }
    if (destination.write(chunk) != chunk.size()) {
      error = QString("Cannot save the output PDF: %1")
                  .arg(destination.errorString());
      return false;
    }
  }
  if (cancelled) {
    return false;
  }
  if (!destination.commit()) {
    error = QString("Cannot commit the output PDF: %1")
                .arg(destination.errorString());
    return false;
  }
  return true;
}

QImage renderPdfPageOnWhite(QPdfDocument &doc, int pageIndex,
                            const QSize &pixelSize,
                            QImage::Format format) {
  if (pixelSize.width() <= 0 || pixelSize.height() <= 0) {
    return QImage();
  }
  const QImage rendered = doc.render(pageIndex, pixelSize);
  if (rendered.isNull()) {
    return QImage();
  }
  QImage canvas(pixelSize, format);
  canvas.fill(Qt::white);
  QPainter painter(&canvas);
  painter.setRenderHint(QPainter::SmoothPixmapTransform, true);
  painter.drawImage(0, 0, rendered);
  painter.end();
  return canvas;
}

bool runGhostscriptProcess(const QString &executable, const QStringList &args,
                           const QString &workingDir,
                           const QString &expectedOutputPath,
                           const std::atomic<bool> &cancelled, QString &error,
                           bool failOnWarnings) {
  QProcess proc;
  proc.setWorkingDirectory(workingDir);
  proc.setProcessChannelMode(QProcess::MergedChannels);
  QProcessEnvironment environment = QProcessEnvironment::systemEnvironment();
  environment.remove("GS_OPTIONS");
  proc.setProcessEnvironment(environment);
  proc.start(executable, args);
  if (!proc.waitForStarted(5000)) {
    error = QString("Cannot start Ghostscript: %1. Check the bundled runtime "
                    "and its DLLs/resources, or reinstall Ghostscript.")
                .arg(proc.errorString());
    return false;
  }

  QElapsedTimer timer;
  timer.start();
  QByteArray diagnostics;
  while (proc.state() != QProcess::NotRunning) {
    proc.waitForFinished(150);
    diagnostics = (diagnostics + proc.readAll()).right(16384);
    if (cancelled || timer.elapsed() > 15 * 60 * 1000) {
      proc.kill();
      proc.waitForFinished(3000);
      if (!cancelled) {
        error = "Ghostscript exceeded the 15-minute processing limit.";
      }
      return false;
    }
  }
  diagnostics = (diagnostics + proc.readAll()).right(16384);
  if (cancelled) {
    return false;
  }
  if (proc.exitStatus() != QProcess::NormalExit || proc.exitCode() != 0) {
    error = QString("Ghostscript failed (exit %1): %2")
                .arg(proc.exitCode())
                .arg(QString::fromLocal8Bit(diagnostics).trimmed().right(4096));
    qWarning().noquote() << error;
    return false;
  }
  if (failOnWarnings && !diagnostics.trimmed().isEmpty()) {
    error = QString("Ghostscript reported warnings; the output was not saved "
                    "to avoid accepting missing or substituted content: %1")
                .arg(QString::fromLocal8Bit(diagnostics).trimmed().right(4096));
    qWarning().noquote() << error;
    return false;
  }
  if (QFileInfo(expectedOutputPath).size() <= 0) {
    error = "Ghostscript did not produce a nonempty PDF.";
    return false;
  }
  return true;
}

QString findGhostscriptExecutable() {
  // Single-file packagers can point to their extracted runtime explicitly.
  const QString configured = qEnvironmentVariable("IMAGECONVERTER_GHOSTSCRIPT");
  if (!configured.isEmpty()) {
    const QFileInfo executable(configured);
    return executable.isFile() && executable.isExecutable()
               ? executable.absoluteFilePath()
               : QString();
  }

  const QDir appDir(QCoreApplication::applicationDirPath());
#ifdef Q_OS_WIN
  const QStringList bundledNames = {"gs/bin/gswin64c.exe", "gswin64c.exe"};
  const QStringList systemNames = {"gswin64c", "gswin32c"};
#else
  const QStringList bundledNames = {"gs/bin/gs"};
  const QStringList systemNames = {"gs"};
#endif
  for (const QString &name : bundledNames) {
    const QFileInfo executable(appDir.filePath(name));
    if (executable.isFile() && executable.isExecutable()) {
      return executable.absoluteFilePath();
    }
  }
  for (const QString &name : systemNames) {
    const QString found = QStandardPaths::findExecutable(name);
    if (!found.isEmpty()) {
      return found;
    }
  }
  return QString();
}

bool parsePageRanges(const QString &rangeSpec, int pageCount,
                     QVector<QPair<int, int>> &outRanges,
                     QString &error) {
  outRanges.clear();
  const QString trimmed = rangeSpec.trimmed();
  if (trimmed.isEmpty()) {
    error = "Please enter at least one page or range (e.g. 1-3, 5, 8-12).";
    return false;
  }

  const QStringList tokens = trimmed.split(',', Qt::SkipEmptyParts);
  if (tokens.isEmpty()) {
    error = "Please enter at least one page or range (e.g. 1-3, 5, 8-12).";
    return false;
  }

  for (const QString &rawToken : tokens) {
    const QString token = rawToken.trimmed();
    if (token.isEmpty()) {
      continue;
    }
    if (token.contains('-')) {
      const QStringList parts = token.split('-');
      if (parts.size() != 2) {
        error = QString("Invalid range syntax '%1'.").arg(token);
        return false;
      }
      bool okStart = false;
      bool okEnd = false;
      const int start = parts[0].trimmed().toInt(&okStart);
      const int end = parts[1].trimmed().toInt(&okEnd);
      if (!okStart || !okEnd || start < 1 || end < start || end > pageCount) {
        error = QString("Page range '%1' is invalid (document has %2 page(s)).")
                    .arg(token)
                    .arg(pageCount);
        return false;
      }
      outRanges.append(qMakePair(start, end));
    } else {
      bool okPage = false;
      const int page = token.toInt(&okPage);
      if (!okPage || page < 1 || page > pageCount) {
        error =
            QString("Page number '%1' is invalid (document has %2 page(s)).")
                .arg(token)
                .arg(pageCount);
        return false;
      }
      outRanges.append(qMakePair(page, page));
    }
  }

  if (outRanges.isEmpty()) {
    error = "Please enter at least one valid page number.";
    return false;
  }
  return true;
}

} // namespace pdfutil

#include "controllers/pdf/split_controller.h"
#include "controllers/pdf/pdf_utils.h"

#include <QDir>
#include <QFile>
#include <QFileInfo>
#include <QImage>
#include <QPageLayout>
#include <QPageSize>
#include <QPainter>
#include <QPdfWriter>
#include <QSet>
#include <QTemporaryDir>
#include <QtConcurrent/QtConcurrent>
#include <memory>

SplitController::SplitController(QObject *parent)
    : PdfToolController(parent) {
  qRegisterMetaType<SplitController::Result>();
}

void SplitController::start(const Params &params) {
  waitForRunningJob();
  m_isCancelled = false;
  QFuture<void> future = QtConcurrent::run([this, params]() {
    runJob(params);
  });
  m_watcher.setFuture(future);
}

bool SplitController::extractPageRangeWithGhostscript(
    const QString &executable, const QString &inputPath, int firstPage,
    int lastPage, const QString &tempOutputPath, QString &error) {
  QStringList args = {"-sDEVICE=pdfwrite",
                      "-dCompatibilityLevel=1.7",
                      "-dSAFER",
                      "-dPDFSTOPONERROR",
                      "-dNOPAUSE",
                      "-dQUIET",
                      "-dBATCH",
                      "-dAutoRotatePages=/None",
                      QString("-dFirstPage=%1").arg(firstPage),
                      QString("-dLastPage=%1").arg(lastPage),
                      "-sOutputFile=candidate.pdf",
                      "-f",
                      QDir::toNativeSeparators(inputPath)};
  return pdfutil::runGhostscriptProcess(
      executable, args, QFileInfo(tempOutputPath).absolutePath(),
      tempOutputPath, m_isCancelled, error, false);
}

bool SplitController::extractPageRangeWithQtPdf(QPdfDocument &sourceDoc,
                                               int firstPage, int lastPage,
                                               const QString &tempOutputPath,
                                               QString &error) {
  QPdfWriter writer(tempOutputPath);
  writer.setResolution(300);
  std::unique_ptr<QPainter> painter;

  for (int page = firstPage - 1; page <= lastPage - 1; ++page) {
    if (m_isCancelled) {
      return false;
    }
    const QSizeF ptSize = sourceDoc.pagePointSize(page);
    QPageLayout layout(QPageSize(ptSize, QPageSize::Point),
                       QPageLayout::Portrait, QMarginsF(0, 0, 0, 0),
                       QPageLayout::Point);
    writer.setPageLayout(layout);
    if (!painter) {
      painter = std::make_unique<QPainter>(&writer);
      if (!painter->isActive()) {
        error = "Failed to initialize PDF writer.";
        return false;
      }
    } else {
      writer.newPage();
    }

    const QSize renderSize(qMax(1, qRound(ptSize.width() * 300.0 / 72.0)),
                           qMax(1, qRound(ptSize.height() * 300.0 / 72.0)));
    const QImage pageImg = pdfutil::renderPdfPageOnWhite(sourceDoc, page, renderSize);
    if (pageImg.isNull()) {
      error = QString("Failed to render page %1.").arg(page + 1);
      return false;
    }
    painter->setRenderHint(QPainter::SmoothPixmapTransform, true);
    painter->drawImage(
        QRectF(0, 0, painter->device()->width(), painter->device()->height()),
        pageImg);
  }

  if (painter) {
    painter->end();
  }
  return QFileInfo(tempOutputPath).size() > 0;
}

void SplitController::runJob(const Params &params) {
  Result result;
  result.outputDir = params.outputDir;

  QStringList sourcePaths = params.sourcePaths;
  if (sourcePaths.isEmpty() && !params.sourcePath.isEmpty()) {
    sourcePaths.append(params.sourcePath);
  }

  emit progressChanged(0, sourcePaths.size(), "Splitting PDF files...");

  int totalFilesCreated = 0;
  int failedDocs = 0;
  QStringList errors;
  QSet<QString> usedFileNames;
  const QString gsExec = pdfutil::findGhostscriptExecutable();

  for (int i = 0; i < sourcePaths.size(); ++i) {
    if (m_isCancelled) {
      break;
    }
    const QString &sourcePath = sourcePaths.at(i);
    const QFileInfo sourceInfo(sourcePath);

    emit fileStatusChanged(sourcePath, static_cast<int>(FileStatus::Processing),
                           "Splitting PDF pages");
    emit progressChanged(
        i, sourcePaths.size(),
        QString("Splitting %1 (%2 of %3)...")
            .arg(sourceInfo.fileName())
            .arg(i + 1)
            .arg(sourcePaths.size()));

    QPdfDocument sourceDoc;
    const auto loadErr = sourceDoc.load(sourceInfo.absoluteFilePath());
    if (loadErr != QPdfDocument::Error::None || sourceDoc.pageCount() <= 0) {
      failedDocs++;
      const QString err = "Cannot open PDF (damaged or encrypted).";
      errors.append(QString("%1: %2").arg(sourceInfo.fileName(), err));
      emit fileStatusChanged(sourcePath, static_cast<int>(FileStatus::Failed), err);
      continue;
    }

    const int pageCount = sourceDoc.pageCount();
    QVector<QPair<int, int>> ranges;
    QString rangeErr;
    if (params.mode == 0) {
      if (!pdfutil::parsePageRanges(params.rangeSpec, pageCount, ranges, rangeErr)) {
        failedDocs++;
        errors.append(QString("%1: %2").arg(sourceInfo.fileName(), rangeErr));
        emit fileStatusChanged(sourcePath, static_cast<int>(FileStatus::Failed), rangeErr);
        continue;
      }
    } else if (params.mode == 1) {
      for (int start = 1; start <= pageCount; start += params.interval) {
        const int end = qMin(pageCount, start + params.interval - 1);
        ranges.append(qMakePair(start, end));
      }
    } else {
      for (int p = 1; p <= pageCount; ++p) {
        ranges.append(qMakePair(p, p));
      }
    }

    int createdForThisPdf = 0;
    QString docError;
    for (const auto &range : ranges) {
      if (m_isCancelled) {
        break;
      }
      const int firstPage = range.first;
      const int lastPage = range.second;
      const int expectedPages = lastPage - firstPage + 1;

      const QString baseStem = (firstPage == lastPage)
                                   ? QString("%1_page_%2")
                                         .arg(sourceInfo.baseName())
                                         .arg(firstPage)
                                   : QString("%1_pages_%2-%3")
                                         .arg(sourceInfo.baseName())
                                         .arg(firstPage)
                                         .arg(lastPage);

      QString uniqueStem = baseStem;
      int suffix = 1;
      while (usedFileNames.contains(uniqueStem.toLower()) ||
             QFile::exists(QDir(params.outputDir).filePath(uniqueStem + ".pdf"))) {
        uniqueStem = QString("%1_%2").arg(baseStem).arg(suffix++);
      }
      usedFileNames.insert(uniqueStem.toLower());
      const QString outFilePath =
          QDir(params.outputDir).filePath(uniqueStem + ".pdf");

      QTemporaryDir tempDir(QDir(params.outputDir).filePath(".pdf-split-XXXXXX"));
      if (!tempDir.isValid()) {
        docError = tempDir.errorString();
        break;
      }
      const QString candidatePath = tempDir.filePath("candidate.pdf");
      bool extracted = false;
      if (!gsExec.isEmpty()) {
        QString gsErr;
        extracted = extractPageRangeWithGhostscript(
            gsExec, sourceInfo.absoluteFilePath(), firstPage, lastPage,
            candidatePath, gsErr);
        if (extracted) {
          QPdfDocument checkDoc;
          if (checkDoc.load(candidatePath) != QPdfDocument::Error::None ||
              checkDoc.pageCount() != expectedPages) {
            extracted = false;
            QFile::remove(candidatePath);
          }
        }
      }
      if (!extracted && !m_isCancelled) {
        extracted = extractPageRangeWithQtPdf(sourceDoc, firstPage, lastPage,
                                              candidatePath, docError);
      }
      if (!extracted || m_isCancelled) {
        if (docError.isEmpty()) {
          docError = "Failed to extract page range.";
        }
        break;
      }

      QPdfDocument verifyDoc;
      if (verifyDoc.load(candidatePath) != QPdfDocument::Error::None ||
          verifyDoc.pageCount() != expectedPages) {
        docError = "Extracted PDF failed validation.";
        break;
      }
      verifyDoc.close();

      if (!pdfutil::savePdfAtomically(candidatePath, outFilePath, m_isCancelled,
                                      docError)) {
        break;
      }
      createdForThisPdf++;
      totalFilesCreated++;
      result.generatedFiles.append(outFilePath);
    }

    sourceDoc.close();

    if (m_isCancelled) {
      emit fileStatusChanged(sourcePath, static_cast<int>(FileStatus::Cancelled),
                             "Split cancelled");
    } else if (docError.isEmpty() && createdForThisPdf > 0) {
      emit fileStatusChanged(
          sourcePath, static_cast<int>(FileStatus::Succeeded),
          QString("Created %1 PDF file(s) in %2")
              .arg(createdForThisPdf)
              .arg(params.outputDir));
    } else {
      emit fileStatusChanged(sourcePath, static_cast<int>(FileStatus::Failed),
                             docError);
    }

    if (!m_isCancelled) {
      emit progressChanged(i + 1, sourcePaths.size(),
                           QString("Processed %1 of %2").arg(i + 1).arg(sourcePaths.size()));
    }

    if (!docError.isEmpty() && !m_isCancelled) {
      failedDocs++;
      errors.append(QString("%1: %2").arg(sourceInfo.fileName(), docError));
    }
  }

  result.success = (totalFilesCreated > 0 && failedDocs == 0);
  result.cancelled = m_isCancelled.load();
  result.createdCount = totalFilesCreated;
  result.failedDocs = failedDocs;
  result.errors = errors;
  if (!errors.isEmpty()) {
    result.error = errors.join("\n");
  }

  emit finished(result);
}


================================================================================
FILE: controllers/pdf/split_controller.h
================================================================================
#ifndef SPLIT_CONTROLLER_H
#define SPLIT_CONTROLLER_H

#include "controllers/pdf/pdf_tool_controller.h"
#include <QPdfDocument>
#include <QString>
#include <QStringList>

class SplitController : public PdfToolController {
  Q_OBJECT

public:
  struct Params {
    QString sourcePath;
    QStringList sourcePaths;
    QString outputDir;
    int mode{0}; // 0: Custom ranges, 1: Fixed interval, 2: Extract all
    QString rangeSpec;
    int interval{1};
  };

  struct Result {
    bool success{false};
    bool cancelled{false};
    int createdCount{0};
    int failedDocs{0};
    QString outputDir;
    QString error;
    QStringList errors;
    QStringList generatedFiles;
  };

  explicit SplitController(QObject *parent = nullptr);
  ~SplitController() override { cancelAndWait(); }

  void start(const Params &params);

  bool extractPageRangeWithGhostscript(const QString &executable,
                                       const QString &inputPath, int firstPage,
                                       int lastPage,
                                       const QString &tempOutputPath,
                                       QString &error);

  bool extractPageRangeWithQtPdf(QPdfDocument &sourceDoc, int firstPage,
                                 int lastPage, const QString &tempOutputPath,
                                 QString &error);

signals:
  void finished(const SplitController::Result &result);

private:
  void runJob(const Params &params);
};

Q_DECLARE_METATYPE(SplitController::Result)

#endif // SPLIT_CONTROLLER_H

  QCoreApplication::setOrganizationName("Illufox Kusanagi");
  QCoreApplication::setApplicationName("Image Converter");

  QIcon appIcon;
  appIcon.addFile(":/icons/icons/icon-logo.png", QSize(16, 16));
  appIcon.addFile(":/icons/icons/icon-logo.png", QSize(32, 32));
  appIcon.addFile(":/icons/icons/icon-logo.png", QSize(64, 64));
  appIcon.addFile(":/icons/icons/icon-logo.png", QSize(128, 128));
  app.setWindowIcon(appIcon);

  MainWindow w;
  w.show();
  return app.exec();
}

MainWindow::MainWindow(QWidget *parent) : QMainWindow(parent) {
  setMinimumSize(460, 620);
  setWindowTitle("ImageConverter");

  m_centralWidget = new QWidget(this);
  QVBoxLayout *rootLayout = new QVBoxLayout(m_centralWidget);
  rootLayout->setContentsMargins(20, 18, 20, 20);
  rootLayout->setSpacing(18);

  // Header: Title & Theme Toggle
  QWidget *headerWidget = new QWidget(m_centralWidget);
  QHBoxLayout *headerLayout = new QHBoxLayout(headerWidget);
  headerLayout->setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0);

  m_titleLabel = new QLabel("ImageConverter", headerWidget);
  m_titleLabel->setFont(ui::Theme::instance().typography().font(
      ui::Theme::instance().typography().sizeXl, QFont::Bold));

  m_themeToggleBtn = new ui::Button(
      ui::Theme::instance().isDark() ? "Light" : "Dark",
      ui::ButtonVariant::Ghost, ui::ButtonSize::Small, headerWidget);
  connect(m_themeToggleBtn, &QPushButton::clicked, this,
          &MainWindow::onThemeToggleClicked);

  headerLayout->addWidget(m_titleLabel, 0, Qt::AlignVCenter);
  headerLayout->addStretch(1);
  headerLayout->addWidget(m_themeToggleBtn, 0, Qt::AlignVCenter);
  rootLayout->addWidget(headerWidget);

  // Main Tabs: Convert Image, Compress PDF, UI Components
  m_tabs = new ui::Tabs(ui::TabVariant::Underline, m_centralWidget);

  m_mainPage = new MainPage(m_tabs);
  m_tabs->addTab("Convert Image", QString(":/icons/icons/lucide-image.svg"),
                 m_mainPage);

  m_pdfPage = new PdfPage(m_tabs);
  m_tabs->addTab("PDF Tools", QString(":/icons/icons/lucide-file-text.svg"),
                 m_pdfPage);

  // m_galleryPage = new GalleryPage(m_tabs);
  // m_tabs->addTab("UI Components",
  // QString(":/icons/icons/lucide-sliders.svg"),
  //                m_galleryPage);

  rootLayout->addWidget(m_tabs, 1);
  setCentralWidget(m_centralWidget);

  connect(&ui::Theme::instance(), &ui::Theme::themeChanged, this,
          &MainWindow::applyThemeStyles);
  applyThemeStyles();
}

void MainWindow::onThemeToggleClicked() { ui::Theme::instance().toggleMode(); }

void MainWindow::applyThemeStyles() {
  const auto &c = ui::Theme::instance().colors();
  ui::ThemeMode mode = ui::Theme::instance().mode();

  setStyleSheet(QString("QMainWindow { background-color: %1; }")
                    .arg(ui::StyleHelper::toHexString(c.background)));

  m_centralWidget->setStyleSheet(
      QString("background-color: %1;")
          .arg(ui::StyleHelper::toHexString(c.background)));

  m_titleLabel->setStyleSheet(
      QString("color: %1; background: transparent;")
          .arg(ui::StyleHelper::toHexString(c.foreground)));

  QString modeText;
  QString iconPath;
  if (mode == ui::ThemeMode::Light) {
    modeText = "Light";
    iconPath = ":/icons/icons/lucide-sun.svg";
  } else if (mode == ui::ThemeMode::Dark) {
    modeText = "Dark";
    iconPath = ":/icons/icons/lucide-moon.svg";
  } else {
    modeText = "System";
    iconPath = ":/icons/icons/lucide-monitor.svg";
  }

  m_themeToggleBtn->setText(modeText);
  m_themeToggleBtn->setIcon(
      ui::Icon::get(iconPath, c.foreground, QSize(16, 16)));
}

QLabel *galleryLabel(const QString &text, QWidget *parent,
                     const char *role = "mutedText") {
  auto *label = new QLabel(text, parent);
  label->setTextFormat(Qt::PlainText);
  label->setWordWrap(true);
  label->setProperty("galleryRole", role);
  return label;
}

QWidget *controlExample(const QString &title, const QString &description,
                        QWidget *control, QWidget *parent) {
  auto *example = new QWidget(parent);
  auto *layout = new QVBoxLayout(example);
  layout->setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0);
  layout->setSpacing(ui::Theme::instance().spacing().space1);
  auto *label = galleryLabel(title, example, "bodyText");
  label->setBuddy(control);
  control->setAccessibleName(title);
  control->setAccessibleDescription(description);
  layout->addWidget(label);
  layout->addWidget(control);
  if (!description.isEmpty()) {
    layout->addWidget(galleryLabel(description, example));
  }
  return example;
}

GalleryPage::GalleryPage(QWidget *parent) : QWidget(parent) {
  setupUi();
  connect(&ui::Theme::instance(), &ui::Theme::themeChanged, this,
          &GalleryPage::applyThemeStyles);
