================================================================================
FILE: ui-kit/components/avatar.cpp
================================================================================
#include "avatar.h"
#include "../theme/style_helper.h"
#include <QPainter>
#include <QPainterPath>

namespace ui {

Avatar::Avatar(QWidget *parent)
    : Avatar("?", AvatarSize::Default, parent) {}

Avatar::Avatar(const QString &initials, AvatarSize size, QWidget *parent)
    : QWidget(parent), m_initials(initials), m_size(size) {
  setSize(size);
  connect(&Theme::instance(), &Theme::themeChanged, this, &Avatar::applyThemeStyles);
}

Avatar::Avatar(const QPixmap &image, AvatarSize size, QWidget *parent)
    : QWidget(parent), m_image(image), m_size(size) {
  setSize(size);
  connect(&Theme::instance(), &Theme::themeChanged, this, &Avatar::applyThemeStyles);
}

void Avatar::setImage(const QPixmap &pixmap) {
  m_image = pixmap;
  update();
}

void Avatar::setInitials(const QString &initials) {
  m_image = QPixmap{};
  m_initials = initials;
  update();
}

AvatarSize Avatar::size() const {
  return m_size;
}

void Avatar::setSize(AvatarSize size) {
  m_size = size;
  int d = diameter();
  setFixedSize(d, d);
  update();
}

AvatarStatus Avatar::status() const {
  return m_status;
}

void Avatar::setStatus(AvatarStatus status) {
  m_status = status;
  update();
}

int Avatar::diameter() const {
  switch (m_size) {
  case AvatarSize::Small:
    return 28;
  case AvatarSize::Large:
    return 56;
  case AvatarSize::Default:
  default:
    return 40;
  }
}

QSize Avatar::sizeHint() const {
  int d = diameter();
  return QSize(d, d);
}

void Avatar::applyThemeStyles() {
  update();
}

void Avatar::paintEvent(QPaintEvent *event) {
  Q_UNUSED(event);

  QPainter painter(this);
  painter.setRenderHint(QPainter::Antialiasing);
  painter.setRenderHint(QPainter::SmoothPixmapTransform);

  const auto &c = Theme::instance().colors();
  const auto &t = Theme::instance().typography();
  int d = diameter();
  QRectF circleRect(0, 0, d, d);

  QPainterPath circlePath;
  circlePath.addEllipse(circleRect);

  if (!m_image.isNull()) {
    painter.save();
    painter.setClipPath(circlePath);
    QPixmap scaled = m_image.scaled(d, d, Qt::KeepAspectRatioByExpanding, Qt::SmoothTransformation);
    int x = (d - scaled.width()) / 2;
    int y = (d - scaled.height()) / 2;
    painter.drawPixmap(x, y, scaled);
    painter.restore();
  } else {
    // Background
    painter.fillPath(circlePath, c.muted);

    // Initials Text
    painter.setPen(c.mutedForeground);
    int fontSize = (m_size == AvatarSize::Small) ? t.sizeXs : (m_size == AvatarSize::Large) ? t.sizeLg : t.sizeSm;
    painter.setFont(t.font(fontSize, QFont::DemiBold));
    painter.drawText(circleRect, Qt::AlignCenter, m_initials.toUpper());
  }

  // Border outline
  painter.setPen(QPen(c.border, 1));
  painter.drawEllipse(circleRect.adjusted(0.5, 0.5, -0.5, -0.5));

  // Status Dot
  if (m_status != AvatarStatus::None) {
    int dotSize = (m_size == AvatarSize::Small) ? 8 : (m_size == AvatarSize::Large) ? 14 : 10;
    qreal ringOffset = 2.0;
    qreal dotX = d - dotSize - ringOffset;
    qreal dotY = d - dotSize - ringOffset;
    QRectF dotRect(dotX, dotY, dotSize, dotSize);

    QColor statusColor;
    switch (m_status) {
    case AvatarStatus::Online:
      statusColor = QColor("#22c55e");
      break;
    case AvatarStatus::Busy:
      statusColor = QColor("#ef4444");
      break;
    case AvatarStatus::Away:
      statusColor = QColor("#f59e0b");
      break;
    case AvatarStatus::Offline:
    default:
      statusColor = QColor("#6b7280");
      break;
    }

    // Outer background ring
    painter.setPen(Qt::NoPen);
    painter.setBrush(c.background);
    painter.drawEllipse(dotRect.adjusted(-1.5, -1.5, 1.5, 1.5));

    // Inner status fill
    painter.setBrush(statusColor);
    painter.drawEllipse(dotRect);
  }
}

} // namespace ui

﻿#include "badge.h"
#include "../theme/style_helper.h"

namespace ui {

Badge::Badge(QWidget *parent)
    : Badge("", BadgeVariant::Default, parent) {}

Badge::Badge(const QString &text, QWidget *parent)
    : Badge(text, BadgeVariant::Default, parent) {}

Badge::Badge(const QString &text, BadgeVariant variant, QWidget *parent)
    : QLabel(text, parent), m_variant(variant) {
  setAlignment(Qt::AlignCenter);
  connect(&Theme::instance(), &Theme::themeChanged, this, &Badge::applyThemeStyles);
  applyThemeStyles();
}

BadgeVariant Badge::variant() const {
  return m_variant;
}

void Badge::setVariant(BadgeVariant variant) {
  if (m_variant != variant) {
    m_variant = variant;
    applyThemeStyles();
  }
}

void Badge::applyThemeStyles() {
  const auto &c = Theme::instance().colors();
  const auto &t = Theme::instance().typography();

  setFont(t.font(t.sizeXs, QFont::DemiBold));

  QString bg, fg, border;

  switch (m_variant) {
  case BadgeVariant::Secondary:
    bg = StyleHelper::toHexString(c.secondary);
    fg = StyleHelper::toHexString(c.secondaryForeground);
    border = "transparent";
    break;

  case BadgeVariant::Outline:
    bg = "transparent";
    fg = StyleHelper::toHexString(c.foreground);
    border = StyleHelper::toHexString(c.border);
    break;

  case BadgeVariant::Destructive:
    bg = StyleHelper::toHexString(c.destructive);
    fg = StyleHelper::toHexString(c.destructiveForeground);
    border = "transparent";
    break;

  case BadgeVariant::Default:
  default:
    bg = StyleHelper::toHexString(c.primary);
    fg = StyleHelper::toHexString(c.primaryForeground);
    border = "transparent";
    break;
  }

  setStyleSheet(QString(
      "QLabel {"
      "  background-color: %1;"
      "  color: %2;"
      "  border: 1px solid %3;"
      "  border-radius: 9999px;"
      "  padding: 2px 8px;"
      "}")
      .arg(bg)
      .arg(fg)
      .arg(border));
}

} // namespace ui

================================================================================
FILE: ui-kit/components/button.cpp
================================================================================
#include "button.h"
#include "../theme/style_helper.h"
#include "spinner.h"
#include <QHideEvent>
#include <QShowEvent>
#include <QStyleOptionButton>
#include <QStylePainter>

namespace ui {

Button::Button(QWidget *parent)
    : Button("", ButtonVariant::Primary, ButtonSize::Default, parent) {}

Button::Button(const QString &text, QWidget *parent)
    : Button(text, ButtonVariant::Primary, ButtonSize::Default, parent) {}

Button::Button(const QString &text, ButtonVariant variant, ButtonSize size,
               QWidget *parent)
    : QPushButton(text, parent), m_variant(variant), m_size(size) {
  setCursor(Qt::PointingHandCursor);
  m_loadingText = tr("Please wait...");
  m_loadingSpinner = new Spinner(iconSize().height(), this);
  m_loadingSpinner->setAttribute(Qt::WA_TransparentForMouseEvents);
  m_loadingSpinner->setFocusPolicy(Qt::NoFocus);
  m_loadingSpinner->stop();
  m_loadingSpinner->hide();
  connect(&Theme::instance(), &Theme::themeChanged, this,
          &Button::applyThemeStyles);
  applyThemeStyles();
}

ButtonVariant Button::variant() const { return m_variant; }

void Button::setVariant(ButtonVariant variant) {
  if (m_variant != variant) {
    m_variant = variant;
    applyThemeStyles();
  }
}

ButtonSize Button::size() const { return m_size; }

void Button::setSize(ButtonSize size) {
  if (m_size != size) {
    m_size = size;
    applyThemeStyles();
  }
}

bool Button::isLoading() const { return m_isLoading; }

void Button::setLoading(bool loading) {
  if (m_isLoading == loading) {
    return;
  }
  if (loading) {
    m_savedText = text();
    m_savedIcon = icon();
    m_savedSizeHint = QPushButton::sizeHint();
    m_savedMinimumSizeHint = QPushButton::minimumSizeHint();
    // isEnabled() also includes the parent's state, which must not become an
    // explicit disable on this button when loading finishes.
    m_wasExplicitlyDisabled = testAttribute(Qt::WA_ForceDisabled);
    m_isLoading = true;
    setText(m_loadingText);
    setIcon(QIcon());
    setEnabled(false);
    // Disabling can synchronously invoke handlers that cancel loading.
    if (!m_isLoading) {
      return;
    }
    m_loadingSpinner->show();
    if (isVisible()) {
      m_loadingSpinner->start();
    }
  } else {
    m_isLoading = false;
    m_loadingSpinner->stop();
    m_loadingSpinner->hide();
    setText(m_savedText);
    setIcon(m_savedIcon);
    setEnabled(!m_wasExplicitlyDisabled);
  }
  updateGeometry();
  update();
}

QString Button::loadingText() const { return m_loadingText; }

void Button::setLoadingText(const QString &text) {
  if (m_loadingText == text) {
    return;
  }
  m_loadingText = text;
  if (m_isLoading) {
    setText(m_loadingText);
    update();
  }
}

QSize Button::sizeHint() const {
  return m_isLoading ? m_savedSizeHint : QPushButton::sizeHint();
}

QSize Button::minimumSizeHint() const {
  return m_isLoading ? m_savedMinimumSizeHint : QPushButton::minimumSizeHint();
}

void Button::paintEvent(QPaintEvent *event) {
  if (!m_isLoading) {
    QPushButton::paintEvent(event);
    return;
  }

  QStylePainter painter(this);
  QStyleOptionButton option;
  initStyleOption(&option);
  option.text.clear();
  option.icon = QIcon();
  option.features.setFlag(QStyleOptionButton::HasMenu, false);
  painter.drawControl(QStyle::CE_PushButton, option);

  const QRect content =
      style()->subElementRect(QStyle::SE_PushButtonContents, &option, this);
  const int extent = qMax(
      1, qMin(iconSize().height(), qMin(content.width(), content.height())));
  const int spacing = Theme::instance().spacing().space2;
  int textFlags = Qt::AlignCenter | Qt::TextSingleLine | Qt::TextShowMnemonic;
  if (!style()->styleHint(QStyle::SH_UnderlineShortcut, &option, this)) {
    textFlags |= Qt::TextHideMnemonic;
  }
  const QString caption =
      m_size == ButtonSize::Icon
          ? QString()
          : fontMetrics().elidedText(
                text(), Qt::ElideRight,
                qMax(0, content.width() - extent - spacing), textFlags);
  const int textWidth = fontMetrics().size(textFlags, caption).width();
  const int groupWidth = extent + (caption.isEmpty() ? 0 : spacing + textWidth);
  const int groupHeight =
      caption.isEmpty() ? extent : qMax(extent, fontMetrics().height());
  const QRect group =
      QStyle::alignedRect(layoutDirection(), Qt::AlignCenter,
                          QSize(groupWidth, groupHeight), content);
  m_loadingSpinner->setSpinnerSize(extent);
  m_loadingSpinner->setGeometry(QStyle::visualRect(
      layoutDirection(), group,
      QRect(group.left(), group.top() + (group.height() - extent) / 2, extent,
            extent)));

  if (!caption.isEmpty()) {
    const QRect textRect = QStyle::visualRect(
        layoutDirection(), group, group.adjusted(extent + spacing, 0, 0, 0));
    painter.drawItemText(textRect, textFlags, option.palette, isEnabled(),
                         caption, QPalette::ButtonText);
  }
}

void Button::showEvent(QShowEvent *event) {
  QPushButton::showEvent(event);
  if (m_isLoading) {
    m_loadingSpinner->start();
  }
}

void Button::hideEvent(QHideEvent *event) {
  m_loadingSpinner->stop();
  QPushButton::hideEvent(event);
}

void Button::applyThemeStyles() {
  const auto &c = Theme::instance().colors();
  const auto &r = Theme::instance().radius();
  const auto &t = Theme::instance().typography();
  bool isDark = Theme::instance().isDark();

  int height = 40;
  int paddingH = 16;
  int fontSize = t.sizeBase;
  int borderRadius = r.md;

  switch (m_size) {
  case ButtonSize::Small:
    height = 32;
    paddingH = 12;
    fontSize = t.sizeSm;
    borderRadius = r.sm;
    break;
  case ButtonSize::Large:
    height = 48;
    paddingH = 24;
    fontSize = t.sizeLg;
    borderRadius = r.lg;
    break;
  case ButtonSize::Icon:
    height = 36;
    paddingH = 0;
    setFixedWidth(36);
    break;
  case ButtonSize::Default:
  default:
    height = 40;
    paddingH = 16;
    fontSize = t.sizeBase;
    borderRadius = r.md;
    break;
  }

  if (m_size != ButtonSize::Icon) {
    setMinimumWidth(0);
    setMaximumWidth(16777215);
  }
  setFixedHeight(height);
  setFont(t.font(fontSize, QFont::DemiBold));

  QString bg, fg, hoverBg, pressedBg, border, hoverBorder;

  switch (m_variant) {
  case ButtonVariant::Secondary:
    bg = StyleHelper::toHexString(c.secondary);
    fg = StyleHelper::toHexString(c.secondaryForeground);
    hoverBg =
        StyleHelper::toHexString(StyleHelper::hoverColor(c.secondary, isDark));
    pressedBg = StyleHelper::toHexString(StyleHelper::darken(c.secondary, 10));
    border = "transparent";
    hoverBorder = "transparent";
    break;

  case ButtonVariant::Outline:
    bg = "transparent";
    fg = StyleHelper::toHexString(c.foreground);
    hoverBg = StyleHelper::toHexString(c.accent);
    pressedBg =
        StyleHelper::toHexString(StyleHelper::hoverColor(c.accent, isDark));
    border = StyleHelper::toHexString(c.border);
    hoverBorder = StyleHelper::toHexString(c.border);
    break;

  case ButtonVariant::Ghost:
    bg = "transparent";
    fg = StyleHelper::toHexString(c.foreground);
    hoverBg = StyleHelper::toHexString(c.accent);
    pressedBg =
        StyleHelper::toHexString(StyleHelper::hoverColor(c.accent, isDark));
    border = "transparent";
    hoverBorder = "transparent";
    break;

  case ButtonVariant::Destructive:
    bg = StyleHelper::toHexString(c.destructive);
    fg = StyleHelper::toHexString(c.destructiveForeground);
    hoverBg = StyleHelper::toHexString(StyleHelper::lighten(c.destructive, 10));
    pressedBg =
        StyleHelper::toHexString(StyleHelper::darken(c.destructive, 10));
    border = "transparent";
    hoverBorder = "transparent";
    break;

  case ButtonVariant::Link:
    bg = "transparent";
    fg = StyleHelper::toHexString(c.primary);
    hoverBg = "transparent";
    pressedBg = "transparent";
    border = "transparent";
    hoverBorder = "transparent";
    break;

  case ButtonVariant::Primary:
  default:
    bg = StyleHelper::toHexString(c.primary);
    fg = StyleHelper::toHexString(c.primaryForeground);
    hoverBg = StyleHelper::toHexString(StyleHelper::withAlphaF(c.primary, 0.9));
    pressedBg = StyleHelper::toHexString(StyleHelper::darken(c.primary, 15));
    border = "transparent";
    hoverBorder = "transparent";
    break;
  }

  QString style =
      QString("QPushButton {"
              "  background-color: %1;"
              "  color: %2;"
              "  border: 1px solid %3;"
              "  border-radius: %4px;"
              "  padding: 0px %5px;"
              "  text-align: center;"
              "}"
              "QPushButton:hover {"
              "  background-color: %6;"
              "  border-color: %7;"
              "}"
              "QPushButton:pressed {"
              "  background-color: %8;"
              "}"
              "QPushButton:focus {"
              "  outline: none;"
              "  border: 2px solid %9;"
              "}"
              "QPushButton:disabled {"
              "  background-color: %10;"
              "  color: %11;"
              "  border-color: transparent;"
              "}")
          .arg(bg)
          .arg(fg)
          .arg(border)
          .arg(borderRadius)
          .arg(paddingH)
          .arg(hoverBg)
          .arg(hoverBorder)
          .arg(pressedBg)
          .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(c.ring))
          .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(StyleHelper::withAlphaF(c.muted, 0.5)))
          .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(c.mutedForeground));

  if (m_variant == ButtonVariant::Link) {
    style += "QPushButton:hover { text-decoration: underline; }";
  }

  setStyleSheet(style);
  m_loadingSpinner->setColor(c.mutedForeground);
}

} // namespace ui

#ifndef UI_BUTTON_H
#define UI_BUTTON_H

#include "../theme/theme.h"
#include <QIcon>
#include <QPushButton>

namespace ui {

class Spinner;

enum class ButtonVariant {
  Primary,
  Secondary,
  Outline,
  Ghost,
  Destructive,
  Link
};

enum class ButtonSize { Small, Default, Large, Icon };

class Button : public QPushButton {
  Q_OBJECT

public:
  explicit Button(QWidget *parent = nullptr);
  explicit Button(const QString &text, QWidget *parent = nullptr);
  Button(const QString &text, ButtonVariant variant,
         ButtonSize size = ButtonSize::Default, QWidget *parent = nullptr);
  ~Button() override = default;

  ButtonVariant variant() const;
  void setVariant(ButtonVariant variant);

  ButtonSize size() const;
  void setSize(ButtonSize size);

  bool isLoading() const;
  void setLoading(bool loading);
  QString loadingText() const;
  void setLoadingText(const QString &text);

  QSize sizeHint() const override;
  QSize minimumSizeHint() const override;

protected:
  void paintEvent(QPaintEvent *event) override;
  void showEvent(QShowEvent *event) override;
  void hideEvent(QHideEvent *event) override;

private slots:
  void applyThemeStyles();

private:
  ButtonVariant m_variant{ButtonVariant::Primary};
  ButtonSize m_size{ButtonSize::Default};
  bool m_isLoading{false};
  bool m_wasExplicitlyDisabled{false};
  QString m_loadingText;
  QString m_savedText;
  QIcon m_savedIcon;
  QSize m_savedSizeHint;
  QSize m_savedMinimumSizeHint;
  Spinner *m_loadingSpinner{nullptr};
};

} // namespace ui

#endif // UI_BUTTON_H

#include "card.h"
#include "../theme/style_helper.h"

namespace ui {

Card::Card(QWidget *parent)
    : Card("", "", parent) {}

Card::Card(const QString &title, const QString &description, QWidget *parent)
    : QFrame(parent) {
  setAttribute(Qt::WA_StyledBackground, true);
  m_rootLayout = new QVBoxLayout(this);
  m_rootLayout->setContentsMargins(24, 24, 24, 24);
  m_rootLayout->setSpacing(16);

  m_headerLayout = new QVBoxLayout();
  m_headerLayout->setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0);
  m_headerLayout->setSpacing(4);

  m_titleLabel = new QLabel(this);
  m_titleLabel->setVisible(false);

  m_descLabel = new QLabel(this);
  m_descLabel->setWordWrap(true);
  m_descLabel->setVisible(false);

  m_headerLayout->addWidget(m_titleLabel);
  m_headerLayout->addWidget(m_descLabel);
  m_rootLayout->addLayout(m_headerLayout);

  m_contentLayout = new QVBoxLayout();
  m_contentLayout->setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0);
  m_contentLayout->setSpacing(12);
  m_rootLayout->addLayout(m_contentLayout, 1);

  m_footerLayout = new QHBoxLayout();
  m_footerLayout->setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0);
  m_footerLayout->setSpacing(12);
  m_rootLayout->addLayout(m_footerLayout);

  if (!title.isEmpty()) {
    setTitle(title);
  }
  if (!description.isEmpty()) {
    setDescription(description);
  }

  connect(&Theme::instance(), &Theme::themeChanged, this, &Card::applyThemeStyles);
  applyThemeStyles();
}

void Card::setTitle(const QString &title) {
  m_titleLabel->setText(title);
  m_titleLabel->setVisible(!title.isEmpty());
}

void Card::setDescription(const QString &description) {
  m_descLabel->setText(description);
  m_descLabel->setVisible(!description.isEmpty());
}

void Card::setContentWidget(QWidget *widget) {
  if (widget) {
    m_contentLayout->addWidget(widget);
  }
}

void Card::setContentLayout(QLayout *layout) {
  if (layout) {
    m_contentLayout->addLayout(layout);
  }
}

void Card::setFooterWidget(QWidget *widget) {
  if (widget) {
    m_footerLayout->addWidget(widget);
  }
}

void Card::setFooterLayout(QLayout *layout) {
  if (layout) {
    m_footerLayout->addLayout(layout);
  }
}

QVBoxLayout *Card::contentLayout() const {
  return m_contentLayout;
}

QHBoxLayout *Card::footerLayout() const {
  return m_footerLayout;
}

void Card::applyThemeStyles() {
  const auto &c = Theme::instance().colors();
  const auto &r = Theme::instance().radius();
  const auto &t = Theme::instance().typography();

  m_titleLabel->setFont(t.font(t.sizeXl, QFont::DemiBold));
  m_titleLabel->setStyleSheet(QString("color: %1; background: transparent;")
                                  .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(c.cardForeground)));

  m_descLabel->setFont(t.font(t.sizeSm, QFont::Normal));
  m_descLabel->setStyleSheet(QString("color: %1; background: transparent;")
                                 .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(c.mutedForeground)));

  setStyleSheet(QString(
      "ui--Card {"
      "  background-color: %1;"
      "  border: 1px solid %2;"
      "  border-radius: %3px;"
      "}")
      .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(c.card))
      .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(c.border))
      .arg(r.lg));
}

} // namespace ui

FILE: ui-kit/components/checkbox.cpp
================================================================================
#include "checkbox.h"
#include "../theme/style_helper.h"
#include "../theme/theme.h"
#include <QPainter>
#include <QPainterPath>
#include <QStyle>
#include <QStyleOptionButton>

namespace ui {

Checkbox::Checkbox(QWidget *parent) : Checkbox(QString(), parent) {}

Checkbox::Checkbox(const QString &text, QWidget *parent)
    : QCheckBox(text, parent) {
  connect(&Theme::instance(), &Theme::themeChanged, this,
          &Checkbox::applyThemeStyles);
  applyThemeStyles();
}

bool Checkbox::hasError() const { return m_hasError; }

void Checkbox::setError(bool hasError) {
  if (m_hasError == hasError) {
    return;
  }
  m_hasError = hasError;
  applyThemeStyles();
}

void Checkbox::paintEvent(QPaintEvent *event) {
  QCheckBox::paintEvent(event);
  if (checkState() == Qt::Unchecked) {
    return;
  }

  QStyleOptionButton option;
  initStyleOption(&option);
  const QRect indicator =
      style()->subElementRect(QStyle::SE_CheckBoxIndicator, &option, this);
  const qreal inset = Theme::instance().spacing().space1;
  const QRectF mark = QRectF(indicator).adjusted(inset, inset, -inset, -inset);
  const auto &c = Theme::instance().colors();

  QPainter painter(this);
  painter.setRenderHint(QPainter::Antialiasing);
  painter.setPen(QPen(isEnabled() ? c.primaryForeground : c.mutedForeground, 2,
                      Qt::SolidLine, Qt::RoundCap, Qt::RoundJoin));
  if (checkState() == Qt::PartiallyChecked) {
    painter.drawLine(QPointF(mark.left(), mark.center().y()),
                     QPointF(mark.right(), mark.center().y()));
  } else {
    QPainterPath path;
    path.moveTo(mark.left(), mark.center().y());
    path.lineTo(mark.left() + mark.width() * 0.4, mark.bottom());
    path.lineTo(mark.right(), mark.top());
    painter.drawPath(path);
  }
}

void Checkbox::applyThemeStyles() {
  const auto &theme = Theme::instance();
  const auto &c = theme.colors();
  const auto &r = theme.radius();
  const auto &s = theme.spacing();
  const auto &t = theme.typography();
  const QColor border =
      m_hasError ? c.destructive
                 : StyleHelper::tint(c.border, c.foreground, 0.35);
  const QColor checkedBorder = m_hasError ? c.destructive : c.primary;
  const QColor focusBorder = m_hasError ? c.destructive : c.ring;

  setFont(t.font(t.sizeBase));
  setStyleSheet(QString("ui--Checkbox {"
                        "  color: %1;"
                        "  spacing: %2px;"
                        "}"
                        "ui--Checkbox::indicator {"
                        "  width: %3px;"
                        "  height: %3px;"
                        "  border: 1px solid %4;"
                        "  border-radius: %5px;"
                        "  background-color: %6;"
                        "  image: none;"
                        "}"
                        "ui--Checkbox::indicator:checked,"
                        "ui--Checkbox::indicator:indeterminate {"
                        "  background-color: %7;"
                        "  border-color: %8;"
                        "}"
                        "ui--Checkbox::indicator:hover { border-color: %8; }"
                        "ui--Checkbox::indicator:focus { border-color: %9; }"
                        "ui--Checkbox:disabled { color: %10; }"
                        "ui--Checkbox::indicator:disabled {"
                        "  background-color: %11;"
                        "  border-color: %12;"
                        "}")
                    .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(c.foreground))
                    .arg(s.space2)
                    .arg(s.space4 - 2)
                    .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(border))
                    .arg(r.sm)
                    .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(c.background))
                    .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(c.primary))
                    .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(checkedBorder))
                    .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(focusBorder))
                    .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(c.mutedForeground))
                    .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(c.muted))
                    .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(c.border)));
  update();
}

} // namespace ui

================================================================================
FILE: ui-kit/components/combobox.cpp
================================================================================
#include "combobox.h"
#include "../theme/style_helper.h"
#include "../theme/theme.h"
#include "popover.h"

#include <QApplication>
#include <QEvent>
#include <QHBoxLayout>
#include <QHideEvent>
#include <QKeyEvent>
#include <QLabel>
#include <QLineEdit>
#include <QListView>
#include <QPalette>
#include <QPointer>
#include <QSignalBlocker>
#include <QSortFilterProxyModel>
#include <QStandardItemModel>
#include <QToolButton>
#include <QVBoxLayout>

namespace ui {

Combobox::Combobox(QWidget *parent) : Combobox(QStringList(), parent) {}

Combobox::Combobox(const QStringList &items, QWidget *parent)
    : QWidget(parent), m_layout(new QHBoxLayout(this)),
      m_lineEdit(new QLineEdit(this)), m_button(new QToolButton(this)),
      m_popup(new Popover(this)), m_searchEdit(new QLineEdit(m_popup)),
      m_list(new QListView(m_popup)),
      m_emptyLabel(new QLabel(tr("No results found"), m_popup)),
      m_model(new QStandardItemModel(this)),
      m_proxy(new QSortFilterProxyModel(this)) {
  setAttribute(Qt::WA_StyledBackground, true);
  setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy::Expanding, QSizePolicy::Fixed);
  setFocusPolicy(Qt::StrongFocus);
  setFocusProxy(m_lineEdit);
  setAccessibleName(tr("Combobox"));
  setAccessibleDescription(tr(
      "Type to search, use arrow keys to choose, and press Enter to select."));

  m_lineEdit->setObjectName(QStringLiteral("uiComboboxEditor"));
  m_lineEdit->setFrame(false);
  m_lineEdit->setPlaceholderText(tr("Select an item"));
  m_lineEdit->setAccessibleName(tr("Selected item"));
  m_lineEdit->setAccessibleDescription(accessibleDescription());
  m_lineEdit->installEventFilter(this);

  m_button->setObjectName(QStringLiteral("uiComboboxButton"));
  m_button->setArrowType(Qt::DownArrow);
  m_button->setFocusPolicy(Qt::NoFocus);
  m_button->setAccessibleName(tr("Show choices"));
  m_button->setToolTip(tr("Show choices"));
  m_layout->addWidget(m_lineEdit, 1);
  m_layout->addWidget(m_button);

  m_searchEdit->setObjectName(QStringLiteral("uiComboboxSearch"));
  m_searchEdit->setPlaceholderText(tr("Search items..."));
  m_searchEdit->setAccessibleName(tr("Search items"));
  m_searchEdit->setAccessibleDescription(accessibleDescription());
  m_searchEdit->installEventFilter(this);
  m_proxy->setSourceModel(m_model);
  m_proxy->setFilterKeyColumn(0);
  m_proxy->setFilterRole(Qt::DisplayRole);
  m_proxy->setFilterCaseSensitivity(Qt::CaseInsensitive);
  m_list->setObjectName(QStringLiteral("uiComboboxResults"));
  m_list->setModel(m_proxy);
  m_list->setFrameShape(QFrame::NoFrame);
  m_list->setSelectionMode(QAbstractItemView::SingleSelection);
  m_list->setEditTriggers(QAbstractItemView::NoEditTriggers);
  m_list->setFocusPolicy(Qt::NoFocus);
  m_list->setHorizontalScrollBarPolicy(Qt::ScrollBarAlwaysOff);
  m_list->setSizeAdjustPolicy(QAbstractScrollArea::AdjustToContents);
  m_list->setTextElideMode(Qt::ElideRight);
  m_list->setUniformItemSizes(true);
  m_list->setMinimumSize(0, 0);
  m_list->setAccessibleName(tr("Matching items"));
  m_list->installEventFilter(this);
  m_emptyLabel->setObjectName(QStringLiteral("uiComboboxEmpty"));
  m_emptyLabel->setAlignment(Qt::AlignCenter);
  m_emptyLabel->setWordWrap(true);
  m_popup->setAccessibleName(tr("Combobox choices"));
  m_popup->contentLayout()->addWidget(m_searchEdit);
  m_popup->contentLayout()->addWidget(m_list);
  m_popup->contentLayout()->addWidget(m_emptyLabel);

  connect(m_lineEdit, &QLineEdit::textEdited, this, &Combobox::showPopup);
  connect(m_searchEdit, &QLineEdit::textEdited, this, &Combobox::updateFilter);
  connect(m_list, &QListView::clicked, this, &Combobox::commitIndex);
  connect(m_button, &QToolButton::clicked, this, [this]() {
    if (m_popup->isVisible()) {
      m_popup->closePopover();
    } else {
      showPopup();
    }
  });
  connect(m_popup, &Popover::closed, this, [this]() {
    restoreCommittedText();
    applyThemeStyles();
  });
  connect(qApp, &QApplication::focusChanged, this,
          [this](QWidget *, QWidget *focused) {
            if (!m_openingPopup && m_popup->isVisible() && focused &&
                focused != m_popup && !m_popup->isAncestorOf(focused)) {
              m_popup->closePopover();
            }
          });
  connect(&Theme::instance(), &Theme::themeChanged, this,
          &Combobox::applyThemeStyles);
  setItems(items);
  applyThemeStyles();
}

Combobox::~Combobox() {
  disconnect(m_popup, nullptr, this, nullptr);
  m_popup->closePopover();
}

void Combobox::setItems(const QStringList &items) {
  const int oldIndex = m_currentIndex;
  const QString oldText = currentText();
  m_popup->closePopover();
  m_currentIndex = -1;
  m_model->clear();
  for (const QString &text : items) {
    auto *item = new QStandardItem(text);
    item->setEditable(false);
    m_model->appendRow(item);
  }
  restoreCommittedText();
  updateResults();
  notifySelectionChanged(oldIndex, oldText);
}

void Combobox::addItem(const QString &text, const QVariant &data) {
  auto *item = new QStandardItem(text);
  item->setEditable(false);
  item->setData(data, Qt::UserRole);
  m_model->appendRow(item);
  if (m_popup->isVisible()) {
    updateFilter(m_searchEdit->text());
  } else {
    updateResults();
  }
}

int Combobox::currentIndex() const { return m_currentIndex; }

void Combobox::setCurrentIndex(int index) {
  if (index < 0 || index >= m_model->rowCount()) {
    index = -1;
  }
  if (index >= 0 && !isSelectable(m_model->index(index, 0))) {
    return;
  }
  const int oldIndex = m_currentIndex;
  const QString oldText = currentText();
  m_currentIndex = index;
  m_popup->closePopover();
  restoreCommittedText();
  notifySelectionChanged(oldIndex, oldText);
}

QString Combobox::currentText() const {
  return m_currentIndex >= 0 ? m_model->index(m_currentIndex, 0)
                                   .data(Qt::DisplayRole)
                                   .toString()
                             : QString();
}

QVariant Combobox::currentData() const {
  return m_currentIndex >= 0
             ? m_model->index(m_currentIndex, 0).data(Qt::UserRole)
             : QVariant();
}

void Combobox::setPlaceholderText(const QString &placeholder) {
  m_lineEdit->setPlaceholderText(placeholder);
}

bool Combobox::hasError() const { return m_hasError; }

void Combobox::setError(bool hasError) {
  if (m_hasError != hasError) {
    m_hasError = hasError;
    applyThemeStyles();
  }
}

void Combobox::showPopup(const QString &query) {
  if (!isEnabled() || !isVisible()) {
    restoreCommittedText();
    return;
  }
  m_openingPopup = true;
  {
    const QSignalBlocker blocker(m_searchEdit);
    m_searchEdit->setText(query);
    m_searchEdit->setCursorPosition(query.size());
  }
  updateFilter(query);
  m_popup->openFor(this);
  m_openingPopup = false;
  if (!m_popup->isVisible()) {
    restoreCommittedText();
  }
  applyThemeStyles();
}

void Combobox::updateFilter(const QString &query) {
  m_proxy->setFilterFixedString(query);
  m_list->clearSelection();
  m_list->setCurrentIndex(QModelIndex());
  {
    const QSignalBlocker blocker(m_lineEdit);
    m_lineEdit->setText(query);
  }
  updateResults();
  if (m_popup->isVisible()) {
    m_popup->openFor(this);
  }
}

void Combobox::updateResults() {
  const int count = m_proxy->rowCount();
  const auto &s = Theme::instance().spacing();
  const int rowHeight = m_list->fontMetrics().height() + 2 * s.space2;
  m_list->setMaximumHeight(qBound(1, count, 8) * rowHeight);
  m_list->setVisible(count > 0);
  m_emptyLabel->setVisible(count == 0);
}

void Combobox::restoreCommittedText() {
  const QSignalBlocker editorBlocker(m_lineEdit);
  const QSignalBlocker searchBlocker(m_searchEdit);
  m_lineEdit->setText(currentText());
  m_searchEdit->clear();
  m_proxy->setFilterFixedString(QString());
  m_list->clearSelection();
  m_list->setCurrentIndex(QModelIndex());
  updateResults();
}

bool Combobox::isSelectable(const QModelIndex &index) const {
  return index.isValid() && (index.flags() & Qt::ItemIsEnabled) &&
         (index.flags() & Qt::ItemIsSelectable);
}

void Combobox::moveHighlight(int direction) {
  const int count = m_proxy->rowCount();
  int row = m_list->currentIndex().isValid() ? m_list->currentIndex().row()
                                             : (direction > 0 ? -1 : count);
  for (row += direction; row >= 0 && row < count; row += direction) {
    const QModelIndex index = m_proxy->index(row, 0);
    if (isSelectable(index)) {
      m_list->setCurrentIndex(index);
      m_list->scrollTo(index);
      return;
    }
  }
}

void Combobox::commitIndex(const QModelIndex &proxyIndex) {
  if (!isEnabled() || !isSelectable(proxyIndex)) {
    return;
  }
  const QModelIndex sourceIndex = m_proxy->mapToSource(proxyIndex);
  if (isSelectable(sourceIndex)) {
    setCurrentIndex(sourceIndex.row());
  }
}

void Combobox::notifySelectionChanged(int oldIndex, const QString &oldText) {
  const int index = m_currentIndex;
  const QString text = currentText();
  QPointer<Combobox> guard(this);
  if (index != oldIndex) {
    emit currentIndexChanged(index);
  }
  if (guard && m_currentIndex == index && currentText() == text &&
      text != oldText) {
    emit currentTextChanged(text);
  }
}

bool Combobox::eventFilter(QObject *watched, QEvent *event) {
  const bool editor = watched == m_lineEdit || watched == m_searchEdit;
  if (editor &&
      (event->type() == QEvent::FocusIn || event->type() == QEvent::FocusOut)) {
    if (watched == m_lineEdit && event->type() == QEvent::FocusOut &&
        !m_openingPopup && !m_popup->isVisible()) {
      restoreCommittedText();
    }
    applyThemeStyles();
  }
  if (editor || watched == m_list) {
    if (event->type() == QEvent::ShortcutOverride) {
      auto *key = static_cast<QKeyEvent *>(event);
      if (key->key() == Qt::Key_Down || key->key() == Qt::Key_Up ||
          key->key() == Qt::Key_F4 ||
          (m_popup->isVisible() &&
           (key->key() == Qt::Key_Return || key->key() == Qt::Key_Enter ||
            key->key() == Qt::Key_Escape || key->key() == Qt::Key_Tab ||
            key->key() == Qt::Key_Backtab))) {
        event->accept();
        return true;
      }
    } else if (event->type() == QEvent::KeyPress && isEnabled()) {
      auto *key = static_cast<QKeyEvent *>(event);
      switch (key->key()) {
      case Qt::Key_Down:
      case Qt::Key_Up:
        if (!m_popup->isVisible()) {
          showPopup();
        }
        moveHighlight(key->key() == Qt::Key_Down ? 1 : -1);
        return true;
      case Qt::Key_Return:
      case Qt::Key_Enter:
        if (m_popup->isVisible()) {
          if (!m_list->currentIndex().isValid()) {
            moveHighlight(1);
          }
          commitIndex(m_list->currentIndex());
          return true;
        }
        break;
      case Qt::Key_Escape:
        if (m_popup->isVisible()) {
          m_popup->closePopover();
          return true;
        }
        break;
      case Qt::Key_F4:
        if (m_popup->isVisible()) {
          m_popup->closePopover();
        } else {
          showPopup();
        }
        return true;
      case Qt::Key_Tab:
      case Qt::Key_Backtab:
        if (m_popup->isVisible()) {
          const bool forward = key->key() == Qt::Key_Tab &&
                               !(key->modifiers() & Qt::ShiftModifier);
          m_popup->closePopover();
          m_lineEdit->setFocus(forward ? Qt::TabFocusReason
                                       : Qt::BacktabFocusReason);
          focusNextPrevChild(forward);
          return true;
        }
        break;
      default:
        break;
      }
    }
  }
  return QWidget::eventFilter(watched, event);
}

void Combobox::changeEvent(QEvent *event) {
  QWidget::changeEvent(event);
  if (event->type() == QEvent::EnabledChange) {
    if (!isEnabled()) {
      m_popup->closePopover();
      restoreCommittedText();
    }
    applyThemeStyles();
  }
}

void Combobox::hideEvent(QHideEvent *event) {
  m_popup->closePopover();
  restoreCommittedText();
  QWidget::hideEvent(event);
}

void Combobox::applyThemeStyles() {
  const auto &theme = Theme::instance();
  const auto &c = theme.colors();
  const auto &r = theme.radius();
  const auto &s = theme.spacing();
  const auto &t = theme.typography();
  const bool focused = m_lineEdit->hasFocus() || m_searchEdit->hasFocus() ||
                       m_popup->isVisible();
  const QColor border =
      m_hasError ? c.destructive : (focused ? c.ring : c.border);
  const QColor hoverBorder = m_hasError ? c.destructive : c.ring;
  const QFont controlFont = t.font(t.sizeBase);
  setFont(controlFont);
  m_lineEdit->setFont(controlFont);
  m_searchEdit->setFont(controlFont);
  m_list->setFont(controlFont);
  m_emptyLabel->setFont(t.font(t.sizeSm));
  setMinimumHeight(t.sizeBase + s.space6);
  m_layout->setContentsMargins(s.space3, s.space1, s.space1, s.space1);
  m_layout->setSpacing(s.space1);
  m_button->setFixedSize(s.space8, s.space8);

  QPalette editorPalette = m_lineEdit->palette();
  editorPalette.setColor(QPalette::Text, c.foreground);
  editorPalette.setColor(QPalette::PlaceholderText, c.mutedForeground);
  editorPalette.setColor(QPalette::Highlight, c.primary);
  editorPalette.setColor(QPalette::HighlightedText, c.primaryForeground);
  editorPalette.setColor(QPalette::Disabled, QPalette::Text, c.mutedForeground);
  m_lineEdit->setPalette(editorPalette);
  editorPalette.setColor(QPalette::Text, c.popoverForeground);
  m_searchEdit->setPalette(editorPalette);

  setStyleSheet(
      QStringLiteral("ui--Combobox {"
                     "  background-color: %1;"
                     "  border: 1px solid %2;"
                     "  border-radius: %3px;"
                     "}"
                     "ui--Combobox:hover { border-color: %4; }"
                     "ui--Combobox:disabled { background-color: %5; }")
          .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(c.background))
          .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(border))
          .arg(r.md)
          .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(hoverBorder))
          .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(c.muted)));
  m_lineEdit->setStyleSheet(QStringLiteral(
      "QLineEdit#uiComboboxEditor { background: transparent; border: none; }"));
  m_searchEdit->setStyleSheet(
      QStringLiteral("QLineEdit#uiComboboxSearch {"
                     "  background-color: %1;"
                     "  border: 1px solid %2;"
                     "  border-radius: %3px;"
                     "  padding: %4px %5px;"
                     "}"
                     "QLineEdit#uiComboboxSearch:focus { border-color: %6; }")
          .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(c.popover))
          .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(c.input))
          .arg(r.sm)
          .arg(s.space1)
          .arg(s.space2)
          .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(c.ring)));
  m_button->setStyleSheet(
      QStringLiteral(
          "QToolButton#uiComboboxButton {"
          "  background: transparent; border: none; color: %1; border-radius: "
          "%2px;"
          "}"
          "QToolButton#uiComboboxButton:hover { background-color: %3; }"
          "QToolButton#uiComboboxButton:disabled { color: %4; }")
          .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(c.foreground))
          .arg(r.sm)
          .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(c.accent))
          .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(c.mutedForeground)));
  m_list->setStyleSheet(
      QStringLiteral(
          "QListView#uiComboboxResults {"
          "  background-color: %1; color: %2; border: none;"
          "}"
          "QListView#uiComboboxResults::item {"
          "  padding: %3px %4px; border-radius: %5px;"
          "}"
          "QListView#uiComboboxResults::item:selected { background-color: %6; "
          "color: %7; }"
          "QListView#uiComboboxResults::item:disabled { color: %8; }")
          .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(c.popover))
          .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(c.popoverForeground))
          .arg(s.space2)
          .arg(s.space3)
          .arg(r.sm)
          .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(c.accent))
          .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(c.accentForeground))
          .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(c.mutedForeground)));
  m_emptyLabel->setStyleSheet(
      QStringLiteral("QLabel#uiComboboxEmpty { color: %1; padding: %2px; }")
          .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(c.mutedForeground))
          .arg(s.space3));
  updateResults();
  if (m_popup->isVisible()) {
    m_popup->openFor(this);
  }
}

} // namespace ui

#ifndef UI_COMBOBOX_H

#include <QModelIndex>

class Combobox : public QWidget {
  Q_OBJECT

public:
  explicit Combobox(QWidget *parent = nullptr);
  explicit Combobox(const QStringList &items, QWidget *parent = nullptr);
  ~Combobox() override;

  void setItems(const QStringList &items);
  void addItem(const QString &text, const QVariant &data = QVariant());

  int currentIndex() const;
  void setCurrentIndex(int index);
  QString currentText() const;
  QVariant currentData() const;

  void setPlaceholderText(const QString &placeholder);
  bool hasError() const;
  void setError(bool hasError);

signals:
  void currentIndexChanged(int index);
  void currentTextChanged(const QString &text);

protected:
  bool eventFilter(QObject *watched, QEvent *event) override;
  void changeEvent(QEvent *event) override;
  void hideEvent(QHideEvent *event) override;

private:
  void applyThemeStyles();
  void showPopup(const QString &query = QString());
  void updateFilter(const QString &query);
  void updateResults();
  void restoreCommittedText();
  void moveHighlight(int direction);
  void commitIndex(const QModelIndex &proxyIndex);
  bool isSelectable(const QModelIndex &index) const;
  void notifySelectionChanged(int oldIndex, const QString &oldText);

  QHBoxLayout *m_layout;
  QLineEdit *m_lineEdit;
  QToolButton *m_button;
  Popover *m_popup;
  QLineEdit *m_searchEdit;
  QListView *m_list;
  QLabel *m_emptyLabel;
  QStandardItemModel *m_model;
  QSortFilterProxyModel *m_proxy;
  int m_currentIndex{-1};
  bool m_hasError{false};
  bool m_openingPopup{false};
};

} // namespace ui

#include "dialog.h"
#include "../theme/style_helper.h"
#include <QAbstractTextDocumentLayout>
#include <QScreen>
#include <QScrollBar>
#include <QTextDocument>
#include <QtMath>

namespace ui {

Dialog::Dialog(QWidget *parent) : Dialog("", "", parent) {}

Dialog::Dialog(const QString &title, const QString &description,
               QWidget *parent)
    : QDialog(parent) {
  setModal(true);
  setMinimumWidth(400);
  setMaximumWidth(520);

  m_rootLayout = new QVBoxLayout(this);
  m_rootLayout->setContentsMargins(24, 24, 24, 24);
  m_rootLayout->setSpacing(16);
  m_rootLayout->setSizeConstraint(QLayout::SetMinimumSize);

  m_headerLayout = new QVBoxLayout();
  m_headerLayout->setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0);
  m_headerLayout->setSpacing(6);

  m_titleLabel = new QLabel(title, this);
  m_titleLabel->setTextFormat(Qt::PlainText);
  m_titleLabel->setWordWrap(true);
  m_descriptionEdit = new QTextEdit(this);
  m_descriptionEdit->setObjectName("dialogDescription");
  m_descriptionEdit->setAccessibleName(tr("Dialog message"));
  m_descriptionEdit->setReadOnly(true);
  m_descriptionEdit->setTextInteractionFlags(Qt::TextSelectableByMouse |
                                             Qt::TextSelectableByKeyboard);
  m_descriptionEdit->setFrameShape(QFrame::NoFrame);
  m_descriptionEdit->setHorizontalScrollBarPolicy(Qt::ScrollBarAlwaysOff);
  m_descriptionEdit->setVerticalScrollBarPolicy(Qt::ScrollBarAsNeeded);
  m_descriptionEdit->setLineWrapMode(QTextEdit::WidgetWidth);
  m_descriptionEdit->setWordWrapMode(QTextOption::WrapAtWordBoundaryOrAnywhere);
  m_descriptionEdit->document()->setDocumentMargin(0);
  m_descriptionEdit->setPlainText(description);
  m_descriptionEdit->setVisible(!description.isEmpty());

  m_headerLayout->addWidget(m_titleLabel);
  m_headerLayout->addWidget(m_descriptionEdit);
  m_rootLayout->addLayout(m_headerLayout);

  m_contentLayout = new QVBoxLayout();
  m_contentLayout->setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0);
  m_contentLayout->setSpacing(12);
  m_rootLayout->addLayout(m_contentLayout, 1);

  m_footerLayout = new QHBoxLayout();
  m_footerLayout->setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0);
  m_footerLayout->setSpacing(8);
  m_footerLayout->addStretch();

  m_cancelButton =
      new Button("Cancel", ButtonVariant::Outline, ButtonSize::Default, this);
  m_confirmButton =
      new Button("Confirm", ButtonVariant::Primary, ButtonSize::Default, this);

  connect(m_cancelButton, &QPushButton::clicked, this, &QDialog::reject);
  connect(m_confirmButton, &QPushButton::clicked, this, &QDialog::accept);

  m_footerLayout->addWidget(m_cancelButton);
  m_footerLayout->addWidget(m_confirmButton);
  m_rootLayout->addLayout(m_footerLayout);

  connect(&Theme::instance(), &Theme::themeChanged, this,
          &Dialog::applyThemeStyles);
  applyThemeStyles();
}

void Dialog::setTitle(const QString &title) { m_titleLabel->setText(title); }

void Dialog::setDescription(const QString &description) {
  m_descriptionEdit->setPlainText(description);
  m_descriptionEdit->setVisible(!description.isEmpty());
  updateDescriptionHeight();
  if (isVisible()) {
    adjustSize();
  }
}

void Dialog::resizeEvent(QResizeEvent *event) {
  QDialog::resizeEvent(event);
  if (m_descriptionEdit && event->size().width() != event->oldSize().width()) {
    updateDescriptionHeight();
  }
}

void Dialog::updateDescriptionHeight() {
  const QString description = m_descriptionEdit->toPlainText();
  if (description.isEmpty()) {
    m_descriptionEdit->setFixedHeight(0);
    return;
  }

  const QMargins margins = m_rootLayout->contentsMargins();
  // Reserve scrollbar width so the measured text still fits when scrolling.
  const int textWidth =
      qMax(1, width() - margins.left() - margins.right() -
                  m_descriptionEdit->verticalScrollBar()->sizeHint().width());
  QTextDocument measurement;
  measurement.documentLayout()->setPaintDevice(m_descriptionEdit->viewport());
  measurement.setDefaultFont(m_descriptionEdit->font());
  measurement.setDefaultTextOption(
      m_descriptionEdit->document()->defaultTextOption());
  measurement.setDocumentMargin(0);
  measurement.setPlainText(description);
  measurement.setTextWidth(textWidth);

  const int lineHeight = m_descriptionEdit->fontMetrics().lineSpacing();
  const int heightLimit = qMax(
      lineHeight, screen() ? screen()->availableGeometry().height() / 2 : 400);
  m_descriptionEdit->setFixedHeight(
      qBound(lineHeight, qCeil(measurement.size().height()), heightLimit));
}

void Dialog::setContentWidget(QWidget *widget) {
  if (widget) {
    m_contentLayout->addWidget(widget);
  }
}

void Dialog::setContentLayout(QLayout *layout) {
  if (layout) {
    m_contentLayout->addLayout(layout);
  }
}

void Dialog::setConfirmText(const QString &text) {
  m_confirmButton->setText(text);
}

void Dialog::setCancelText(const QString &text) {
  m_cancelButton->setText(text);
}

void Dialog::setDestructive(bool destructive) {
  m_confirmButton->setVariant(destructive ? ButtonVariant::Destructive
                                          : ButtonVariant::Primary);
}

Button *Dialog::confirmButton() const { return m_confirmButton; }

Button *Dialog::cancelButton() const { return m_cancelButton; }

bool Dialog::confirm(QWidget *parent, const QString &title,
                     const QString &description, const QString &confirmText,
                     bool isDestructive) {
  Dialog dlg(title, description, parent);
  dlg.setConfirmText(confirmText);
  dlg.setDestructive(isDestructive);
  return dlg.exec() == QDialog::Accepted;
}

void Dialog::info(QWidget *parent, const QString &title,
                  const QString &description) {
  Dialog dlg(title, description, parent);
  dlg.cancelButton()->setVisible(false);
  dlg.setConfirmText("OK");
  dlg.exec();
}

void Dialog::applyThemeStyles() {
  const auto &c = Theme::instance().colors();
  const auto &r = Theme::instance().radius();
  const auto &t = Theme::instance().typography();

  m_titleLabel->setFont(t.font(t.sizeLg, QFont::DemiBold));
  m_titleLabel->setStyleSheet(QString("color: %1; background: transparent;")
                                  .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(c.foreground)));

  m_descriptionEdit->setFont(t.font(t.sizeSm, QFont::Normal));
  m_descriptionEdit->document()->setDefaultFont(m_descriptionEdit->font());
  m_descriptionEdit->setStyleSheet(
      QString("QTextEdit { color: %1; background: transparent; border: none; "
              "padding: 0; }")
          .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(c.mutedForeground)));
  updateDescriptionHeight();

  setStyleSheet(QString("QDialog {"
                        "  background-color: %1;"
                        "  border: 1px solid %2;"
                        "  border-radius: %3px;"
                        "}")
                    .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(c.card))
                    .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(c.border))
                    .arg(r.lg));
}

} // namespace ui

#include "empty_state.h"
#include "../theme/style_helper.h"
#include "../theme/theme.h"
#include <QLabel>
#include <QPainter>
#include <QPixmap>
#include <QSizePolicy>
#include <QVBoxLayout>

namespace ui {

EmptyState::EmptyState(QWidget *parent)
    : EmptyState(QString(), QString(), parent) {}

EmptyState::EmptyState(const QString &title, const QString &description,
                       QWidget *parent)
    : QFrame(parent) {
  setFrameShape(QFrame::NoFrame);
  setAttribute(Qt::WA_StyledBackground, true);
  setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy::Expanding, QSizePolicy::Preferred);

  m_layout = new QVBoxLayout(this);
  m_layout->setAlignment(Qt::AlignVCenter);
  m_iconLabel = new QLabel(this);
  m_iconLabel->setAlignment(Qt::AlignCenter);
  m_iconLabel->setAttribute(Qt::WA_TransparentForMouseEvents, true);
  m_layout->addWidget(m_iconLabel, 0, Qt::AlignHCenter);

  m_titleLabel = new QLabel(this);
  m_descriptionLabel = new QLabel(this);
  QLabel *labels[] = {m_titleLabel, m_descriptionLabel};
  for (QLabel *label : labels) {
    label->setTextFormat(Qt::PlainText);
    label->setWordWrap(true);
    label->setTextInteractionFlags(Qt::TextSelectableByMouse |
                                   Qt::TextSelectableByKeyboard);
    label->setFocusPolicy(Qt::StrongFocus);
    label->setAlignment(Qt::AlignCenter);
    label->setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy::Expanding, QSizePolicy::Preferred);
    m_layout->addWidget(label);
  }

  m_actionButton = new Button(this);
  // A long action caption must not impose a minimum width on the whole state.
  m_actionButton->setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy::Ignored, QSizePolicy::Fixed);
  m_layout->addWidget(m_actionButton);
  connect(m_actionButton, &Button::clicked, this, &EmptyState::actionTriggered);

  setTitle(title);
  setDescription(description);
  setActionText(QString());
  connect(&Theme::instance(), &Theme::themeChanged, this,
          &EmptyState::applyThemeStyles);
  applyThemeStyles();
}

void EmptyState::setTitle(const QString &title) {
  m_titleLabel->setText(title);
  m_titleLabel->setAccessibleName(title);
  m_titleLabel->setVisible(!title.isEmpty());
  setAccessibleName(title);
}

QString EmptyState::title() const { return m_titleLabel->text(); }

void EmptyState::setDescription(const QString &description) {
  m_descriptionLabel->setText(description);
  m_descriptionLabel->setAccessibleName(description);
  m_descriptionLabel->setVisible(!description.isEmpty());
  setAccessibleDescription(description);
}

QString EmptyState::description() const { return m_descriptionLabel->text(); }

void EmptyState::setIcon(const QIcon &icon) {
  m_icon = icon;
  updateIcon();
}

void EmptyState::setActionText(const QString &text) {
  m_actionButton->setText(text);
  m_actionButton->setAccessibleName(text);
  m_actionButton->setVisible(!text.isEmpty());
}

Button *EmptyState::actionButton() const { return m_actionButton; }

void EmptyState::updateIcon() {
  const auto &theme = Theme::instance();
  const int extent = theme.spacing().space10;
  QPixmap pixmap = m_icon.pixmap(QSize(extent, extent));
  if (pixmap.isNull()) {
    m_iconLabel->clear();
    m_iconLabel->hide();
    return;
  }

  {
    QPainter painter(&pixmap);
    painter.setCompositionMode(QPainter::CompositionMode_SourceIn);
    painter.fillRect(pixmap.rect(), theme.colors().mutedForeground);
  }
  m_iconLabel->setPixmap(pixmap);
  m_iconLabel->show();
}

void EmptyState::applyThemeStyles() {
  const auto &theme = Theme::instance();
  const auto &c = theme.colors();
  const auto &s = theme.spacing();
  const auto &r = theme.radius();
  const auto &t = theme.typography();

  m_layout->setContentsMargins(s.space6, s.space6, s.space6, s.space6);
  m_layout->setSpacing(s.space3);
  m_iconLabel->setFixedSize(s.space10, s.space10);
  m_titleLabel->setFont(t.font(t.sizeLg, QFont::DemiBold));
  m_descriptionLabel->setFont(t.font(t.sizeSm));

  const QString labelStyle = QString("QLabel {"
                                     "  color: %1;"
                                     "  background-color: transparent;"
                                     "  border: none;"
                                     "  margin: %2px;"
                                     "  padding: %2px;"
                                     "}");
  m_titleLabel->setStyleSheet(
      labelStyle.arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(c.cardForeground)).arg(s.space0));
  m_descriptionLabel->setStyleSheet(
      labelStyle.arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(c.mutedForeground))
          .arg(s.space0));
  m_iconLabel->setStyleSheet(
      labelStyle.arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(c.mutedForeground))
          .arg(s.space0));

  setStyleSheet(QString("ui--EmptyState {"
                        "  background-color: %1;"
                        "  border: 1px solid %2;"
                        "  border-radius: %3px;"
                        "}")
                    .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(c.card))
                    .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(c.border))
                    .arg(r.lg));
  updateIcon();
}

} // namespace ui

================================================================================
FILE: ui-kit/components/form_field.cpp
================================================================================
#include "form_field.h"
#include "../theme/style_helper.h"

namespace ui {

FormField::FormField(QWidget *parent) : FormField("", nullptr, parent) {}

FormField::FormField(const QString &label, QWidget *control, QWidget *parent)
    : QWidget(parent) {
  setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy::Expanding, QSizePolicy::Preferred);

  m_layout = new QVBoxLayout(this);
  m_layout->setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0);
  m_layout->setSpacing(Theme::instance().spacing().space2);

  m_labelLayout = new QHBoxLayout();
  m_labelLayout->setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0);
  m_labelLayout->setSpacing(4);

  m_label = new QLabel(this);
  m_label->setVisible(false);

  m_requiredIndicator = new QLabel("*", this);
  m_requiredIndicator->setVisible(false);

  m_labelLayout->addWidget(m_label);
  m_labelLayout->addWidget(m_requiredIndicator);
  m_labelLayout->addStretch();
  m_layout->addLayout(m_labelLayout);

  m_descLabel = new QLabel(this);
  m_descLabel->setWordWrap(true);
  m_descLabel->setVisible(false);

  m_errorLabel = new QLabel(this);
  m_errorLabel->setWordWrap(true);
  m_errorLabel->setVisible(false);

  if (!label.isEmpty()) {
    setLabel(label);
  }
  if (control) {
    setControl(control);
  }

  m_layout->addWidget(m_descLabel);
  m_layout->addWidget(m_errorLabel);

  connect(&Theme::instance(), &Theme::themeChanged, this,
          &FormField::applyThemeStyles);
  applyThemeStyles();
}

QString FormField::label() const { return m_label->text(); }

void FormField::setLabel(const QString &label) {
  m_label->setText(label);
  m_label->setVisible(!label.isEmpty());
}

bool FormField::isRequired() const { return m_isRequired; }

void FormField::setRequired(bool required) {
  m_isRequired = required;
  m_requiredIndicator->setVisible(m_isRequired && !m_label->text().isEmpty());
}

QString FormField::description() const { return m_descLabel->text(); }

void FormField::setDescription(const QString &description) {
  m_descLabel->setText(description);
  m_descLabel->setVisible(!description.isEmpty() && !m_hasError);
}

void FormField::setError(const QString &errorMessage) {
  m_hasError = !errorMessage.isEmpty();
  m_errorLabel->setText(errorMessage);
  m_errorLabel->setVisible(m_hasError);

  if (m_hasError) {
    m_descLabel->setVisible(false);
  } else {
    m_descLabel->setVisible(!m_descLabel->text().isEmpty());
  }
}

void FormField::clearError() { setError(""); }

bool FormField::hasError() const { return m_hasError; }

QString FormField::error() const { return m_errorLabel->text(); }

void FormField::setControl(QWidget *control) {
  if (m_control == control) {
    return;
  }
  if (m_control) {
    m_layout->removeWidget(m_control);
    m_control->setParent(nullptr);
  }
  m_control = control;
  if (m_control) {
    m_layout->insertWidget(1, m_control);
  }
}

QWidget *FormField::control() const { return m_control; }

void FormField::applyThemeStyles() {
  const auto &c = Theme::instance().colors();
  const auto &t = Theme::instance().typography();

  m_label->setStyleSheet(
      QString("color: %1; background: transparent; font-size: %2px; "
              "font-weight: 600; font-family: '%3';")
          .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(c.foreground))
          .arg(t.sizeBase)
          .arg(t.fontFamily));

  m_requiredIndicator->setStyleSheet(
      QString("color: %1; background: transparent; font-size: %2px; "
              "font-weight: bold;")
          .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(c.destructive))
          .arg(t.sizeBase));

  m_descLabel->setStyleSheet(
      QString("color: %1; background: transparent; font-size: %2px; "
              "font-family: '%3';")
          .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(c.mutedForeground))
          .arg(t.sizeXs)
          .arg(t.fontFamily));

  m_errorLabel->setStyleSheet(
      QString("color: %1; background: transparent; font-size: %2px; "
              "font-weight: 600; font-family: '%3';")
          .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(c.destructive))
          .arg(t.sizeXs)
          .arg(t.fontFamily));
}

} // namespace ui

FILE: ui-kit/components/input.cpp

#include "input.h"
#include "../theme/style_helper.h"

namespace ui {

Input::Input(QWidget *parent)
    : Input("", parent) {}

Input::Input(const QString &placeholder, QWidget *parent)
    : QWidget(parent) {
  setAttribute(Qt::WA_StyledBackground, true);
  setFixedHeight(40);
  setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy::Expanding, QSizePolicy::Fixed);

  m_layout = new QHBoxLayout(this);
  m_layout->setContentsMargins(12, 0, 8, 0);
  m_layout->setSpacing(6);

  m_prefixLabel = new QLabel(this);
  m_prefixLabel->setVisible(false);

  m_lineEdit = new QLineEdit(this);
  m_lineEdit->setFrame(false);
  m_lineEdit->setPlaceholderText(placeholder);
  m_lineEdit->setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy::Expanding, QSizePolicy::Expanding);
  m_lineEdit->installEventFilter(this);

  m_suffixLabel = new QLabel(this);
  m_suffixLabel->setVisible(false);

  m_clearButton = new QPushButton(QString::fromUtf8("\u2715"), this);
  m_clearButton->setFixedSize(18, 18);
  m_clearButton->setCursor(Qt::PointingHandCursor);
  m_clearButton->setToolTip("Clear");
  m_clearButton->setVisible(false);
  connect(m_clearButton, &QPushButton::clicked, m_lineEdit, &QLineEdit::clear);

  m_layout->addWidget(m_prefixLabel);
  m_layout->addWidget(m_lineEdit, 1);
  m_layout->addWidget(m_suffixLabel);
  m_layout->addWidget(m_clearButton);

  connect(m_lineEdit, &QLineEdit::textChanged, this, [this](const QString &text) {
    if (m_clearButtonEnabled) {
      m_clearButton->setVisible(!text.isEmpty());
    }
    emit textChanged(text);
  });
  connect(m_lineEdit, &QLineEdit::textEdited, this, &Input::textEdited);
  connect(m_lineEdit, &QLineEdit::returnPressed, this, &Input::returnPressed);

  connect(&Theme::instance(), &Theme::themeChanged, this, &Input::applyThemeStyles);
  applyThemeStyles();
}

QString Input::text() const {
  return m_lineEdit->text();
}

void Input::setText(const QString &text) {
  m_lineEdit->setText(text);
}

QString Input::placeholderText() const {
  return m_lineEdit->placeholderText();
}

void Input::setPlaceholderText(const QString &placeholder) {
  m_lineEdit->setPlaceholderText(placeholder);
}

void Input::setPrefixText(const QString &prefix) {
  m_prefixLabel->setText(prefix);
  m_prefixLabel->setVisible(!prefix.isEmpty());
}

void Input::setSuffixText(const QString &suffix) {
  m_suffixLabel->setText(suffix);
  m_suffixLabel->setVisible(!suffix.isEmpty());
}

bool Input::isClearButtonEnabled() const {
  return m_clearButtonEnabled;
}

void Input::setClearButtonEnabled(bool enable) {
  m_clearButtonEnabled = enable;
  m_clearButton->setVisible(enable && !m_lineEdit->text().isEmpty());
}

bool Input::isReadOnly() const {
  return m_lineEdit->isReadOnly();
}

void Input::setReadOnly(bool readOnly) {
  m_lineEdit->setReadOnly(readOnly);
  applyThemeStyles();
}

bool Input::hasError() const {
  return m_hasError;
}

void Input::setError(bool hasError) {
  if (m_hasError != hasError) {
    m_hasError = hasError;
    applyThemeStyles();
  }
}

QLineEdit *Input::lineEdit() const {
  return m_lineEdit;
}

bool Input::eventFilter(QObject *watched, QEvent *event) {
  if (watched == m_lineEdit) {
    if (event->type() == QEvent::FocusIn) {
      m_isFocused = true;
      applyThemeStyles();
    } else if (event->type() == QEvent::FocusOut) {
      m_isFocused = false;
      applyThemeStyles();
    }
  }
  return QWidget::eventFilter(watched, event);
}

void Input::applyThemeStyles() {
  const auto &c = Theme::instance().colors();
  const auto &r = Theme::instance().radius();
  const auto &t = Theme::instance().typography();

  setFont(t.font(t.sizeBase, QFont::Normal));
  m_lineEdit->setFont(t.font(t.sizeBase, QFont::Normal));
  m_prefixLabel->setFont(t.font(t.sizeSm, QFont::DemiBold));
  m_suffixLabel->setFont(t.font(t.sizeSm, QFont::Normal));

  QColor activeBorder = m_hasError ? c.destructive : (m_isFocused ? c.ring : c.border);
  int borderWidth = m_isFocused ? 2 : 1;
  QColor hoverBorder = m_hasError ? c.destructive : (m_isFocused ? c.ring : c.primary);
  setStyleSheet(QString(
      "ui--Input {"
      "  background-color: %1;"
      "  border: %2px solid %3;"
      "  border-radius: %4px;"
      "}"
      "ui--Input:hover {"
      "  border-color: %5;"
      "}")
      .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(c.background))
      .arg(borderWidth)
      .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(activeBorder))
      .arg(r.md)
      .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(hoverBorder)));

  m_lineEdit->setStyleSheet(QString(
      "QLineEdit {"
      "  background: transparent;"
      "  border: none;"
      "  color: %1;"
      "  selection-background-color: %2;"
      "  selection-color: %3;"
      "}")
      .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(c.foreground))
      .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(c.primary))
      .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(c.primaryForeground)));

  m_prefixLabel->setStyleSheet(QString("color: %1; background: transparent;")
                                   .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(c.mutedForeground)));
  m_suffixLabel->setStyleSheet(QString("color: %1; background: transparent;")
                                   .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(c.mutedForeground)));

  m_clearButton->setStyleSheet(QString(
      "QPushButton {"
      "  background: transparent;"
      "  border: none;"
      "  color: %1;"
      "  font-size: 11px;"
      "  font-weight: bold;"
      "}"
      "QPushButton:hover {"
      "  color: %2;"
      "}")
      .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(c.mutedForeground))
      .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(c.destructive)));
}

} // namespace ui

#include "menu.h"
#include "../theme/style_helper.h"
#include "../theme/theme.h"

#include <QPalette>

namespace ui {

Menu::Menu(QWidget *parent) : Menu(QString(), parent) {}

Menu::Menu(const QString &title, QWidget *parent) : QMenu(title, parent) {
  connect(&Theme::instance(), &Theme::themeChanged, this,
          &Menu::applyThemeStyles);
  applyThemeStyles();
}

void Menu::applyThemeStyles() {
  const auto &theme = Theme::instance();
  const auto &c = theme.colors();
  const auto &r = theme.radius();
  const auto &s = theme.spacing();
  const auto &t = theme.typography();

  setFont(t.font(t.sizeBase));
  QPalette menuPalette = palette();
  menuPalette.setColor(QPalette::Window, c.popover);
  menuPalette.setColor(QPalette::WindowText, c.popoverForeground);
  menuPalette.setColor(QPalette::Text, c.popoverForeground);
  menuPalette.setColor(QPalette::ButtonText, c.popoverForeground);
  menuPalette.setColor(QPalette::Highlight, c.accent);
  menuPalette.setColor(QPalette::HighlightedText, c.accentForeground);
  menuPalette.setColor(QPalette::Disabled, QPalette::WindowText,
                       c.mutedForeground);
  menuPalette.setColor(QPalette::Disabled, QPalette::Text, c.mutedForeground);
  menuPalette.setColor(QPalette::Disabled, QPalette::ButtonText,
                       c.mutedForeground);
  setPalette(menuPalette);

  // This local stylesheet also covers native submenus owned by this menu.
  // Indicators and arrows are deliberately left to the Qt style.
  setStyleSheet(QStringLiteral("QMenu {"
                               "  background-color: %1;"
                               "  color: %2;"
                               "  border: 1px solid %3;"
                               "  border-radius: %4px;"
                               "  padding: %5px;"
                               "}"
                               "QMenu::item {"
                               "  padding: %6px %7px;"
                               "  border-radius: %8px;"
                               "}"
                               "QMenu::item:selected {"
                               "  background-color: %9;"
                               "  color: %10;"
                               "}"
                               "QMenu::item:disabled {"
                               "  color: %11;"
                               "}"
                               "QMenu::separator {"
                               "  height: 1px;"
                               "  background-color: %3;"
                               "  margin: %5px %6px;"
                               "}")
                    .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(c.popover))
                    .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(c.popoverForeground))
                    .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(c.border))
                    .arg(r.md)
                    .arg(s.space1)
                    .arg(s.space2)
                    .arg(s.space6)
                    .arg(r.sm)
                    .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(c.accent))
                    .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(c.accentForeground))
                    .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(c.mutedForeground)));
}

} // namespace ui

#include "number_input.h"
#include "../theme/style_helper.h"
#include "../theme/theme.h"
#include <QEvent>
#include <QLineEdit>
#include <QPalette>

namespace ui {
namespace {

void applySpinBoxTheme(QAbstractSpinBox *spinBox, QLineEdit *editor,
                       const QString &selector, bool hasError) {
  const auto &theme = Theme::instance();
  const auto &c = theme.colors();
  const auto &r = theme.radius();
  const auto &s = theme.spacing();
  const auto &t = theme.typography();
  const bool isDark = theme.isDark();
  const bool readOnly = editor->isReadOnly();
  const QColor background = readOnly ? c.muted : c.background;
  const QColor border =
      hasError ? c.destructive
               : (readOnly ? c.border
                           : StyleHelper::tint(c.border, c.foreground, 0.25));
  const QColor hoverBorder =
      hasError ? c.destructive : (readOnly ? c.border : c.primary);
  const QColor focusBorder = hasError ? c.destructive : c.ring;
  const QColor compartmentBg =
      readOnly ? c.muted
               : (isDark ? c.secondary
                         : StyleHelper::tint(c.background, c.primary, 0.08));
  const QColor compartmentHover =
      readOnly ? c.muted
               : (isDark ? StyleHelper::lighten(c.secondary, 18)
                         : StyleHelper::tint(c.background, c.primary, 0.18));
  const QColor compartmentPressed =
      readOnly ? c.muted
               : (isDark ? StyleHelper::lighten(c.secondary, 30)
                         : StyleHelper::tint(c.background, c.primary, 0.28));
  const QString upIcon =
      isDark ? QStringLiteral(":/icons/icons/arrow-up.svg")
             : QStringLiteral(":/icons/icons/arrow-up-black.svg");
  const QString downIcon =
      isDark ? QStringLiteral(":/icons/icons/arrow-down.svg")
             : QStringLiteral(":/icons/icons/arrow-down-black.svg");

  spinBox->setFont(t.font(t.sizeBase));
  spinBox->setMinimumHeight(s.space10);
  QPalette colors = spinBox->palette();
  colors.setColor(QPalette::Window, background);
  colors.setColor(QPalette::WindowText, c.foreground);
  colors.setColor(QPalette::Base, background);
  colors.setColor(QPalette::Text, c.foreground);
  colors.setColor(QPalette::Button, c.secondary);
  colors.setColor(QPalette::ButtonText, c.secondaryForeground);
  colors.setColor(QPalette::Light, c.background);
  colors.setColor(QPalette::Midlight, c.secondary);
  colors.setColor(QPalette::Mid, c.border);
  colors.setColor(QPalette::Dark, c.input);
  colors.setColor(QPalette::Shadow, c.border);
  colors.setColor(QPalette::Highlight, c.primary);
  colors.setColor(QPalette::HighlightedText, c.primaryForeground);
  colors.setColor(QPalette::Disabled, QPalette::Window, c.muted);
  colors.setColor(QPalette::Disabled, QPalette::WindowText, c.mutedForeground);
  colors.setColor(QPalette::Disabled, QPalette::Base, c.muted);
  colors.setColor(QPalette::Disabled, QPalette::Text, c.mutedForeground);
  colors.setColor(QPalette::Disabled, QPalette::Button, c.muted);
  colors.setColor(QPalette::Disabled, QPalette::ButtonText, c.mutedForeground);
  colors.setColor(QPalette::Disabled, QPalette::Highlight, c.secondary);
  colors.setColor(QPalette::Disabled, QPalette::HighlightedText,
                  c.mutedForeground);
  spinBox->setPalette(colors);
  editor->setFont(t.font(t.sizeBase));
  editor->setPalette(colors);

  spinBox->setStyleSheet(
      QString("%1 {"
              "  background-color: %2;"
              "  color: %3;"
              "  border: 1px solid %4;"
              "  border-radius: %5px;"
              "  padding: 0px 36px 0px %7px;"
              "  selection-background-color: %8;"
              "  selection-color: %9;"
              "}"
              "%1:hover:enabled { border-color: %10; }"
              "%1:focus { border-color: %11; }"
              "%1:disabled {"
              "  background-color: %12;"
              "  color: %13;"
              "  border-color: %14;"
              "  selection-background-color: %15;"
              "  selection-color: %13;"
              "}"
              "%1::up-button {"
              "  subcontrol-origin: border;"
              "  subcontrol-position: top right;"
              "  width: 30px;"
              "  border-left: 1px solid %4;"
              "  border-bottom: 1px solid %4;"
              "  border-top: 1px solid %4;"
              "  border-right: 1px solid %4;"
              "  border-top-right-radius: %6px;"
              "  background-color: %16;"
              "}"
              "%1::down-button {"
              "  subcontrol-origin: border;"
              "  subcontrol-position: bottom right;"
              "  width: 30px;"
              "  border-left: 1px solid %4;"
              "  border-bottom: 1px solid %4;"
              "  border-right: 1px solid %4;"
              "  border-top: none;"
              "  border-bottom-right-radius: %6px;"
              "  background-color: %16;"
              "}"
              "%1:hover:enabled::up-button, %1:hover:enabled::down-button {"
              "  border-color: %10;"
              "}"
              "%1:focus::up-button, %1:focus::down-button {"
              "  border-color: %11;"
              "}"
              "%1::up-button:hover:enabled, %1::down-button:hover:enabled {"
              "  background-color: %17;"
              "}"
              "%1::up-button:pressed:enabled, %1::down-button:pressed:enabled {"
              "  background-color: %18;"
              "}"
              "%1::up-button:disabled, %1::down-button:disabled {"
              "  background-color: %12;"
              "  border-color: %14;"
              "}"
              "%1::up-arrow {"
              "  image: url(%19);"
              "  width: 10px;"
              "  height: 10px;"
              "}"
              "%1::down-arrow {"
              "  image: url(%20);"
              "  width: 10px;"
              "  height: 10px;"
              "}")
          .arg(selector)
          .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(background))
          .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(c.foreground))
          .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(border))
          .arg(r.md)
          .arg(r.md - 1)
          .arg(s.space3)
          .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(c.primary))
          .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(c.primaryForeground))
          .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(hoverBorder))
          .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(focusBorder))
          .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(c.muted))
          .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(c.mutedForeground))
          .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(c.border))
          .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(c.secondary))
          .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(compartmentBg))
          .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(compartmentHover))
          .arg(StyleHelper::toHexString(compartmentPressed))
          .arg(upIcon)
          .arg(downIcon));
}

} // namespace

NumberInput::NumberInput(QWidget *parent) : QSpinBox(parent) {
  setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy::Expanding, QSizePolicy::Fixed);
  lineEdit()->installEventFilter(this);
  connect(&Theme::instance(), &Theme::themeChanged, this,
          &NumberInput::applyThemeStyles);
  applyThemeStyles();
}

bool NumberInput::hasError() const { return m_hasError; }

void NumberInput::setError(bool hasError) {
  if (m_hasError == hasError) {
    return;
  }
  m_hasError = hasError;
  applyThemeStyles();
}

bool NumberInput::eventFilter(QObject *watched, QEvent *event) {
  const bool handled = QSpinBox::eventFilter(watched, event);
  if (watched == lineEdit() && event->type() == QEvent::ReadOnlyChange) {
    applyThemeStyles();
  }
  return handled;
}

void NumberInput::applyThemeStyles() {
  applySpinBoxTheme(this, lineEdit(), QStringLiteral("ui--NumberInput"),
                    m_hasError);
}

          &DecimalInput::applyThemeStyles);

  applyThemeStyles();
}

bool DecimalInput::eventFilter(QObject *watched, QEvent *event) {

  if (watched == lineEdit() && event->type() == QEvent::ReadOnlyChange) {
    applyThemeStyles();
  }
  return handled;
}

  applySpinBoxTheme(this, lineEdit(), QStringLiteral("ui--DecimalInput"),
                    m_hasError);
}
